# Decisions — 2026-11

Entries dated 2026-11, oldest first; a new one is appended at the end.
Rules and the index of every month: [docs/decisions.md](../decisions.md).

---

## 2026-11-02 · Pacientes: the «Turnos» column counts the appointments granted, the ficha lists them, and an account with no name and no requests stays off the default list
Decided: (1) «Turnos» in the Pacientes roster was the number of attendance facts (past visits only); it is now the number of approved appointments (past and future) with a link to the ficha, the next one in its tooltip, «—» at zero, and it is a default column (nine defaults). (2) The ficha gets a «Turnos» table above the form: every request of the patient (up to 100, newest first) with when, service, where and the state in words (Vino / No vino / Confirmado / Esperando respuesta / Cancelado / Rechazado); a request without a slot shows «Pidió <fecha>». It lists only what the viewer may see (`booking_requests.staff_can_see`: a doctor sees his own, a center account its centers); the roster count is the whole count. (3) An account with neither first nor last name shows «Sin nombre» (not a bare dash), sorts last in the alphabetical mode, and the default «recientes» list hides it unless it has at least one request; searching by DNI still finds it. · Why: Salud (the hospital's technical contact) 2026-10-30: «hay gente registrada sin nombre, es mucha» and «cuesta mucho encontrar qué turnos tiene cada paciente, en esta sección no informa qué turnos se le otorgaron»; the name fix of 2026-10-30 (`8229e610`) only covers new portal accounts · Rejected: deleting nameless accounts (they hold a DNI and may hold requests); a separate screen for turnos (the ficha is where the desk already looks) · Cost: one grouped query per roster page and one per ficha · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `admin_panel/admin_patients.py`, `admin_panel/admin_patients_router.py`, `core/db_booking.py`, `core/db_accounts.py`, `modules/turnos/booking_requests.py`, `static/platform/admin-patients.mjs`, `locales/es/admin.queue.json`; `test/test_admin_patients.py`, `test/admin-patients-lifecycle.test.mjs`.

## 2026-11-02 · Médicos: a list of professionals is loaded from the panel, a portal email already in use is refused before anything is written, and the month calendar marks the days a doctor attends
Decided: (1) Médicos gets «Cargar lista» next to «Invitar médico»: the desk pastes or uploads a table (name, surname, email, specialty; header row optional; tab, `;` or `,`), up to 100 rows. Each row goes through the same `POST /api/admin/content/medicos` as «Nuevo médico» (doctor and account in one entity), one at a time; a row already in the roster (same email, or same full name when an email is missing) or repeated inside the list is skipped and named, so loading the same list twice creates nothing twice. The «Enviar invitaciones» box (on by default) invites each row that has an email. (2) On an installation with portal login, `POST …/medicos` answers 409 `field: email` before any write when the email already belongs to an active account (`admin_users.portal_email_conflict`); before, the doctor was saved and the invitation then failed, leaving a doctor with no usable account. (3) The desk's month calendar (`queue_calendar_data`) also returns `atencion`, the days from today to the end of the shown range on which the filtered doctor attends (`_effective_day_hours`); the calendar rings them and a line says how many have no turnos. Empty for «todos los médicos». · Why: promises from the call with Salud on 2026-10-13 (calendar for professionals, bulk creation of users); the bulk load of 2026-10-18 (2 admins, 32 admisión, 85 professionals) was done by scripts, which is not repeatable by the hospital · Rejected: a spreadsheet parser in the browser (CSV/TSV text is enough, the desk copies from Excel); one bulk endpoint (rows reuse the single create, so every rule of «Nuevo médico» holds) · Cost: one request per row · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent. The invitations to the 85 professionals loaded on 2026-10-18 are still unsent: they wait for Salud's word.
Source: `static/platform/admin-content.mjs` (`medImportHtml`, `parseDoctorList`, `wireMedImport`), `admin_panel/admin_content.py`, `admin_panel/admin_users.py`, `modules/turnos/doctor_appointments.py`, `static/platform/admin-queue.mjs`, `locales/es/admin.content.json`, `locales/es/admin.queue.json`; `test/admin-create-appointment-native.test.mjs`, `test/test_doctor_account_unify.py`, `test/test_schedule_policy_slice4.py`.

## 2026-11-02 · Editing a patient is written to the access log, and Servicios shows who attends each service («consultorios sin médico»)
Decided: (1) The desk could already fill in a name from the ficha (`PUT /api/admin/patients/<dni>`; `user_fill_name` from the portal only ever fills BLANK fields, so a desk edit is never overwritten by a later portal login); the edit now writes an `edicion` row to the access log before it happens (a row that can't be written refuses it; an unknown DNI is a 404 with no row). (2) In the hospital's words a «consultorio» is a service. The Servicios list gets a default «Médicos» column (the names, linking to each record, or «Sin médico»; sortable, so the empty ones come first) and the service form a «Médicos que lo atienden» row with the same list and, when empty, where to assign one. The list is `schedule_policy.pool` (the assignable doctors the booking wizard uses), exposed as `_medicos` next to `_schedule_doctor_count`. Assigning stays on the doctor's side (the schedule band's service), where the relation lives. · Why: Salud (hospital technical contact) 2026-11-02: «posibilidad de editar esos pacientes que no tienen nombre» and «todavía hay consultorios que no tienen médico asignado»; the panel never said which services had nobody · Rejected: a doctor picker on the service form (it would write the doctor's schedule bands from the service side, a second place that defines the same relation); an access row for the edit that is written after it (a failed write would leave an unlogged change) · Cost: one `pool` pass per service on the Servicios list · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `admin_panel/admin_patients_router.py`, `core/access_log.py`, `admin_panel/admin_content.py`, `static/platform/admin-content.mjs`, `locales/es/admin.content.json`, `locales/es/admin.queue.json`; `test/test_admin_patients_router.py`, `test/test_slice6_design_pull.py`.

## 2026-11-02 · Pacientes: one line per person, the doctor is found by typing, no counters, «Nuevo paciente» on the title row
Decided: (1) The «Paciente» column reads like people write it, «Nombre Apellido, 48» (one shared formatter, `personaEdad` in `format.mjs` mirrored by `person_age` in `format.py`, parity-tested), with the DNI as small text under it; the separate DNI and Edad columns are gone, so the age is not repeated per row. (2) The doctor dropdown and the `?medico=` parameter are removed: the search box (`?q=`) also finds a doctor by name and returns that doctor's patients (the list «Pacientes del médico» reads, assignment-approved ones included), added to the by-name matches without repeats; a query of only digits is still a DNI. The doctor role stays scoped to its own patients. (3) The turnos count stays as a plain number; what it counts and the next appointment are in the hover hint. The counter chips are gone. (4) «Nuevo paciente» sits at the right of the title row (template `title_band`), not in the toolbar. · Why: Product owner 2026-11-02 (one row for the title and the button, no counters, no doctor filter because there will be many doctors, «name, surname, comma, age», the DNI small underneath; finding 22 of the polish findings) · Rejected: a doctor combo with type-ahead (the search box already does the job); keeping the DNI in its own column (the same datum in two typefaces) · Cost: a search by doctor name does one extra lookup of that doctor's patients · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `static/platform/admin-patients.mjs`, `static/platform/format.mjs`, `core/format.py`, `admin_panel/admin_patients.py`, `templates/admin/pacientes.html`, `admin-direct.css`, `locales/es/admin.queue.json`, `locales/en/admin.queue.json`; `test/test_admin_patients.py`, `test/admin-patients-lifecycle.test.mjs`, `test/admin-pacientes-ui.test.mjs`, `test/ui-evals.test.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-patients-lifecycle.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-02 · Pacientes: what repeats on every row is not written (no «Sin cobertura», no «Activo», no «—»), the phone card keeps one size
Decided: (1) A patient without coverage has an empty Cobertura cell, a patient without granted appointments an empty Turnos cell, and a phone equal to the email an empty Teléfono cell: only what differs is shown. (2) The Estado column is off by default («Activo» on every row is noise); an archived person is marked «· Archivado» next to the DNI and the Activos/Archivados filter still separates them. (3) On the phone card every fact after the name (Turnos N, coverage, status) has the DNI's face, 14px `--text-2`, so rows measure the same. (4) The link underline stays as the theme tokens define it (`tokens.css`, rule of 2026-09-14, `ui-evals` eval 8). (5) A column flagged `ocultaVacia` (Cobertura, Email, Turnos) disappears whole, header included, while every visible row is empty there, and returns with the first value (`activeCols()` applies it, so the width engine agrees). · Why: ui-expert review of the 390/1440 shots, 2026-11-02, applying the product owner's «if you write the same thing in every row, take it out of the rows» · Rejected: removing the underline of the names (a repo-wide theme rule, not a Pacientes choice); deleting the «Prueba Barra» demo patients (data, not code) · Cost: the default list can no longer sort by state without turning the column on · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `static/platform/admin-patients.mjs`, `test/admin-pacientes-ui.test.mjs`, `test/admin-patients-lifecycle.test.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-patients-lifecycle.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-03 · One way to load a list of doctors: «Cargar lista» in Médicos; /admin/users/import is gone
Decided: (1) A roster is loaded only from «Cargar lista» inside «Invitar médico» on `/admin/doctors`. It took over what the separate `/admin/users/import` page had and it lacked: an Excel (.xlsx) file is sent as it is, a CSV/TXT file fills the text box, and the list is first read without writing (a preview: per line what will be created, what already exists, what has an error), then «Crear N médicos» sends the same list to create. Columns empty in every row are not drawn; the line number only when a row failed; a doctor created without email gets the access link in its row. (2) No invitation checkbox: as with «Invitar médico», a row with an email gets the invitation by email, a row without one gets the link to copy. (3) The one server path is `POST /api/admin/doctors/import` (permission «medicos», same as the button; the engine `admin_panel/doctor_import.py` is unchanged); `POST /api/admin/users/import` is removed. (4) `GET /admin/users/import` answers 308 to `/admin/doctors?import=1`, which opens the panel with the list in focus; its page, script, story, e2e spec and the link in Usuarios are deleted. · Why: two screens did the same job with different rules (the Usuarios page with a preview and Excel, the Médicos panel without either but where the desk works); pinamar-turnos 2026-11-03 (m1587346): «one path: «Cargar lista» in Médicos stays» · Rejected: keeping the Usuarios page as a second entry (two permission sets, «usuarios» vs «medicos», for one action); creating row by row from the browser as before (no preview, one request per doctor, no Excel) · Cost: the preview reads the file twice (once to show, once to create) · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `admin_panel/admin_content_router.py`, `admin_panel/admin_users_router.py`, `admin_panel/admin_users_screen.py`, `core/permissions.py`, `static/platform/admin-content.mjs`, `admin-direct.css`, `templates/admin/users.html`, `locales/es/admin.content.json`, `locales/en/admin.content.json`, `locales/es/admin.doctor_import.json`; `test/test_doctor_import.py`, `test/admin-create-appointment-native.test.mjs`, `test/e2e/journey-j28-role-and-session-boundaries.spec.mjs`; supersedes the 2026-10 entry for `/admin/users/import`.

## 2026-11-03 · Pacientes de hoy: the day sits beside the search, the filters wrap as one group; stacked cards have no inset of their own
Decided: (1) The date, «Hoy» and «Ver mes» follow the search box on the same row; the doctor/specialty/center/service filters, «Mostrar horarios libres» and the column picker are one group (`.queue-filters`) that wraps whole onto its own row when it does not fit: two rows on a laptop, never three ragged ones. (2) A stacked doctor card and, on the phone, a stacked row of the day start on the section's edge (no extra 16px of their own), the same edge as the search, the date and «Médicos». (3) On the phone the doctor's checkbox in Médicos sits on the name's line (float), not alone on a line above it. · Why: Opus review of the admin UI built on Sonnet (the product owner 2026-11-03, m1583745), findings 9 and 10 · Rejected: keeping the day inside the wrapping group (it dropped under the search with the filters and left the search alone on its row) · Cost: none measured · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-03 · The hosted GitLab pipeline no longer starts by itself; the patients list sorts the same on any server collation; the «Solicitud enviada» receipt links the request's calendar file from the date row
Decided: (1) `.gitlab-ci.yml` keeps its five jobs but its `workflow` admits only a pipeline started by hand (`web`): a push or a merge request starts nothing, because the same gate runs on this machine (`~/.claude/bin/local-ci`). (2) The alphabetical order of the patients list (`core.db_accounts.users_admin_list`) is computed on a key that folds case and accents in SQL (`_clave_alfabetica`), so it no longer depends on the collation of the server: the CI database (`postgres:16-alpine`, C collation) put «Álvarez» after «Zárate» and «de la Torre» before «Pérez». (3) The success receipt of a booking shows «Agregar al calendario» next to «Fecha y hora» as a link to `/api/appointment/<id>.ics` (the file of the saved request, session-gated), and no longer repeats the Google/.ics block under the summary; Confirmá keeps its own block. A booking without a real date shows no link. · Why: main's GitLab checks had been red since 31.10: the e2e `patient-portal` expected the date-row link that the 31.10 rebase of the receipt change had dropped from `service.mjs`, and the patients ordering test failed on the CI collation; the «Servicios» layout failures of 02–03.11 (`admin-table-columns-fit`, `catalog-screens-lifecycle` S01) came from the new «Médicos» column and were already fixed by `2639599f`; `hsi-sync-entries` passed on its retry (one flaky) · Rejected: pinning the CI database collation (hides the dependence instead of removing it); weakening the e2e expectation (the link is the product behaviour the product owner asked for on 28.10) · Cost: with no hosted run the lane-balance refresh (`scripts/update_backend_timings.py`, reads the last green `main` runs) has no fresh data; run a pipeline by hand when it is needed · Undo: restore the three `workflow` rules · By: agent.
Source: `.gitlab-ci.yml`, `core/db_accounts.py`, `modules/turnos/service.mjs`; `test/test_admin_patients.py`, `test/e2e/confirma-telefono.spec.mjs`, `test/e2e/patient-portal.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-03 · Phone and email are icons everywhere in the panel, Pacientes shows attendance as three bars and the doctors as «name +N», the search also finds a specialty, and the ficha opens on the patient's turnos
Decided: (1) A phone or an email is never printed as text in a panel list or card: it is an Icons8 icon (`phone`, `email`) that opens the phone or mail client (`tel:`, `mailto:`), the value and whose it is sit in the tooltip, a right click (a long press on a phone) copies it and says «Copiado». One shared function, `Format.contactoIconoHtml`, used by Pacientes, the doctor's «Mis turnos» / «Mis pacientes», Centros and the request modal; the WhatsApp links of the doctor's screens are gone with the printed numbers. (2) Pacientes: the age filter is removed (age is in the name, «Nombre Apellido, 48»); the DNI is its own column again, small; «Asistencia» is three bars (level, no words; computed from the patient's turnos when the account has no record) with the detail in the tooltip; a new «Médico» column shows the doctor with most turnos and «+1», «+2» for the rest, all of them in the tooltip (`Format.medicosTexto`, mirrored in `format.py`). (3) The search box also matches a doctor's specialty. (4) The ficha opens on the patient's turnos with their actions (Confirmar, Cancelar, Atendió, No vino); the data form (name, DNI, contact, coverage, dependents) is behind «Editar datos», which opens by itself for a new patient or one with no name, so completing nameless accounts stays one click. The top is one line, «Nombre Apellido, 48», the DNI small beside it, phone and email as icons, the three bars, the doctors, and «Editar datos» at the right. · Why: Product owner 2026-11-03 (translated from Russian): why were phones and emails typed out for everyone, make icons that open the mail or phone client and can be copied; one line per patient with the DNI apart; no age filter; search by the doctor's specialty; show which doctors a patient is booked with (the most frequent plus «+1»); and about the ficha, fix the patient's turnos (cancel, confirm), not the patient · Rejected: keeping the numbers as text next to the icon (the same datum twice); a doctor filter (many doctors, the search does it); editing data as the ficha's first screen · Cost: one grouped query per roster page for the doctors · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `static/platform/admin-patients.mjs`, `static/platform/format.mjs`, `core/format.py`, `static/platform/admin-content.mjs`, `admin_panel/admin_patients.py`, `core/db_booking.py`, `static/icons/phone-sf-regular.svg`, `locales/es/admin.queue.json`, `locales/en/admin.queue.json`; `test/test_admin_patients.py`, `test/formato.test.mjs`, `test/ui-evals.test.mjs`, `test/admin-patients-lifecycle.test.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-patients-lifecycle.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-03 · The deploy smoke checks every fingerprinted asset, not only those the home page links
Decided: `scripts/smoke_prod.py` (and `smoke_demo.py`, which reuses it) compare with the local checkout every platform asset in `core.static_fingerprint.MIGRATED` and every module asset declared with `fingerprint: true`, each fetched by its content-hashed URL, instead of only the assets the pages it opens happen to link. A test serves the previous build of a panel-only stylesheet and expects the smoke to fail. · Why: `/admin/login` does not link `admin-direct.css`, so a deploy that left the panel's stylesheet on the previous build passed the smoke (pinamar-turnos 2026-11-03, m1615528) · Rejected: opening a logged-in panel page in the smoke (needs a staff credential on the test sites for a check that only compares bytes) · Cost: a few dozen more requests per smoke run · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `scripts/smoke_prod.py`; `test/test_smoke_prod_assets.py`, `test/test_smoke_demo_assets.py`.

## 2026-11-03 · A doctor has one email: «Email» in Información básica is also its account's
Decided: (1) The code has no login email separate from the contact one: the invitation, the password reset and the municipal-portal match all read the account's `operators.email`, while the doctor record's `email` was read nowhere else except as a fallback. So the doctor has ONE field, «Email» in Información básica, and the two copies follow each other: saving the doctor form writes the account's email (`admin_users.doctor_email_changed`), editing the account's email (Usuarios, «Mi cuenta» of another user) writes the record's (`admin_users.follow_account_email` → `db_catalog.catalog_set_doctor_email`), a cleared field clears both. (2) The «Usuario» tab of a doctor shows that email read-only, with a line pointing to Información básica when there is none; it no longer has an email input of its own, and «Invitar» uses the record's email. (3) On an installation where staff log in through the portal (`TURNOS_STAFF_PORTAL_LOGIN=1`) an email already used by another active account is refused on the doctor form's field (409 `email_en_uso`, «Ese email ya es de otra cuenta activa.») before anything is saved. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-03 (m1615528), point 4: «no se distinguen — reducir a un campo» · Rejected: keeping two fields with an explanation (two places to type one fact; they had already drifted: the old fill-only copy never overwrote the record) · Cost: an account's email change rewrites the doctor's catalog overlay row · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `admin_panel/admin_users.py`, `admin_panel/admin_content.py`, `admin_panel/admin_account.py`, `core/db_catalog.py`, `static/platform/admin-users.mjs`, `static/platform/admin-content.mjs`, `locales/es/admin.system.json`, `locales/es/admin.content.json`; `test/test_doctor_account_unify.py`, `test/test_admin_users.py`, `test/test_db_catalog_store.py`, `test/admin-users-native.test.mjs`, `test/doctor-user-onboarding.browser.mjs`.

## 2026-11-03 · Horarios: a booking limit says whether it is the doctor's own or inherited, and from where
Decided: Under each booking limit of a doctor's (and a service's) Horarios a line tells its origin, resolved by the same chain the booking uses (`booking_limits.own_limit`, `booking_horizon.own_days`, doctor → service → site): «Propio de este médico», «Heredado del servicio <nombre>: N», or «Heredado del sitio: N». The admin catalog projection carries it as `_booking_defaults.patient_from` / `days_from`; the panel renders it with `admin-hours-editor.limitOriginHtml`, and the line follows the field as it is typed (empty → inherited, a number → own). · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-03 (m1615528), point 5 · Rejected: a tooltip (hidden on a phone) · Cost: one more line per limit · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-03 · Usuarios: the owner's row is read-only; «Mis pacientes» is not in the admin's menu
Decided: (1) The installation owner's row in Usuarios is shown without edit controls, with one line saying who can change it. (2) A module's `admin_sections[]` entry may carry `nav_hidden_for: [roles]`; `screen_nav.nav_items` leaves that entry out of the menu for those roles while the route keeps answering (a link or a bookmark still opens). «Mis pacientes» carries `["admin"]`: the admin reaches any patient from Pacientes. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-03 (m1615528), point 3a · Rejected: removing the route for admins (breaks deep links and the doctor's own preview) · Cost: none · Undo: drop the manifest key · By: agent.

## 2026-11-03 · Service form: one layout, booking limits as plain number fields
Decided: Every field of the service form is a `cnt-row` (label left, control right): «Quién define el horario» is a checkbox row with its hint and status under it, «Horario del servicio» and «Cierres» are rows, the booking limits are rows (`bookingLimitsHtml(prefix, item, defaults, formRow)`), «Destacado» is a checkbox row with «Posición» and «Etiqueta» as rows under it. A booking limit is a plain number input (`limitFieldHtml`, placeholder = the inherited value), as in Ajustes › Turnos, everywhere it appears (service, doctor, center window, overbooking percent); the «cada» stepper of a time band stays, it is an interval, not a limit. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-03 (m1615528), point 2a: «una sola disposición, el límite como campo, como en Ajustes» · Rejected: keeping sections with their own headings next to rows (three layouts in one form) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-03 · Calendario: a day with appointments shows how many
Decided: The doctor's Calendario month grid shows the booked count under the day number (`medCalGridDias` passes `count`, the shared `calendar-grid.mjs` renders it, as Pacientes de hoy's calendar already did); the occupancy colour stays. A free working day shows only its number. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-03 (m1615528), point 6: a free day and a day with bookings were the same green cell · Rejected: a dot (says «something», not how much) · Cost: taller cells on grids that carry counts · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-03 · Erasures are applied again to a restored database, from tombstones kept outside it
Decided: after any restore from a backup, `scripts/reapply_erasures.py` carries the recorded erasures out again before the app serves anything (runbook step 7 of `docs/operations/backup-and-restore.md`). Dry-run by default; `--apply` writes. The tombstones are the `erased` events of the ledger (the person as an HMAC of the DNI, the mode, the instant) of the restored database plus any `--from-file` written by `--export` on the live installation: a backup older than an erasure holds neither the erasure nor its ledger line, so the export is kept next to the recovery key, outside the database. Agent calls (reversible): the erasure that applies to an account is the first one after its creation, an account newer than all erasures of its DNI is a new person and is left alone; a person with a live appointment, a pending HSI intent or an unresolved conflict is not touched unless `--despite-blockers` (the erasure's own rule, and its proofs do not cover those HSI rows); the tombstone file carries a fingerprint of the access-log key and a file of another key is refused; idempotent by the same proofs as the erasure; the report holds counts, never a DNI, a name or a hash. Legacy B2 versions, processors and people found only by phone, email or text stay manual (Q6) · Why: an erased person survives in every older backup (spec P5, `TURNOS_BACKUP_RETENTION_APPLY=0` by default) · Rejected: keeping the tombstones only in the database (a restore removes them with the data); one tombstone per person (a DNI erased twice is two people) · Undo: revert the commit; an erasure already re-applied is not undone — restore the backup again · By: agent
Source: `core/erasure_reapply.py`, `core/subject_erase.py` (`carry_out`, `pending`), `core/subject_requests.py` (`erasures`, `note_reapplied`), `scripts/reapply_erasures.py`; `test/test_reapply_erasures.py`.

## 2026-11-04 · Ficha del paciente: empty «Personas a cargo» is one button; «Quién abrió» actions are normal buttons; panel hints are dark grey
Decided: (1) A patient with nobody in charge and nobody they are in charge of shows no heading, no «Sin personas a cargo.» line and no small link: only the button «Agregar persona a cargo»; with people the list stays as it was. (2) The two actions under «Quién abrió sus datos» («Ver el registro», «Descargar sus datos») are `btn btn-quiet pat-act` buttons (44px, one line, never a column at 390). (3) The table engine draws no resize handle on a last column with no title (the actions column); its handle was the stray divider at the end of the header. (4) The hints under form fields of the panel (`.cnt-hint`, the limit copy of Horarios and its placeholders) take one token, `--hint-ink` in `tokens.css` (dark grey in both themes, ~9:1 on the panel), not the translucent `--text-2` scale or a colour written in place; the booking limits of the service, doctor, centre window and Ajustes keep one geometry (40px, 16px, 8/12 padding) and the native number spinner, which Chrome shows on hover only. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-04 (d1628839), review of build eb164c36 · Rejected: a single merged action under «Quién abrió» (the two actions differ: read the log vs download) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-04 · Security review of patient data: a referral file is served with a type taken from its bytes; a staff session dies when the password changes
Decided: (1) A referral file (the patient's photo or PDF of the order) is served with a type taken only from its sniffed bytes (png, jpg, webp, pdf, gif, heic); anything else is an attachment named orden.bin as application/octet-stream, always with nosniff, on both the desk route and the doctor route. The type the patient declared at upload is never echoed. (2) The staff cookie carries a short stamp of the account's password hash; changing or resetting the password (self, admin, owner) closes every other session of that account, and the browser that changed it gets a fresh cookie. A cookie minted before this change has no stamp and stays valid until it expires or the password changes, so the deploy logs nobody out. Surfaces, findings and cards (TASK-68 to TASK-73) are in docs/compliance/security-review-2026-11-surfaces.md. · Why: pinamar-turnos sprint 2026-11-04, security review of patient data · Rejected: a server-side session table (a new table with personal data for no gain over the stamp); sanitising HTML in uploads (the allowlist of types is simpler and holds) · Cost: one indexed password-hash read per staff request; an automatic hash upgrade at login closes the account's other sessions once · Undo: revert the commit; no schema change · By: agent.

## 2026-11-04 · Pacientes, tercera ola: WhatsApp junto al teléfono, columna «Servicio» en lugar de Cobertura, título y ficha sin repeticiones
Decided: (1) The shared contact formatter (`Format.contactoIconoHtml`, used by Pacientes, the ficha, Mis turnos, Mis pacientes, Centros and the request window) draws an Icons8 WhatsApp icon (`whatsapp-sf-regular.svg`, project pack) next to the phone icon: `https://wa.me/<549…>` in a new tab, same tooltip pattern («Escribirle por WhatsApp — <número> — <de quién>») and same right-click copy as the phone; a mail and a 0800 get none. (2) The list column «Cobertura» becomes «Servicio»: the main service of the person, derived from their live appointments (approved or pending, `db_booking.services_by_dnis`, one query per page) and shown only when one service has at least 2 appointments and at least half of them (`admin_patients.MAIN_SERVICE_MIN = 2`, `MAIN_SERVICE_SHARE = 0.5`); a tie for first place or no leader leaves the cell empty, the column hides itself when every visible row is empty, and it sorts like any other. Cobertura stays in the ficha («Editar datos») and as a filter. (3) The appointments table of the ficha never prints the patient's name (it is the title of the page). (4) Rows of that table are as tall with buttons as without (60px, buttons 44px; 52px action line on the 390 cards). (5) The panel title above an open ficha is hidden (the breadcrumb «Pacientes ›» stays; the patient's name is the page's one h1). (6) «Agregar persona a cargo» opens a form without its own heading (only editing one person has the h3 with their name) and the button is hidden while the form is open. (7) The title rows (name + «Editar datos»; «Pacientes» + «Nuevo paciente») align on the text baseline, not on the box centre. · Why: Product owner 2026-11-04 on the Space page «Turnos Pinamar: todos los turnos del paciente» and the demo (d1643347) · Rejected: an AI-written specialty per patient (the appointments already say it); the most frequent service with no threshold (one visit would name a «main» service) · Cost: one grouped query per list page · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-04 · Pacientes, tercera ola (adición): una sola función para copiar el DNI, contador en pastilla, médicos que entran, turno en dos renglones
Decided: (1) Every DNI shown in the panel goes through `Format.dniHtml` (`data-copy-dni` carries the digits only; `Format.instalarCopiaDni` is the one delegated handler): on a pointer device hovering the DNI shows a small Icons8 copy icon (`copy-sf-regular.svg`) and a click copies; on a phone there is no icon and a tap copies; both show the toast «Copiado» (fallback `execCommand` when `navigator.clipboard` is missing). 15 places use it (Pacientes list, ficha head, personas a cargo, appointments table, queue, requests window, Mis turnos, Mis pacientes, create-appointment result, content search, dependents); left out on purpose: the create-appointment search list and the `dep-choice` button (the whole row is a choice button, a nested control would be invalid), and the auth-test row. (2) «Turnos N» is no longer repeated in each list row: the number is a pill at the right edge of the first line (`title`/`aria-label` say «Turnos»); the column header stays on desktop. (3) The attendance bars come right after the name on the same line, 14px high with 4px bars, bottom on the text baseline (checked on 3x frames), in the list and in the ficha title; the ranking column is not a default column any more. (4) The doctors line of the list shows as many names as fit and «+N» only for those that do not (`ajustarMedicos`, re-run by a ResizeObserver on the table); the ficha has no doctors line, every appointment already names its doctor. (5) An appointment of the ficha is two lines: line 1 = status (dot + word) + «Hoy, 09:00» + doctor, large and dark; line 2 = service · centre in dark grey (`--hint-ink`, the translucent `--text-2` of the humanrounds theme read as light grey). Past appointments show the date without time («2 de octubre», the year only when it is not the current one: `Dates.pasada`) and the doctor on the same line. (6) Status, date and doctor are ONE line at every width (the product owner, 2026-11-04, second message: on the phone «time + doctor + status in the first line»). What does not fit is shortened in steps, the least visible first, each step measured with the previous one applied (`ajustarTurnos`, re-run by a ResizeObserver): 1) the pending status in its short word («Pendiente», key `patient_appts.pending_short`) and the date abbreviated (`Dates.corta`: «Sáb 14 nov, 09:00»); 2) one point smaller type (16/13 px); 3) the doctor as «C. Larralde» (title kept, two last words with four or more; the full name stays in the `title`); 4) an ellipsis on the doctor. Measured on 50 patients and 50 appointments with the real name lengths of the Pinamar pack (doctors median 15, p90 21, max 27 characters; services median 17, p90 34, max 64; centres median 20, p90 39, max 44): at 1440 all 50 fit in full (step 0); at 390 all 50 are on one line, 28 in full, 11 at step 1, 5 at step 2, 6 at step 3, none needs the ellipsis; nothing overflows at either width. The first version (status on its own line when the unit did not fit, 22 of 50 at 390) was replaced. (7) 390px edges: one left and one right edge at 16px for the list cards, the ficha, the appointments and «Personas a cargo» (cards instead of a five-column table). · Why: Product owner 2026-11-04 (messages after d1643347: edges, copy DNI, doctors that fit, bars, appointment layout) · Rejected: a tooltip-only copy (invisible on a phone); status on its own line at 390 (the product owner wants all three in the first line); wrapping the line (the row height would depend on the name) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-04 · Opus review of the panel decisions made by Sonnet: what was redone
Decided: every panel decision of the Sonnet-written commits since 2026-10-28 was reviewed by Opus against ui-design-polish, ui-rules and `admin_panel/AGENTS.md`; the ones that broke a rule are redone, the rest stay. (1) Médicos: «Cargar lista» is its own toolbar button and panel next to «Invitar médico» (it was hidden under the one-doctor form); its file chooser is the portrait's control (native input hidden, «Elegir archivo» button, the chosen name beside it); `?import=1` opens it; on a phone the column button and the two quiet entries share one row and «+ Nuevo médico» takes the next. (2) Servicios: «Sin médico» only on a service booked by appointment; a walk-in service never gets a doctor, so it shows «—» and its form has no doctors row; the hint is one phrase with a link to Médicos. (3) Estado: the WhatsApp handoff number goes through the one phone formatter; «Dirección de los enlaces» shows only when the public origin is wrong or differs from the domain. (4) The form hint colour (`--hint-ink`) also covers the service preview and the Ajustes lede. (5) Pacientes de hoy › Ver mes: no counter line under the calendar; an attention day with nothing booked says so in the cell's title. (6) Mi cuenta › Mis turnos, empty: the button says «Sacar turno», as everywhere else. (7) Reportes › Capacidad: a tie names up to three specialties and counts the rest; «sumar horas» appears only at ≥90 % (the hero's threshold, pinned by a test); the card puts specialties with demand and no agenda first, as the hero does; one request / one specialty is written in the singular. (8) Médico › Usuario: the role is a «Rol» row with an «Administrador» checkbox that saves on change (the «Activo» checkbox of Usuarios), not a button that flips its own label; a refusal puts the box back and says why. (9) Same tab: the account buttons stay on one line, and at ≤520 px stack in one column on one left edge. (10) Same tab: «Cambiar la dirección del calendario» asks first, in place, with the doctor's own words from Mis turnos («Cambiar la dirección» / «No cambiarla»); the answer that kills the old address is the panel's destructive button (`btn btn-no`) on both screens, replacing the doctor's `mt-cal-danger`, whose red the panel's button rule overrode. (11) Same tab: «Reenviar invitación» is gone once the account has logged in and is active; a disabled account keeps «Invitar», which re-enables it. (12) Médico › Horarios: the center's field is «Días de anticipación en este centro» with one line saying where the value comes from (inherited from the doctor / service / site, or own), instead of a second «Días de anticipación» and a developer hint. (13) Solicitudes: the HSI column is as wide as its longest label (208 px, «Envío automático a HSI» on one line), and it shows only where HSI really takes the turno — the push is on or the extension marked the page — the same rule as Pacientes de hoy; the list repaints when the extension's mark appears; with a request open on a wide screen the list takes its 761-1200 px layout (the same five tracks and hidden columns, HSI included — the detail carries the open request's control), because hiding Centro alone left its track in the grid and every later cell slid one track left, «Cargar en HSI» past the list's edge; the HSI cell lets the extension's hint popover out instead of cutting it. (14) «Recién aprobadas» is a group of the same queue engine (Paciente / Servicio / Turno / HSI) above the column header, not a separate bordered card that pushed the queue down. (15) The request card's agenda «Reintentar» is the quiet small button every other inline retry uses, not the reject red next to «Aprobar». (16) After «Derivar», the card writes the new center into the request's own center span (`.sol-pide-centro`) — it went to a dead `.svc-current` and the card kept the old center. (17) A cancelled request's «Turno / motivo» cell says «Por el paciente» / «Por el centro» (the Estado column already says «Cancelada»); without who cancelled, it shows the turno date. · Why: Product owner 2026-11-03 (m1583745): re-check with Opus the panel decisions Sonnet made · Rejected: reverting the Sonnet commits wholesale (most decisions were right; the product owner's accepted changes stay) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

Decided: The copy-DNI icon (`Format.dniHtml`, `.dni-cp-ico`) keeps the 20px drawing of the phone and WhatsApp icons beside it (mask size 20px), but its box is 14px with a 4px left margin: the row takes 18px, as before the 20px change. Two node tests hold it: the mask size equals `.cnt-ico`, and margin + box is at most 18px with the drawing inside the box. · Why: main red since 8a23ca8f (pinamar-turnos, 2026-11-04) · Rejected: a 20px box with negative margins (a right overhang widens a zero-padding cell by the overhang: the Pacientes list at 390 overflowed by 4px; all of it on the left leaves 2px between the digits and the icon); an absolutely placed icon over the cell (overlaps the next column) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-04 · The width engine drops an empty column before cutting a name; the Calendario day table restacks on a phone
Decided: Two panel layout defects, both markup. (1) «Pacientes de hoy» fitted its table against the FIRST `.cnt-table-wrap` of `#cola-results` — with a doctor picked that is the doctors' list (353px at 1440) — so every column sat at its minimum («Miguel Sosa…», «Ginecologí…») and ~120px stayed empty beside Asistencia; `medio` now returns the wrap of `table.queue-table`. The shared engine (`admin-table.mjs`) gains two column options: `cfg.vacia(key)` names a column whose cells are all «—» (the queue: every cell `.queue-td-empty`), which falls before the declared `drop` order; `entero: true` (the queue's Paciente and Servicio) counts its measured width in the floor and shrinks only after the other elastic columns reached their minimum, so a name is cut only when nothing else can give way. The doctors' list column takes `clamp(240px,28%,360px)` (was 30%). (2) The doctors' LIST phone rules (`#sec-medicos .cnt-table{display:block…}`, wrapper `overflow:visible`) also caught the Calendario day table in the same section, stretching a 390 page to 1066px; they now name `.cnt-table--medicos` (and the wrapper `:has(>.cnt-table--medicos)`), and the day table restacks under 1024 like «Mis turnos»: time and patient on the first line, age, DNI, contact, service and centre under the name, an empty cell takes no room (each cell carries `data-col`/`data-label`). On a computer the day table's patient and service wrap to a second line instead of «…», like the centre. Node tests: `test/admin-table-fit-priority.test.mjs` (red on the old engine), `admin-content-direct`/`admin-content-ui` pin the scoped selectors and the restack. · Why: pinamar-turnos, 2026-11-04 (demo, after 790fa020) · Rejected: horizontal scroll for the day table on a phone (the neighbouring tables restack); `fill` for the queue (the empty space was a wrong medium, not a missing fill); dropping DNI first at 1440 (it has data, the empty Cobertura goes first) · Cost: at 1280 the queue still cuts the age and service of the longest names by ~100px — the whole row needs more than the screen has · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-05 · Four audit leftovers: «Ver mes» toolbar, the demo doctor's state, «Mis turnos», the account's buttons
Decided: (1) In the doctor's day queue an open «Ver mes» month made the controls row tall and the centred row put the search and the filters halfway down the calendar. On a screen wider than 640px the open month now leaves the date's box (`display:contents` on `.queue-date-picker`) and takes a line of its own under the whole toolbar, at its left edge (`order:1` plus a right margin that fills the line); the search, the day and the filters keep exactly the closed row's geometry at every width, like every other list's toolbar. The phone keeps the month under the date. (2) The demo doctor read «Invitado» in the admin's Médicos list: the account state is right (an active account that never logged in IS invited) — the root was the demo. The one-click `/admin/demo-doctor-access` minted a session without recording the visit every login records (`admin_auth.marcar_acceso`), and the seed never finished the doctor's onboarding although the demo doctor arrives with profile, agendas and a full day. Now the click records the visit and the seed completes the onboarding (idempotent, the first time stays), so the account reads «Perfil completo» and its password login opens the day's work instead of the first-day onboarding. (3) The patient account's «Mis turnos» tab titled its page «Mis solicitudes»: the h1 now uses the tab's own key (`account.dash.nav_turnos`). The other texts that send the patient to «Mis solicitudes» (mail, the wizard's success, the offer, the pre-consult back links, the chat's section chip) are left for a retranslation pass of the eight generated locales. (4) The doctor account's buttons (Usuario tab) were 44-48px tall with a dead 20px font rule: they take the panel's button token (`--adm-btn-h`, 44px on touch) and its 14px type; the catalog form's Guardar keeps 48px beside its 48px fields, without a font size of its own. · Why: Opus audit of the panel and the patient account, pinamar-turnos 2026-11-05 · Rejected: top-aligning the row (items of 40, 44 and 47px still put the column picker 3px off the day's line, and the closed row moved too); a grid for the open state (where the closed row wraps the filters, the grid squeezed them into a column); relabelling «Invitado» or changing `operator_account_state` (the rule is right for real accounts); showing the onboarding to demo visitors (the demo shows a doctor at work; the one-click entry already skipped it) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit; a reseed does not undo the completed onboarding, `UPDATE operators SET onboarding_completed_at=NULL WHERE username='gonzalez'` does · By: agent.

## 2026-11-05 · Blind review of the Opus panel redo: four Sonnet calls come back, and one plural rule for every «N + noun»
Decided: A blind review of 20 Sonnet/Opus pairs of the panel audit (616ba35a) preferred Sonnet in four; those return. (A8) The doctor account's «Restablecer contraseña» and «Restablecer 2FA» share one row, at 390px too (`.doctor-user-actions.cnt-actions{flex-wrap:wrap}`; the one-per-line phone column is gone). (D14) The patient's button in «Mis solicitudes» says «Sacar un turno», as the original brief asked. (C1/C2) The HSI column of Solicitudes Aprobadas/Todas shows wherever the desk extension serves this site (`hsi_extension`) or the push is on, without waiting for the page mark the extension sets after load — on the demo the mark never came, so the column was never there; with a request open above 1200px the column keeps its track and its label wraps instead of being cut. (C4) «Recién aprobadas» is a card above the queue (`#hsi-recientes`), one line «name · service · time» per approval and no counter; it is no longer a group of rows inside `#list`, so J11/J23 count only pending rows. (B1) A tie: the service row keeps «Sin médico» as Opus left it. Number agreement: `static/platform/i18n.mjs` `pluralCategory()`/`tn()` and their server twin `core/i18n.py` `plural_category()`/`tn_or()` are the one rule; a catalog key `k` holds the plural and `k_one` the singular; the CLDR «one» category picks it (Intl.PluralRules on the client, a ten-language table on the server, both pinned by `test/fixtures/plural-one.json`); a singular with no `{n}` in it (a whole phrase, «¿Aprobar la solicitud?») is used only for exactly 1, since French «one» covers 0 and Russian «one» covers 21; few/many fold into the plural key. 39 client call sites in 16 files and 9 server sites now go through it, plus the «(s)»/«(es)» strings («archivo(s)», «idioma(s)», «emisión(es)», «módulo(s)»); `test/i18n-plural.test.mjs` fails when a `Tn("key", …)` call lacks `key` or `key_one` in es or en. Log-only lines (housekeeping, worker) keep «(s)». · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-05, blind review by ui-expert of the Opus redo; the panel said «1 solicitudes», «1 especialidades» · Rejected: per-call `n === 1 ? a : b` ternaries (they were the bug: each site decided alone, most never did); Russian few/many keys (the panel is used in Spanish; the generated languages get one/other) · Cost: 47 new catalog keys per language · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-05 · The home's centers are a black-and-white map drawn once from OpenStreetMap and served by us
Decided: «Centros de salud» on the home is a map instead of the compact list. (1) The drawing is a pack file, `centros-mapa.svg`, built once by `scripts/build_centers_map.py` from OpenStreetMap (Overpass): white land, sea and lakes in light grey, streets in four greys by importance, no street names, no icons, `non-scaling-stroke`; ~60 KB, one request, served from `/webdata/` with the content hash in `?v=` (cached a year). (2) `centros-mapa.json` next to it holds the frame (bbox, size), area names and one pin per center taken for the center's street key: a pin applies only while the center is at that place (the panel moves it → it leaves the map for the row under it), and lat/lng typed in the panel win (`site_centers._map_point`). `/api/centers` adds `mapa` and each center's `map_x`/`map_y` (0..1); without the file the home keeps the old list. (3) `static/platform/centers-map.mjs` lays a black dot per place and the names over it as HTML links (17 px, 600, a solid white outline that hides the streets under the letters; a service under a center at 16 px on one line; two places at one dot 8 px apart), each opening the Google Maps route in a new tab, the dot a 44 px target to the same route. Places closer than 26 px share one dot at their middle; an exact search puts every name beside its dot with no overlap, then a greedy one with leader lines only when that fails; every zoom from the closest down to the drawing filling the frame is tried, and the winner places every name with the least leader line, preferring the closer zoom; a name keeps at least 10 px from any other dot, and between layouts equal on lines and zoom the one whose names stay further from the other dots (under 22 px the eye may pair a name with the neighbour's dot) wins, a tie-break that never buys a line or a farther zoom (a hard 14 px gave the phone four lines). Deterministic, and the same whatever the order of the list (a test reverses it). 560 px high on a phone, 520 px above. (4) Centers without a point (CAPS Móvil) stay in a row under the map with their address written under the name. (5) The credit «© OpenStreetMap» links to the licence page, bottom right. · Why: Product owner 2026-11-05 (pinamar-turnos): a handsome black-and-white map instead of the list, a widget or a generated drawing · Rejected: a live map widget (Google Maps embed, OSM tiles, Mapbox): dozens of third-party requests on every visit with no CDN in front of us, the provider learns who opens a health site, its labels are small and grey; names inside the SVG (not links, not translatable, not read by screen readers, unreadable when the phone crop scales the drawing down); a raster image (blurry, heavier) · Cost: the drawing is rebuilt by hand when a center opens somewhere new (a center with panel coordinates is placed without a rebuild if it falls inside the frame); one more file per pack · Undo: delete `centros-mapa.json` from the pack (the home falls back to the list) or revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-05 · The specialties at the foot of the home fold under «Todas las especialidades»
Decided: The «Especialidades» links at the foot of the home are folded into one line, «Todas las especialidades» with the Icons8 sf-regular `expand-arrow`, pointing right while folded and down when open (`<details>`), under the one hairline it shares with the footer, which opens the same `/d/<section>` links packed by measured width as before (the packing now runs when the fold opens). The grid of specialties higher up already shows them, so the screen no longer repeats them; the links stay in the server-rendered HTML for crawlers and screen readers (the directory grid above is drawn by script), and the sitemap lists every `/d/` page on its own. Origin: the block came on 2026-10-09 in an SEO series (internal links from the home to every section page, after an SEO audit of 2026-10-01); nobody at the hospital asked for it; on 2026-10-17 the product owner shrank it to footer size. · Why: Product owner 2026-11-05 (pinamar-turnos): why are they there, they duplicate the page, fold or minimise them · Rejected: deleting the block (the home would lose its only crawlable links to the section pages); hiding it with `display:none` or off-screen text (hidden link text is what search engines treat as spam, and a hidden link is no use to a reader); the old open list at footer size (still the duplicate the product owner asked to remove) · Cost: one more tap for the reader who wants the list at the foot · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `templates/index.html`, `static/icons/expand-arrow-sf-regular.svg`; `test/test_site_identity.py`, `test/e2e/public-chrome.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-05 · When a name and a service cannot both fit, the width goes where less text hides
Decided: The shared width engine (`admin-table.mjs`) gains `cfg.anchosCelda(key)`: while measuring, it returns what each cell of an `entero` column needs. With it, the shortfall left after the other elastic columns reached their minimum is taken pixel by pixel from the `entero` column where that pixel cuts the fewest cells, times the column weight (`entero: 2` = its hidden text weighs double). Hidden text costs more the more is hidden, so the cheapest pixel each time gives the split that hides the least text overall. «Pacientes de hoy» measures the text of each Paciente and Servicio cell (the HSI button is not text: its place is the right padding) and gives Paciente weight 2 (the service is also in the doctor header). On the live demo at 1440 (Emiliano Molina, 5 Nov) one long name («Maximiliano Domínguez 14 años») split the shortfall with Servicio and all 31 rows read «Ginecología y Obstetri…»; now every service is whole and only that name loses part of its age. Without `anchosCelda` the old proportional split stays. Node tests in `test/admin-table-fit-priority.test.mjs`. · Why: live check of the 2026-11-04 fix (pinamar-turnos, demo after d21a4baf) · Rejected: dropping Historial at 1440 (it has data); narrowing the doctors list (its specialty and centre line would wrap); a fixed priority of Servicio over Paciente (with a different doctor on every row the services vary) · Cost: at 1280 the row still needs more than the screen has (44 cut cells instead of 51 on the same day) · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-05 · HSI extension: what has passed never enters the panel of changes; «Ocultar» after a refusal
Decided: the extension's box of changes left out nothing and closed nothing: a turno cancelled in the panel stayed there until HSI confirmed the cancel, and HSI refuses to change a turno whose hour has passed («HSI no deja cancelar este turno»), so cancelled turnos of past days stayed on a desk of the municipal installation for good. Now (extension 0.7.3) GET `/api/admin/day-queue/changes` also answers `ahora` (the installation's hour, `core.clock.now()`) and the extension leaves out every place behind it (`lib/shared.js` `cambiosPorPasar`, `yaPaso`): a cancel or a load of a past place is never offered, a move keeps only the half still ahead (an old place that passed only loads the new one, a move into the past only frees the old one), presence is marked until the day ends; without `ahora` (a panel not yet updated) the computer's own clock decides. An HSI refusal for a turno that passed meanwhile is not an error: the row goes with «Ya pasó, queda así en HSI». Any other refusal keeps its reason and a quiet «Ocultar»: POST `/api/admin/day-queue/changes/<id>/hide` (`hide_change_data`, the read's scopes, 404 outside them, access `cola`) writes an `appointment_events` row of kind `hsi_oculto` with the state, time and attendance unchanged (`db_booking.hide_hsi_change`), and the read answers `oculto` while the latest such row still matches the turno, so it holds for every computer of the desk until the turno changes again; the computer that hid it also keeps its own mark in `turnosEnHsi`. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-05, three past cancelled turnos on a desk with «Cancelar en HSI…» buttons that HSI refused · Rejected: filtering past turnos on the server (only the extension knows the place HSI holds, which differs from the turno after a move); a new table or column for the mark (the turno's history already records who did what to it, and no schema change keeps the release package small); a close button for the whole box (it would hide changes still to pass) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit; the `hsi_oculto` rows stay in the history and the old code ignores them · By: agent
Source: `extensions/hsi-humanizador/lib/shared.js`, `content-turnos.js`, `content-turnos.css`; `modules/turnos/doctor_appointments.py` (`day_changes_data`, `hide_change_data`), `doctor_appointments_router.py`; `core/db_booking.py` (`hide_hsi_change`, `hsi_hidden_marks`, `hsi_change_hidden`); `test/turnos-hsi-extension.test.mjs`, `test/test_cola_dia.py`, `test/e2e/hsi-extension-changes.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-05 · booking_requests.py: line coverage from 82.0 % to about 94 % with tests only, and which number «coverage» means
Decided: The coverage of `modules/turnos/booking_requests.py` is quoted as coverage.py's statements («lines» in `scripts/coverage_floor.py`: covered statements / statements); the branch share and coverage.py's own `percent_covered` (statements and branches together) are named as such when quoted. On `main` 4e8dc090 (full CI, `coverage-files.json` of the backend surface) the file had 1594/1943 lines = 82.04 %, 579/692 branches = 83.67 %, combined 82.47 % — the «82.0 / 82.4» of earlier reports were the first and the third. A new file, `test/test_booking_requests_paths.py`, pins the paths the journey suites leave out, through each function's boundary with the store, catalog, EMR and clock replaced by recorders (no database): the EMR push and its answers, the owner's settings (theme, sign-in mode, anti-bot gate), the referral retention sweep and pending analyses, the referral analysis before the request exists (400/403/413/500, AI or cache down), approval (400 on date/service/overbooking flag, 409 on a lost race or overbooking), assignment, rejection, desk and patient cancellation, attendance, EMR retry, the patient's reschedule (400/401/404/409, hold failing closed, linked and unlinked moves) and the waitlist opt-in/out. Measured as the CI baseline plus this file: 1826/1943 lines = 93.98 %, 649/692 branches = 93.79 %. No behaviour changed. · Why: audit 2026-10-27 top-4 asked for ≥ 90 % before the file is cut by action; a separate file keeps the work off the suites and functions that are being edited (`_admin_row`, `_public_row`, `_step_persist`, `_staff_row_or_error`, the referral sniffing) · Rejected: adding cases to `test_booking_requests.py` (3 087 lines, edited by the feature work); pinning the row serializers `_admin_row`/`_public_row` (they change with the panel) · Undo: delete the test file · By: agent.
Source: `test/test_booking_requests_paths.py`.

## 2026-11-05 · The backend job hands over the refreshed shard seed and a guard caps the files without seconds; two `_norm` bodies become shared helpers
Decided: (1) `scripts/run_backend_shards.py --seed-out` writes the seed a run would give (the old seed with the run's seconds folded in, files that no longer exist dropped; `--update-seed` keeps its old behaviour); the `backend` job and the local ladder (`scripts/ci_local.sh`, next to `backend-timings.json` in the run's artifacts) keep it as `backend-timings-seed.json`, to be copied over `scripts/backend_test_timings.json`, so the seed is no longer refreshed by hand from several artifacts. `test_run_backend_shards` fails when more than `MAX_UNSEEDED_SHARE` (5 %) of the test files have no seconds in the seed (it was 11 %: 60 of 556, weighed by size); the runner also prints a line when the run it just finished shows the same. The seed was re-seeded from the median of three full runs (790fa020, 73e0eb5c, p5r4): the slowest of the 5 lanes on each of them, as the planner would split it, fell from 685/608/540 s to 603/521/474 s (even split 592/516/466 s), so no lane order got slower. (2) Of the seven `_norm` bodies, two were byte-equal to a shared function and went away: `chat_tools._norm` is the new `text_fold.ascii_words` (its 33 call sites call it directly, no alias) and `intake_catalog._norm` is `text_fold.plain(x).strip()`. The proof is `test_text_fold` (the old line against the new one over the code-point sweep and over None, 0, False, 5) and the golden of `test_text_folders_contract`, whose `chat_tools._norm` entry became `core.text_fold.ascii_words` unchanged. The other five stay, each for a reason the golden already pins: `hsi_client._norm` keeps `str(0)` = "0" and lowercases after the fold (`ϒ` differs from `plain`); `msd_data._norm` drops the parentheses between the lowercase and the whitespace collapse (`⑴` becomes "" there, "(1)" in `ascii_words`); `doctor_appointments._norm` folds with NFD and lowercases after, keeping `ª` where NFKD gives `a`; `remedies._norm` strips category `Mn` instead of combining classes and keeps punctuation; `confirmations._norm` turns every non-ASCII-alphanumeric into a space, so `µ` vanishes where `fold_words` keeps `μ`. Merging any of them would move a match.
Source: `scripts/run_backend_shards.py`, `scripts/backend_test_timings.json`, `.gitlab-ci.yml`, `scripts/ci_local.sh`, `test/test_run_backend_shards.py`, `core/text_fold.py`, `modules/chat/chat_tools.py`, `modules/intake/intake_catalog.py`, `test/test_text_fold.py`, `test/test_text_folders_contract.py`, `test/golden/text_folders.json`

The assertions, the edge and the thresholds are unchanged. · Why: the flaky line 266 reported by the product owner's hub, 2026-11-05 («wait for a stable box via expect.poll on boundingBox, no sleeps») · Rejected: `waitForTimeout`/sleeps (ratchet, and a guess); a shared helper in `test/helpers/` (no other spec needs it yet; `wait.mjs` is Playwright-free) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-06 · booking_requests.py is cut by action into eight modules, with direct imports and no facade
Decided: The 3 794 lines of `modules/turnos/booking_requests.py` (125 top-level functions and classes) are cut by what the code does: `booking_create` (POST /api/requests: `create_request` and its steps), `booking_referrals` (the referral file: analysis before registration, pending tokens, serving it, the Confirmá preview, retention), `booking_queue` (the desk's queue and REFEPS), `booking_decisions` (approve, assign, reject, fields, patient data, desk cancel, attendance; `_persist_approval` with `OverbookingPolicyDenied`), `booking_patient` (the patient's list, detail, cancel, reschedule, waitlist), `booking_emr` (the HSI push and the panel's «HSI» cores), `booking_capacity` (is the doctor free, is there room) and `panel_settings` (theme, sign-in mode, Turnstile — never a booking). `booking_requests.py` keeps only what two or more of them, or outside callers, use (catalog and doctor pool, session user, public/admin row shapes, ART time, staff access, the interview CTA, `REFERRALS_DIR`/`SERVICES_PATH`/`MAX_REFERRAL_BYTES`): 754 lines, 33 definitions. Rules, pinned by the new `test/test_booking_cut.py`: (1) `booking_requests.py` imports none of the split modules and re-exports nothing; every caller (router, admin panel, chat, accounts, worker, scripts, tests) imports the module that owns the name. (2) Nobody binds a name of these modules at import time; split modules call shared helpers as `bkr.<name>` and each other as `<module>.<name>` at call time, so one patch on the owning module reaches every caller. (3) Each name lives in one module, and every test patch (`patch.object`, patch tables, `setattr`, assignment, `patch("modules.turnos...")`) names the module that defines it — 2 057 patch records of the 158 test files that touch the module were traced per test on `main` and on the branch: each patch that was used on `main` is used after the cut, each one unused there is unused here. The persistence, EMR-boundary and intake-boundary guards that read only `booking_requests.py` now read the eight modules too. Commits go top-down (create/referrals → queue/decisions → patient → emr/capacity/settings), the reverse of the 2026-11-05 plan's order, so that no commit ever has `booking_requests.py` reaching into a split module; the plan's grouping and its reason (the product owner's open work) still hold — none of his open branches touches the file. No behaviour changed: answers, codes and texts are the same code moved. · Why: audit 2026-10-27 top-4 (cut the 3.8k-line core of booking by action after its coverage reached ~94 %, decision of 2026-11-05); the owner's rule of 2026-10-30 that a module cut ends with direct imports and the facade deleted · Rejected: the plan's `booking_requests.py` as a facade re-exporting every moved name (two names for one function, and a test patching the old name silently stops reaching the caller); `booking_settings.py` or `admin_panel/settings_admin.py` for the three Settings switches (not a booking; the turnos router serves them, admin_panel would invert the dependency) · Cost: 62 test files and 20 product files outside the cut changed their imports (or a comment that named the old place) · Undo: revert the series · By: agent.
Source: `modules/turnos/booking_requests.py`, `booking_create.py`, `booking_referrals.py`, `booking_queue.py`, `booking_decisions.py`, `booking_patient.py`, `booking_capacity.py`, `booking_emr.py`, `panel_settings.py`, `booking_requests_router.py`; `test/test_booking_cut.py`, `test/test_booking_requests_store_contract.py`, `test/test_emr_sync_boundary.py`, `test/test_emr_payload_boundary.py`, `test/test_hsi_connector.py`, `test/test_intake_hook_boundary.py`.

## 2026-11-06 · test_servicio_info runs on a database of its own
Decided: `test/test_servicio_info.py` renders the service pages and the wizard, which read the catalog overlay and the free slots from Postgres, but it had no database of its own: run alone (`backend-focused`, the serial `backend-full`) it talked to whatever `DATABASE_URL` named, the shared development database on 5432. That database carries the Alembic revision `0035_booking_requested_at`, the number the booking_requests.requested_at revision had on its branch before the 2026-10-31 rebase renumbered it to `0037_booking_requested_at` (commit 8c03f260); the pre-rebase branch's tests stamped the shared database and no checkout knows that id any more. With an unknown revision every `db._connect()` fails the schema bootstrap ("Can't locate revision") and, because a failed bootstrap is not remembered, the next connection takes the migration lock and runs Alembic again (0.1 to 2.6 s each): the file took over 55 minutes instead of seconds, on a clean `main` too. The sharded local-ci was not affected (each file gets a lane database there). The file now owns a throwaway database at head (`_dbtest.PerModuleDb("sana_turnos_test_servicio_info")`, on the test lane), and `TestBaseDelModulo` pins that the module reads its own database. Alone it runs 46 tests in about 30 s under load. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-06, an executor's focused run hung on this file · Rejected: rolling the shared database back by hand (it moves with branches; the next branch run would stamp it again); stamping it at 0037 (same reason, and it hides the dependency instead of removing it) · Cost: one database create and drop per run of the file (about 10 s) · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent
Source: `test/test_servicio_info.py` (`setUpModule`, `TestBaseDelModulo`).

## 2026-11-05 · Russian and Ukrainian count with «few» and «many»
Decided: The number-agreement rule of the same day gains the two CLDR categories Russian and Ukrainian need (2, 3, 4, 22… take one word, 0, 5–20, 25… another; the panel printed the 5–20 word after 2). A catalog key `k` keeps the plural and `k_one` the singular; `k_few` and `k_many` exist only in `locales/ru/` and `locales/uk/`, and `tn()` (`static/platform/i18n.mjs`) and `tn_or()` (`core/i18n.py`) read them when the count falls in that category and the catalog has them, `k` otherwise (the Spanish/French «many» of a million finds none). `core/i18n.py` `plural_category()` now answers few/many for ru/uk (integers; a fraction is «other»), the other languages still one/other. `scripts/translate_locales.py` derives `k_few`/`k_many` for ru/uk from every counted pair of the shared catalog — the plural carries a placeholder, or both forms are a bare unit word of at most two words («médicos», «turnos») — translated from the Spanish plural with the form named in the prompt, hashed with that form so a changed hint retranslates; a form the Spanish catalog already names is never derived over. 76 pairs per language. The older `X_one`/`X_many` convention (32 pairs, each site an `n === 1 ?` ternary, so it printed the wrong Russian word for 2–4 and collided with the CLDR `_many`) is migrated: `X_many` became `X` in all ten catalogs and every site calls `Tn`/`tn_or` (admin-app, admin-content, admin-queue, admin-solicitudes, admin-my-appointments, admin-patients, admin-prompts, admin-hsi, admin-create-appointment, attendance-forecast, dates, directory, the home, `admin_content.py`, `lab_analysis.py`); three dead pairs (`ver_agendas`, `vencidos`, `appointments_count`) are deleted. Two singulars gained `{n}` so Russian 21 reads its own number («Se conserva {n} turno ya registrado.», «Tiene {n} turno por venir»). `setCatalog(dict, language)` takes the catalog's language for the test harnesses. The module catalogs (es/en only) fall back to Spanish and get no derived forms. Supersedes «few/many fold into the plural key» and the rejection of Russian few/many keys in the entry above. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-05 — the patient site ships in ten languages, so the demo printed the 5–20 word of «files» after 2 · Rejected: hand-written few/many in es/en (Spanish has no such words); a per-language ternary at each site (the bug class the shared rule removed) · Cost: 152 keys in ru and uk each · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `core/i18n.py`, `static/platform/i18n.mjs`, `scripts/translate_locales.py`, `locales/`, `static/platform/attendance-forecast.mjs`, `static/platform/dates.mjs`, `static/platform/directory.mjs`, `templates/index.html`, `admin_panel/admin_content.py`, `ai/lab_analysis.py`; `test/i18n-plural.test.mjs`, `test/test_i18n_plural.py`, `test/fixtures/plural-one.json`.

## 2026-11-06 · e2e photo tests of the catalog audit name their record uniquely
Decided: the two photo tests of `test/e2e/admin-forms-audit-catalog.spec.mjs` (the new doctor's portrait with «Restaurar original», «Volver a procesar» and Quitar; the new center's photo) add a random 8-character tag to the record's name (`uniqueName`), so the id the server derives from it (`catalog._slug`) is not shared by two copies of the test. Each Playwright worker has its own database, but the portraits live in the checkout's `data/medico-fotos/` and `data/centro-fotos/`, shared by every worker; every copy of the doctor test created «Auditoría Retrato» = `auditoria-retrato` (`-2`, `-3`… on later copies in the same worker), so copies running at once in different workers wrote, restored and deleted the same files: one read the other's «Procesada» after its own «Restaurar original», an empty status, or a 404 «sin foto» after the other's cleanup. Portrait processing is synchronous (`doctor_photos._save_portrait` publishes image, status and original under one lock with one atomic pointer before the answer), so the product has no race here: in one installation there is one database and the derived id is unique in it. Measured with `--repeat-each=N --workers=4 --retries=0`, the test alone: the doctor test on main c183d4fb 9 red of 20 (4 «Procesada» expected and empty, 2 «Original» expected, one avatar without the photo, one 404 «sin foto», one `has_upload` never true), with this change 0 of 50; the center test on main 2 of 20 (no photo on the reopened center), with this change 0 of 20. · Why: the product owner's hub, 2026-11-06 («fix the cause, not a bigger retry; separate commit») · Rejected: more retries or a longer timeout (the files are still shared); a per-worker data folder in the harness (`data/` is a fixed path in the product, `doctor_photos.PHOTOS_DIR` and others; a configurable root is a product change for a test-only need); `Date.now()` as the tag, like other specs (the copies start in the same millisecond) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `test/e2e/admin-forms-audit-catalog.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-06 · stableBox lives in test/helpers/box.mjs, shared by the e2e specs and the node:test files
Decided: the core of `stableBox(locator, what)` (poll until two reads in a row are the same non-null `boundingBox()`, up to 15 s, a read every 50 ms, no `waitForTimeout`) moves from `test/e2e/lib/fixtures.mjs` to `test/helpers/box.mjs`, built on `until` from `wait.mjs` so it needs no Playwright Test `expect`. the 17 e2e specs that used the 2026-11-05 helper now import it from there directly and `fixtures.mjs` no longer exports it (a re-export would be a facade, and the rule is direct imports); the three node:test files that read a box right after a click or a `goto` (`account-coverage-browser`, `chat-viewport-browser`, `admin-create-appointment-native`) call the same function for all 9 of their `boundingBox()` reads.
