## 2026-11-05 · The sharded backend runner stops at the first file that dies, with its signal or exit code, a deadline and a heartbeat
Decided: `scripts/run_backend_shards.py` stops every lane as soon as a file ends without its result report (a signal, an exit code, the 1500 s per-file timeout) or a lane raises, prints the cause and the last 20 lines of that file at once, reports the cut-short files as «stopped while running» and the rest as «never ran», and fails with `FAILED (run stopped: <reason>)`. The whole run has a deadline (`TURNOS_BACKEND_DEADLINE`, 5400 s) and a heartbeat (`TURNOS_BACKEND_HEARTBEAT`, 120 s); `--progress-fd 3` in `ci_local.sh` sends the announcements, heartbeats and verdict to the local-ci output while the full log still goes to `backend.log`. A signal prints the stop line immediately, stdout is line-buffered, the report precedes the database drops, waits on killed files are bounded and DROP/CREATE DATABASE carry a statement timeout. Detail in [backend-tests-run-in-parallel-shards.md](../solutions/dev/backend-tests-run-in-parallel-shards.md). · Why: three local-ci runs on 05.11 sat silent on the backend banner for an hour and ended with no runner and no exit code; they were stopped by the 2-hour limit on a background command under load 100-250, and the runner left nothing in its file-redirected, block-buffered log (pinamar-turnos) · Rejected: only raising the per-file timeout or the outer limit (the silence stays); letting the other lanes finish after a death to collect more failures (a dead interpreter usually means the machine or the database, and the run would stay silent up to an hour more) · Cost: one dead file hides the results of the files that had not run yet · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-06 · A doctor or center deleted for good takes its photos along
Decided: when `admin_content.delete_item` deletes a doctor or a center for good (not in the pack, no appointment, nothing else names it), `doctor_photos.erase` removes its files before the row goes: for a doctor the public `data/medico-fotos/<id>.<ext>` pointer and the whole private `data/medico-fotos/.originals/<id>/` (originals, bundles, metadata, lock), under the same `_portrait_lock` that `_save_portrait` publishes under, so an upload already running finishes first and its bundle goes too; for a center `data/centro-fotos/<slug>.<ext>`. The id must pass the uploads' own pattern with `fullmatch` (no `.`, `/` or trailing newline) and the private folder must resolve directly inside `.originals`, otherwise nothing is removed; files that are already missing are not an error; a removal that fails answers 507 «No se pudo quitar la foto.» and the record stays, so the desk can try again. A disabled record (appointments, any row naming the doctor, a pack entry) keeps its photos, so «Habilitar» brings them back. Found by the e2e-photo-flake work (2026-11-06): ids are derived from names, so a new doctor with the name of a deleted one showed the deleted person's face at once, and the private original of someone no longer in the catalog stayed on disk (Ley 25.326). `core/personal_data` and `core/subject_erase` stay as they are: their subjects are people found by DNI, and staff and doctors as data subjects are an open question (Q8); a doctor's photo on request is the photo's «Quitar» or this deletion. `scripts/reapply_erasures.py` does not carry the deletion over a restore: one archive restores the database and the volume together, so the doctor and its photo come back together and the deletion is repeated in the panel. Photos orphaned before this change are not swept. In the same change both tests of `test/e2e/center-photo-create.spec.mjs` stop sharing a file in `data/centro-fotos/` between copies: the first names its new center with a random tag, the second creates its own center through the API with a tagged slug before opening its editor, instead of the pack's `riverside-clinic` (measured with `--repeat-each=10 --workers=4 --retries=0`: the second test on `riverside-clinic` 5 red of 10, the tagged first test 0 of 10). · Why: the product owner's hub, 2026-11-06 («on hard delete of a doctor remove data/medico-fotos/ID.webp and .originals/ID/, failing unit test first; same for center photos if delete_item has the same gap») · Rejected: removing the files after the row (a failed removal would leave the photo of a record that no longer exists, with nothing in the panel to retry from); a periodic orphan sweep instead (the photo would show on a same-name record until it runs); journaling the removed paths in `purged_files` (the id carries the doctor's name, which that table must not hold) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `admin_panel/admin_content.py`, `patient_web/doctor_photos.py`, `test/test_doctor_delete_references.py`, `test/e2e/center-photo-create.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-06 · One-off sweep of photos left by doctors and centers deleted before
Decided: `scripts/fixes/purge_orphan_photos.py` removes the photos that doctors and centers deleted for good before 2026-11-06 left in the volume (`data/medico-fotos/<id>.<ext>`, `data/medico-fotos/.originals/<id>/`, `data/centro-fotos/<slug>.<ext>`). Dry run by default (prints each orphan, removes nothing); `--apply` removes and prints each removal; the last line is a JSON report. An orphan is a photo whose id is neither in `catalog.known_ids` (pack and overlay, disabled and incomplete records included) nor in any overlay row of its type. Names outside the uploads' id pattern, non-photo files and anything outside the two folders are ignored and counted. Removal goes through `doctor_photos.erase`, the function the deletion uses (same id and path checks, the same portrait lock), which gains `unless`: the catalog is read again right before the files go, for a doctor under the lock, so a record created between the listing and the removal keeps its photo. A catalog that lists no doctor or no center is refused (exit 2), because every photo would look like an orphan. How to run it: `docs/operations/orphan-photos.md`. We do not run it on any installation: the test stands are run by the product owner's side, salud.pinamar.gob.ar only through a release package. · Why: the product owner's hub, 2026-11-06 («dry run by default, --apply only removes files whose id is in no catalog row, active or disabled, and prints what it removed») · Rejected: a periodic sweep in the worker (deletion already removes photos; this backlog is one-off) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit; files already removed come back only from a backup · By: agent.
Source: `scripts/fixes/purge_orphan_photos.py`, `patient_web/doctor_photos.py`, `test/test_purge_orphan_photos.py`, `docs/operations/orphan-photos.md`.

## 2026-11-06 · Russian and Ukrainian 21, 31… take the singular with its own number; no «¿» or «¡» in a generated catalog
Decided: CLDR «one» in Russian and Ukrainian is also 21, 31, 101…, and `tn()` used a `_one` text without `{n}` only for exactly 1, so the demo printed the plural after 21 («21 doctors», 26 keys per language). (1) Every Spanish `k_one` that wrote a literal «1» now writes `{n}` (15 keys: `date.hours_ago_one`, `date.minutes_ago_one`, the doctor import counts, `admin.hsi.rescan_resolved_one`, `admin.srv.doctor.upcoming_one`, `admin.srv.reports.hero.capacity_critico_one`, `admin.queue.forecast.based_on_one`, `admin.settings.holidays.appointments_one`, `admin.content.schedule.reset_title_one`, `admin.content.prompts.changes_count_one`, `account.mine.lab.fallback_review_one`), the code fallbacks with them; Spanish output is unchanged and the eight generated languages were regenerated. (2) A `_one` that is a bare unit word (at most two words, no placeholder, no sentence punctuation: «médico», «turno», printed after the caller's number) serves every «one» count — `singularFits()` in `static/platform/i18n.mjs`, `_singular_fits()` in `core/i18n.py`; a whole phrase with no number in it («¿Aprobar la solicitud…?») still serves only 1. (3) The four sites that still chose with `n === 1 ?` go through `Tn`/`tn_or` (holidays count in Ajustes, schedule reset dialog, attendance forecast line, the capacity hero of Reportes). (4) `scripts/translate_locales.py` removes «¿» and «¡» from every value written to a non-Spanish catalog, the kept ones too, and now rewrites the catalogs even when nothing is pending; the model had kept them in two Russian questions of Solicitudes and one French one. Tests: `test/i18n-plural.test.mjs` reads every counted pair through the real ru/uk catalogs for 1, 2, 5, 21, 22, 25; `test/test_i18n_plural.py` pins the bare-word rule, the real catalog forms and that no Spanish `_one` carries a literal 1; `test/test_translate_locales_marks.py` pins the cleaning and that no generated catalog carries the marks. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-06, findings of the few/many slice (de848ca7) · Rejected: hand-editing the generated catalogs (they are regenerated); a per-key list of fragments (the two-word rule is the one `_counted()` already uses to derive few/many) · Cost: 15 keys retranslated in eight languages · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `static/platform/i18n.mjs`, `core/i18n.py`, `scripts/translate_locales.py`, `locales/`, `static/platform/admin-settings.mjs`, `static/platform/admin-content.mjs`, `static/platform/attendance-forecast.mjs`, `static/platform/dates.mjs`, `static/platform/admin-hsi.mjs`, `static/platform/admin-prompts.mjs`, `admin_panel/reports.py`, `admin_panel/admin_content.py`, `ai/lab_analysis.py`; `test/i18n-plural.test.mjs`, `test/test_i18n_plural.py`, `test/test_translate_locales_marks.py`.

## 2026-11-05 · The pre-Alembic bridge (`migrations/`) is removed; a database without `alembic_version` that carries pre-Alembic tables is refused at boot and converted by one boot of the release tagged `bridge-last`
Decided: (1) `migrations/` (the frozen numbered runner 001-005 and its shape classifier, repair and convergence check, 2645 lines) is deleted. `db_schema._ensure_schema` now takes the advisory lock, runs `_refuse_pre_alembic` and then `upgrade head`; there is no stamp path and no bridge path any more. (2) `_refuse_pre_alembic`: when `alembic_version` has no row and any of the 21 pre-Alembic table names (`schema_migrations`, `booking_requests` and the Spanish-era names the old migration 001 renamed, `PRE_ALEMBIC_TABLES`) exists, the boot raises before any DDL, names the tables and points at the release tag `bridge-last` and docs/UPGRADING.md ("A database from before Alembic"); the lock is released as on any failed boot. A fresh database and an Alembic database boot as before. (3) Revision 0007 no longer imports `migrations`: it builds its table and its two indexes again under probe names inside a savepoint, compares what the catalog renders for both (columns, constraints, index definitions; `lab_report_analysis.fecha` may already be a date, as the frozen baseline has it after 0030) and the `operators.role` default, and rolls the probes back; a same-name wrong index still fails the upgrade with the same message. (4) `test/test_alembic_cutover.py` (4449 lines, 128 tests) becomes `test/test_alembic_revisions.py` (36 tests): the baseline, snapshot and forward-revision tests stay, the 0006/0007/0008 tests move to `TestBootPath` with the refusal tests, and the 93 bridge tests go; the bridge parts of `test_db_ensure_schema_steps`, `test_translate`, `test_slice8_verification_fixes`, `test_integrity_races` (the 005 backfill through the bridge), `test_backup_inventory`, `test_data_map` and `test_raw_sql_ratchet` go with it. (5) `schema_migrations` stays where it is on the installations that have it: a fossil, ignored once `alembic_version` has its row. (6) Rehearsal on a scratch PostgreSQL 16 (synthetic data, 5 requests and 3 accounts): a partial Spanish-era database and a complete pre-Alembic one, both built with the bridge-carrying code, are refused by this release with no table created; one boot of `4e8dc090` converts each to `0037` with every row kept and the schema equal to `alembic/schema-head.txt`; this release then boots them unchanged. · Why: hospital TASK-62 (2026-11-05): pinamar.humanrounds.org and the demo answer `/health` with build `4e8dc09` and `/ready` 200, which holds only at Alembic head; salud.pinamar.gob.ar runs `a97f8118` and serves patients since 2026-10-30; the municipality confirmed (2026-11-06, through the product owner's hub) that its `alembic_version` is 0035 and that its oldest backup, of 2026-10-19 (about 0021-0022), is restored physically, so every database it can bring back is already under Alembic. For every known database the bridge was dead code that every fresh install still imported (0007) and every boot classified through; only an old dump of a never-upgraded database still needs it, and the refusal plus the tag keeps that path open without keeping the code. · Rejected: deleting the bridge without a refusal (a partial dump would get empty English tables next to the Spanish ones holding the data); keeping the bridge; dropping `schema_migrations` in a new revision (a write to production schemas for tidiness) · Cost: an old pre-Alembic dump needs two boots (the `bridge-last` image, then the current one) instead of one · Undo: revert the commit; no table, column or revision is added or removed · By: the product owner's hub (2026-11-06: the Alembic revisions are not squashed, every file 0001-0037 keeps its revision and down_revision; only the bridge goes; a pre-Alembic database is refused with a clear message), hospital (A1/B1/V1: prepare now, merge after salud's revision is confirmed, refusal plus tag, keep `schema_migrations`), agent (the refusal's table list, the 0007 probe, the test split).
Source: `core/db_schema.py`, `alembic/versions/0007_existing_install_shape.py`, `alembic/versions/0001_baseline.py`, `docs/UPGRADING.md`, `docs/architecture/deploy.md`, `CLAUDE.md`; `test/test_alembic_revisions.py`, `test/test_db_ensure_schema_steps.py`.

## 2026-11-05 · Pacientes re-checked with Opus: 17 findings on the list and the ficha, all fixed
Decided: An Opus pass over the Pacientes screen Sonnet built (waves 1-3 up to 790fa020), measured on the demo at 390, 768, 1024 and 1440 and over 50 patients, found 17 defects and fixes them all, keeping every decision the product owner accepted on 04.11 (one left and right edge, the «turnos N» pill, WhatsApp, the copyable DNI, the doctors line with «+N» only for the rest, three bars after the name, the turno's first line, past turnos with the date only, the empty actions line on the phone). List: (L1) one 44px height for the search, the filters and the column gear (they were 48/36/44 in one row); (L1b) at 768 the two filters share the search's line instead of dropping below it; (L2) on the phone the service and the doctors share line 3 (cards were 73, 92, 93 and 113 px tall in one list; the service is cut with an ellipsis past 60%); (L3) a patient without attendance history draws no bars (three empty grey bars on 57 of 109 rows, and in the ficha head); (L4) the columns are measured at the table's natural width (`medirClase: "pat-measuring"`), so «Paciente» is as wide as its longest name (344 px for 221 of name before) and the rest goes to «Médico»; (L5) the service does not wrap on the computer (a two-line «Ginecología y Obstetricia» made rows 63 px against 43); (L6) the last column ends on the right edge of «Nuevo paciente» and the gear (12 px inside before; its resize grip is hidden, the table's edge is no boundary); (L7) the «turnos» pill is 14px, the screen's only 13px text; (L8) the doctors cell re-fits its «+N» when the engine re-sizes the columns or the web font arrives (it kept an ellipsis); (L9) «Paciente» is not elastic, so at 768-1024 no name is cut (10-27 were); (L10) on a tablet the filters keep the width of what they say beside the search, the gear stays on the first line, and three filters that do not fit go together on the second line (a lone filter stretched 602-760 px and the gear fell to a third line). Ficha: (F1) on the phone the name has the whole first line and «Editar datos» goes to the end of the DNI line (31 of 50 names broke in two beside it); (F2) the DNI is the bare number, as in the list (no «DNI» label); (F3) what the desk can act on comes first — a pending request, then upcoming turnos nearest first, then the rest newest first (`admin_patients._orden_ficha`; a December turno came before tomorrow's and pending requests sat among old visits); (F4) the DNI stands 16 px from the contact icons like the rest of the line (28-40 px before: the icons' empty touch area); (F5) at 1440 a turno's buttons stand right after its text, one column for every row (600-850 px away before), the second line cut with an ellipsis and whole in its title; (F6) the ficha's row buttons end on the same right edge as «Editar datos». The column «Servicio» is titled «Servicio más usado» (the product owner, 05.11 screencast: it is the service the patient uses most, not «a» service). Kept from 790fa020 because they were right: everything the product owner accepted on 04.11. Out of scope and reported to the screen's owner: the same toolbar height mismatch in the shared `admin-direct.css` on other lists. · Why: Product owner 2026-11-03/05 (m1682368, m1583745): re-prototype and re-check the screens with Opus and say how many defects were found and what share was fixed · Rejected: editing the shared `admin-direct.css` / `admin-table.mjs` (every list would move; the fixes stay inside `#sec-pacientes`); keeping «Paciente» elastic with a smaller weight (still cut names at 768-1024) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-05 · The ficha undoes a desk mistake: «No vino» back to «Atendió», a cancelled turno restored or rescheduled
Decided (the product owner, 05.11: if the patient did not come, a button to change it in case it was pressed by accident; if it was cancelled, reschedule it or bring it back): in the patient's ficha, (1) a turno marked «No vino» shows «Atendió», which posts the same `/api/admin/requests/{id}/attendance` the day queue uses, with the same permission; the new mark replaces the old one and both stay in the audit log. A mark cannot be removed, only changed: the database's CHECK allows `presente` or `ausente`, and «present» is the only other truth. (2) A cancelled turno shows «Reprogramar»: the desk's own «Nuevo turno» modal (`admin-create-appointment.mjs`) opens with this patient, the cancelled turno's service and doctor already chosen, and only the time left to pick; after it is created the ficha redraws. (3) It also shows «Restaurar» when the server says the turno can come back exactly as it was (`restaurable` in `patient_turnos`): it was an approved appointment, its time is still ahead, no other appointment of that doctor (or, with no doctor, of that service and time: capacity 1) takes it, no web patient holds it and the waitlist was not offered it; it was not an overbooking (that spends a quota on top of another turno, so «Reprogramar» books it through those checks); and its service still gives turnos (a service now walk-in, or a same-day listing on another day, is not restored). The desk holds the minute (`slot_holds.take_for_restore`, the patient's own atomic hold) from its last check until the write, as «Crear turno» does, so a patient cannot pick it in between. The new `POST /api/admin/requests/{id}/restore` is an ordinary desk approval (`booking_decisions._desk_restore_core` → `_persist_approval(..., restaurar=True)` → `db_booking.approve`): the same doctor checks, a compare-and-set on `cancelada`, the audit event `restaurada`, the cancellation and confirmation fields cleared, and the approval email. No booking limit applies (limits bind only the patient's own POST, never the desk). A turno linked to HSI while HSI sync is on is not restored (HSI already freed it): the desk reschedules it. Refusals: `no_restaurable`, `turno_pasado`, `restaurar_con_hsi`, `turno_tomado`, `turno_medico_tomado` and the doctor's out-of-agenda code; on a 409 the ficha says «Ese turno ya no se puede restaurar. Reprogramalo.» and reloads, so «Restaurar» leaves that row. The buttons are quiet, in the turno's actions line (on the phone the same line, one card height). Tests: `test/test_desk_restore.py` (failing first), `test/admin-patients-lifecycle.test.mjs`, `test/admin-create-appointment-native.test.mjs` and the browser journey `test/e2e/admin-patient-turno-fixes.spec.mjs` (marked absent → fixed → present; cancelled → restored, same time and doctor, second email; cancelled with its time given to someone else → no «Restaurar», a direct POST is 409; cancelled → rescheduled with the same doctor). To keep `core/db_booking.py` within its 1500 lines (`test/test_db_domain_modules.py`), the «Ocultar» marks of the HSI extension's panel of changes (`hide_hsi_change`, `hsi_hidden_marks`, `hsi_change_hidden`) moved unchanged to `core/db_hsi.py`. · Why: Product owner 2026-11-05 (voice note to the hub) · Rejected: an «undo» that blanks the attendance mark (the database refuses an empty mark, and «no mark» on a past turno is the state the queue asks the desk to resolve); restoring through the patient's booking path (it would apply the patient's booking limit to the desk); restoring a turno whose slot is taken by double-booking it (a silent second patient at the same time) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `modules/turnos/booking_decisions.py`, `modules/turnos/booking_requests_router.py`, `modules/turnos/booking_requests_store.py`, `modules/turnos/slot_holds.py`, `core/db_booking.py`, `core/db_hsi.py`, `modules/turnos/doctor_appointments.py`, `docs/architecture/hsi.md`, `admin_panel/admin_patients.py`, `static/platform/admin-patients.mjs`, `static/platform/admin-create-appointment.mjs`, `locales/es/admin.queue.json`, `locales/en/admin.queue.json`; `test/test_desk_restore.py`, `test/test_booking_requests_store_contract.py`, `test/admin-patients-lifecycle.test.mjs`, `test/admin-create-appointment-native.test.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-patient-turno-fixes.spec.mjs`, `test/snapshots.json`, `test/tier4_composition_inventory.json`.

## 2026-11-05 · The centers map: every name nearer its own dot, one dot only for one address, the building's services under the map on a phone; one section heading; the stuck search fades the page; the chat button steps aside
Decided: (1) On the home's map every name — each line's own box, not the whole block — keeps at least 10 px from every other dot and at least 8 px more than its place's nearest name keeps from its own dot; a placement that breaks it is no placement. (2) A leader line is at most 36 px on a phone and 60 px above (the 92 and 132 px steps are gone). (3) Places share a dot only where their dots would overlap on screen (16 px, was 26), and a closer zoom that keeps them apart wins over sharing: two addresses 300 m apart (CSI and Coordinación de Discapacidad in Ostende) always get two dots, two 40 m apart (CAPS Ostende and CAPS DiabetPin) share one at any zoom. (4) A dot is the link to a route only when all its names lead to that one route (one place, or centers at one address); a dot two addresses share is a plain mark and its names stay the links. (5) A building with services (the Hospital with Hemoterapia and Oncología, Hospital de Día) whose block fits nowhere beside its dot is named on the map by its center alone, and the whole building — center first, then its services, with the route button — goes into the row under the map (on the phone; on the desktop the block fits under the dot). A place whose names fit nowhere by these rules (three places in a row closer than a name is wide, the town already filling the frame) leaves the map, dot and all, for that row, and the others are laid out again without its dot; the old fallback that put such a name tight against its dot whatever the neighbours is gone. Order of cost: a short line < a closer zoom < the center alone < a shared dot < the place in the row under the map. (6) The home's section headings — «Centros de salud», the directory groups, «Guías y trámites» — read one token, `--section-h-size` 18 px and `--section-h-weight` 650 (`tokens.css`; the first two were 13.5 px 600); «Centros de salud» is an `h2`. (7) Once the search sticks to the top, the page behind its box fades into the page's own background over the 20 px under it (`.searchbar--stuck::before`, set by an IntersectionObserver): a line passing under the box is no longer cut in half by its edge; at rest nothing is painted, the strip stays transparent. (8) The floating «Chateá con IA» steps aside (opacity 0, no pointer) while a one-line control at the foot of a page — «Todas las especialidades» or a footer link — crosses its height, and is back once nothing passes beside it. · Why: the product owner's hub 2026-11-05 (pinamar-turnos): on the phone a name sat 11 px from the neighbour's dot and another beside the hospital's, the hospital's line was 92 px, CSI and Coordinación are two addresses, the heading at 13.5 px was lost, at 1440 a sliced line under the search, at 390 the chat button stood in one row with «Todas las especialidades» · Rejected: a name placed anyway beside a dot it cannot own (a second reading of the map is worse than one row more); a dot without its name; a line to the hospital's block on the phone (it read as a road); merging places under 26 px (it merged CSI and Coordinación); the shared dot opening the first place's route (half its names lead elsewhere); a solid band behind the search (the product owner 2026-10-25: the box casts a shadow, not a shadow on a white band); stepping aside for every list row (the button would blink all along /centers); moving the button up or left (it would cover other text) · Cost: on a phone the hospital's services are read in the row under the map, not at its dot; in a town crowded like that a place may be found only in that row (Pinamar today: none at 390 or 1440); the chat button vanishes for a moment at the foot of the home · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `static/platform/centers-map.mjs`, `static/platform/chat-widget.mjs`, `static/platform/directory.mjs`, `templates/index.html`, `tokens.css`; `test/centers-map.test.mjs` (the Pinamar town measured at 390 and 1440, names stripped: `test/fixtures/centers-map-town.json`), `test/e2e/tiles-canonical.spec.mjs` (a crowded town on the example map), `test/e2e/public-chrome.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-05 · The Médicos list's statistics resolve each doctor's agenda once, not once per day
Decision: `GET /api/admin/doctors/stats` resolves which services manage a doctor's agenda ONCE per doctor (`doctor_appointments._doctor_agendas`: the windows and exceptions of each managing service, or the doctor's own), and the hours of each day and the capacity of the next 14 days are computed from that one result; the catalog is read once per request. `_attendance_days` and the admin «Pacientes de hoy» free hours reuse the same resolution instead of reading the catalog per day. · Why: the Médicos list (`renderListaShell("medicos")`) waits for its statistics before it paints, and they took 17–27 s on the demo database (about 1 000 reads of the catalog and of the managing services for some 70 doctors × 14 days; 2 s after the change), so the screen stayed empty and the panel's Médicos browser tests timed out on a clean `main` · Rejected: painting the list before the statistics (two layouts of the same rows); a cache of the catalog across requests (an edit in the panel would show late) · Cost: none — same numbers, `test_schedule_policy_slice4` pins one catalog read and one resolution per doctor · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `modules/turnos/doctor_appointments.py`; `test/test_schedule_policy_slice4.py`.

## 2026-11-06 · A turno cancelled with an archived patient is restored only after the patient is

Decided: while a patient is archived, their cancelled turnos offer no «Restaurar» of their own in the ficha, and `POST /api/admin/requests/{id}/restore` refuses them with `paciente_archivado` (`booking_decisions.restore_blocker`, read once per ficha in `admin_patients.patient_turnos`). The ficha of an archived patient therefore carries one «Restaurar», the patient's own (`#pac-arch`, the data form's archive/restore button); the cancelled turnos keep «Reprogramar». Once the patient is restored, a turno that still meets the restore rules of 2026-11-05 shows its «Restaurar» again; restoring the patient never brings turnos back by itself (J18 step 5). A request whose DNI has no account is not treated as archived. The J18 and J14 journeys now press `#pac-arch` instead of the first button labelled «Restaurar»/«Archivar»: the turno button added on 2026-11-05 made J18 press the turno's restore and wait in vain for the patient PUT. · Why: two buttons with the same word on one card, one of which approves a turno for a person who cannot sign in; the patient comes back first · Rejected: renaming the buttons («Restaurar paciente» / «Restaurar turno») and keeping both (the turno one would still approve an appointment for an archived account); restoring the archive's turnos together with the patient (J18 pins that the archive's cancellation stands) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `modules/turnos/booking_decisions.py`, `admin_panel/admin_patients.py`; `test/test_desk_restore.py`, `test/e2e/journey-j18-reject-decline-restore.spec.mjs`, `test/e2e/journey-j14-disable-doctor-archive-patient.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-06 · The routes tier waits for an answer up to a ceiling, and a hang says which request hung
Decided: every HTTP request of the routes tier and of `LiveServerMixin` waits up to `_livehttp.response_ceiling()` (120 s, `TURNOS_TEST_HTTP_CEILING` overrides it) instead of a fixed socket timeout: `_routes_fixture.RouteTestCase._request` and `LiveServerMixin._get` had 5 s, `test_api_docs_gate.py` and `test_validation_contract.py` 5 s and 30 s, `_body_contract.request` 30 s; all go through `_livehttp.call()` now (`_get` keeps http.client, which does not follow redirects, and shares the ceiling). The worker's Postgres connects (`connect_timeout=5`, four places in `_routes_fixture.py`, where a timeout became the skip «Postgres no accesible») and the LiveServer boot wait (30 s) use the same ceiling. A server silent past it raises `_livehttp.NoAnswer`, an AssertionError naming the method and path, the ceiling, the seconds waited and the load average, instead of `TimeoutError: timed out`. No assertion of any test changed: how long an answer takes was never what a routes test checks. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-06, pre-commit red on `test/routes/test_cancel_label.py` at load 100+ by the 5 s timeout alone, the change under commit had nothing to do with it · Rejected: retrying the request (it hides a real hang and repeats side effects of a POST); scaling the timeout by the load average (the load at the start of a request says nothing about its end) · Cost: a server that really hangs fails after 120 s instead of 5 s · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent
Source: `test/_livehttp.py` (`response_ceiling`, `call`, `NoAnswer`, `no_answer_is_a_failure`), `test/_routes_fixture.py`, `test/routes/_body_contract.py`, `test/routes/test_api_docs_gate.py`, `test/routes/test_validation_contract.py`; `test/test_livehttp_ceiling.py`.

## 2026-11-06 · The patient's MCP token is stored only as its SHA-256 hash
Decided: `users.mcp_token` holds the hex SHA-256 of the token, never the token itself, and the new column `users.mcp_token_hint` holds its last 4 characters. The token is still `secrets.token_urlsafe(24)` (192 random bits), so an unsalted fast hash is enough: there is nothing to guess, and the hash only has to make a leaked row useless as a Bearer. `db_accounts.user_by_mcp_token` looks the Bearer up by its hash (same unique index, one indexed equality); `mcp_token_issue(dni)` creates a token only when the account has none, in one atomic UPDATE, and returns the plaintext only then, otherwise only the hint; `mcp_token_regenerate(dni)` rotates and returns the new plaintext once; `mcp_token_of` is gone. `GET /api/account/mcp` answers `{url, token, hint}`: «Mi cuenta» shows the token with a Copiar link only on the answer that created or regenerated it, afterwards `••••` plus the last 4 characters and «Generar uno nuevo». Alembic 0038 adds the column and hashes the existing tokens in place (`encode(sha256(convert_to(...)), 'hex')`, hint `right(mcp_token, 4)`), so an assistant already holding a token keeps working without the patient regenerating it; an empty token becomes NULL; the shape guard `!~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'` makes a rerun touch nothing (precedent: 0033, staff reset tokens). One set-based UPDATE over the rows that hold a token, a few per installation. The same change closes a second leak: `_user_public` returned `mcp_token` in every `/api/account/*` answer and in the panel's patient answers; it now drops the hash and the hint, and `AccountUser` no longer declares the field. `core/personal_data.REGISTRY` lists `mcp_token_hint` as a secret (never exported, blanked on erasure). · Why: finding of 2026-11-06 (pinamar-turnos hub): a database or backup leak handed out working patient MCP tokens, and a working token books and cancels appointments in the patient's name. · Rejected: a salted or slow hash (bcrypt/scrypt: needed for low-entropy passwords, not for 192 random bits, and it would cost a scan per MCP call instead of an index lookup); a new column `mcp_token_hash` next to the old one (two columns for one datum and a drop of the old one later); reissuing every token (every connected assistant would stop working). · Cost: the patient sees the plaintext once; one who did not copy it must press «Generar uno nuevo», and the old one stops working. During a rolling deploy of a test site, for about a minute the old container still answers: it compares the raw Bearer with the hashed column (401) and could show the hash as a token or store a new raw token; a token stored that way does not work and the patient regenerates it. salud.pinamar.gob.ar gets the change in its next release package, which stops the service before migrating. · Undo: a revert of the code leaves hashed tokens that the old code cannot use: every patient regenerates once; the hash cannot be turned back into the token, so 0038 has no downgrade. · By: pinamar-turnos hub (hash only, show once, last 4 characters afterwards, hash in place without reissue), agent (same column, atomic issue, the `_user_public` leak).
Source: `core/db_accounts.py`, `alembic/versions/0038_mcp_token_hash.py`, `core/schema_model.py`, `core/personal_data.py`, `cuentas/api_models.py`, `modules/chat/mcp_api.py`, `static/platform/account.mjs`, `locales/es/account.json`, `locales/en/account.json`; `test/test_db_accounts_store.py`, `test/test_alembic_revisions.py`, `test/test_mcp_api.py`, `test/test_mcp_api_router.py`.

## 2026-11-07 · The browser node-test tier waits up to one ceiling, and a hang says what it waited for
Decided: every wait of the browser lane of the frontend tests (the files that import playwright, run by `scripts/run_frontend_tests.mjs` at concurrency 1) is bounded by one ceiling, `test/helpers/ceiling.mjs`: 120 s per wait by default, `TURNOS_TEST_BROWSER_CEILING` (seconds) overrides it, a test gets three ceilings and a launch hook three ceilings plus 5 s. It replaces the fixed budgets of 21 files: `page.setDefaultTimeout` of 3, 4, 5, 8 and 15 s, test timeouts of 10 to 60 s, locator, request and navigation timeouts of 2.5 to 20 s, and the hand-rolled `Promise.race` timers of 5, 8 and 20 s (`within(promise, what)` now). `helpers/wait.mjs` `until()` defaults to the same ceiling. A hang fails as an AssertionError naming what was awaited, the ceiling, the seconds waited and the load average (`hang`, `within`, and `explained` for a Playwright TimeoutError, which keeps Playwright's own message and locator); the wrapped `test` reports a test that never ends the same way. Where Chromium was launched per test or per page (admin-consult, admin-draft-preservation, admin-editorial-save, admin-users-native, chat-widget-native, turnos-upload-native, demo-banner-links-gap, upload-order-notice-paste) the file launches one Chromium in `before` and each page gets a fresh context (`openPage`). Negative windows (a request that must NOT happen within 1.5 s), `stays` windows and the product's own timers on the page clock keep their values; no assertion changed. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-07, pre-commit red at load 90+ on admin-consult-browser, admin-editorial-save-browser and postbooking-coverage-browser by the 4-10 s budgets alone, green one by one; same cause as the routes tier on 2026-11-06 · Rejected: retries (a retry hides a real hang, and `PW_FAIL_ON_FLAKY` already counts a pass on retry as a failure); scaling the budgets by the load average; raising each literal (21 files of numbers that drift apart again) · Cost: a page that really hangs fails after 120 s instead of 3-15 s · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent
Source: `test/helpers/ceiling.mjs`, `test/helpers/wait.mjs`; `test/browser-ceiling.test.mjs` (with `test/fixtures/ceiling-tier-runner.mjs`).

## 2026-11-07 · «Recorrido del paciente»: how far the requests got, only through steps the database records, with the steps as a setting
Decided: Reportes gets a thirteenth view, «Recorrido del paciente» (`?view=recorrido`, `GET /api/admin/reports/funnel`). The unit is a booking request created in the period, because it is the only record that follows a patient from asking to being seen. Steps, in the default order: pedido (the row), aprobada (status `aprobada` or an `aprobada`/`restaurada` event, so a turno cancelled later still counts as given), confirmada (`confirmation='si'`, off by default: the confirmation question is not sent everywhere), turno_paso (the day arrived and the turno still stood), presente (the desk marked it). A step counts the requests that pass it and every earlier step that is on; the loss between two steps is split by reason (rejected, cancelled, pending, no-show, not marked…), and losses still in course (pending, turno ahead) are said as such, not as losses. Filters: period (7/30/90), center, service, doctor (assigned, else the slot's), kept in the URL. Which steps show and in what order is a setting (`catalog` config/`report_funnel`, no schema change) the admin edits from the report's gear: switch on/off, move up/down; at least one step stays on, an unknown or repeated key is a 400. Steps of the path that have no record are named under the report and never estimated: visits to the site or a service page (no visit log), a booking started and abandoned (expired slot holds are deleted by the sweep), signing up. The payload carries counts only (no DNI, name, contact or request id). No export: the shared export component is not in main yet. Rejected: counting abandoned bookings from holds (the rows are gone), and a step from a side signal (emails sent, page views from Sentry).
Source: `admin_panel/reports_funnel.py`, `admin_panel/reports_router.py`, `core/db_reports.py`, `static/platform/admin-reports.mjs`, `admin-direct.css`, `locales/es/admin.reports.json`, `locales/en/admin.reports.json`, `locales/es/admin.srv.json`, `locales/en/admin.srv.json`, `docs/architecture/reportes.md`; `test/test_reports_funnel.py`, `test/admin-reports-disclosure.test.mjs`, `test/admin-reports-lifecycle.test.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-reports-lifecycle.spec.mjs`, `test/e2e/journey-j27-empty-first-morning.spec.mjs`.

The line hides itself once the extension marks the page (`html[data-hr-humanizador="1"]`), so the server never has to know whether this browser has it; while the line is shown, the click hint no longer repeats the store link. · Why: the desk never found how to reach the extension (hospital technical counterpart, recording of 2026-10-27: nothing in the panel led to it) · Rejected: a separate «Extensión» screen (one more place to find); showing the line only while the extension is unpublished (it is published, and the desk still could not find it). ## 2026-11-07 · One print layer for the panel and the patient site; every sheet says which screen, which filter and when
Decided: printing is one `@media print` block at the end of `human-rounds.css`, the stylesheet both the panel and the patient site load, plus one `@page` rule (A4, 16/12/14 mm). On paper the menu, the navigation bar, the search, the toasts, the chat launcher and every control (buttons, selects, search and file inputs, toolbars, tab lists) are gone; checkboxes and radios keep their grid cell but are invisible; ink is black on white with no underline; a table repeats its head on every sheet (`thead{display:table-header-group}`), never splits a row, lays out `auto` at the sheet's width (`table[class]` outweighs a screen's own `table-layout:fixed!important`, as the doctor queue sets between 641 and 1024 px) and wraps text instead of cutting it with an ellipsis; cards, tiles, rows, list items and details do not break across sheets unless they hold a table or a list themselves (a wrapper around a long table that refuses to break leaves a blank sheet). Backgrounds go, except where the colour or the picture is the datum: status dots, the report bars and the doctor's portrait from the avatar sprite keep theirs with `print-color-adjust:exact`. A contact icon prints its number or address (`data-copy`). The sheet's head comes from `static/platform/print-head.mjs`: it composes «screen name · filters · Impreso: date, time» (the `#adm-h1` or first `h1`, the `[data-print-filter]` texts and the unlabelled filter controls that are not on their first option, the installation's date and hour through `dates.mjs`; `date.printed_at` in the ten languages) into the `--print-head` custom property of `html`, which `@page @top-left` prints; the sheet number goes `@bottom-right`. The panel loads it from `templates/admin/base.html`, the patient site lazily from `nav.mjs` (idle callback, and on `beforeprint`). A screen hides one more thing on paper with `data-print="off"`. The separate printouts (`templates/admin/agenda-print.html`, the j30 sheet and the door poster) do not link `human-rounds.css` and keep their own print rules. · Why: Product owner 2026-11-07, «make the pages printable»; every /admin screen and the key patient pages (center, service, my appointments, the booking receipt) printed from the demo to A4 before and after: the AI register went from 28 sheets to 6, the doctor's queue from a cut-off table to one sheet · Rejected: a print stylesheet per screen (the same twenty rules drift apart); `beforeprint` alone for the head (a PDF made without a print dialog never fires it, so the head is kept current on screen); `overflow-wrap:anywhere` (it shrinks a column's minimum width to one letter and splits DNIs) · Cost: a screen with a new floating control or toolbar that is neither a button nor a role prints it until it is added to the list or marked `data-print="off"` · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent
Source: `human-rounds.css` (`@media print`), `static/platform/print-head.mjs`, `templates/admin/base.html`, `static/platform/nav.mjs`, `core/static_fingerprint.py`, `locales/*/date.json`; `test/e2e/print-layer.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-07 · Panel tables: a name is not cut while there is room beside it, and what still cuts says so
Decided: the shared column engine (`static/platform/admin-table.mjs`) now measures every body cell itself, not only the columns a screen declared `entero` with its own measuring function. A column whose cells are all empty takes its heading's width, not its `min` («Cómo prepararse» at 1440: «En una línea» was empty in every row and kept 220 px while every long name was cut). Spare width goes first, pixel by pixel, to the column whose text that pixel uncovers, even past its `max` (the doctor's queue at 1440 cut «María de los Ángeles Fernández de la To» with 170 px of empty room to the right, because `fill:false` left the room unused); only what is left is spread by `flex`. A column of names (key or heading: nombre, paciente, médico, servicio, centro, preparación…) is weighed as `entero: 2` when it shrinks, without declaring it; a wrapping column never is, `entero: false` opts out, and only a declared `entero` raises the floor for dropping columns (one long name in Pacientes does not remove «Médico» at 768). Whatever still cuts gets an ellipsis and the full text: a block line inside a cell (the name line in the queue and in Pacientes) is marked `tbl-corta` while it cuts, so the cell no longer hides it with a hard edge, and the clipped element gets the full text in `title` (marked `data-tbl-title`, removed when it fits again; a title the screen set itself wins). In Pacientes the doctor cell keeps its «+N» in sight: the first name shrinks with an ellipsis instead of the ellipsis eating the number. · Why: Product owner 2026-11-07, «in the panel I saw tables where names are badly cut» · Rejected: cutting at a word boundary in JS (rewrites the text the copy and the search read; the ellipsis plus the full text in the globe already shows the whole name); a fixed `max` raise per screen (the screens differ, the engine owns the widths); dropping columns for an undeclared name column (drops a whole column for one row) · Cost: one extra measurement pass per fit, capped at 300 rows · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent
Source: `static/platform/admin-table.mjs` (`esNombre`, `pesoEntero`, `celdaVacia`, `envuelveCelda`, `filasDeDatos`, `titularRecortes`, the measuring block in `autoFit`), `admin-direct.css` (`.tbl-corta`, `.tbl-midiendo`), `static/platform/admin-patients.mjs` (`.pat-docs`, `ajustarMedicos`); `test/e2e/admin-table-names-whole.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-07 · e2e of the doctor's queue header: the seed opens Cardiología every day, so the spec is green on weekends and at any hour
Decided: `test/e2e/doctor-queue-header.spec.mjs` no longer depends on the day of the run. The example-town pack's «Cardiología» (which owns Levin's hours) runs Monday to Friday, so on a Saturday or Sunday her day had no free hours, the panel rightly hid «Mostrar horarios libres», and R1 at 1024/1280 and R11 failed 3/3 on clean main (07.11). The spec's seed now has the admin save that service's agenda for all seven days through `PUT /api/admin/content/services/<id>` (with `confirmar_reinicio_agendas`), the way the panel does; the free hours of a day are not cut at the current time (`_free_of_day`), so the hour of the run does not matter either. «Today» is the installation's day: when a run crosses midnight ART the seed books Delhoy's visit (with its interview) on the new day as well. The product is unchanged: with no free hours the checkbox stays hidden. The one other e2e seed tied to the hour, `fixture.py --queue-later` (the desk's «Cancelar turno» needs an appointment still ahead today), moved from now + 30 min to now + 4 h, capped at 23:59: it is seeded when the worker starts, and a loaded run took longer than 30 min to reach the step. · Why: pinamar-turnos finding names-crop-1107, a red e2e on clean main that depended on the weekday · Rejected: a frozen clock on the server (no `core/clock` seam in the day queue and nothing to pin, the day's hours do not read the hour); a `waitForTimeout`; editing the pack's agenda (other specs read the Monday-to-Friday hours) · Cost: in this spec's throwaway database Ruiz and Levin also have hours on weekends · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent
Source: `test/e2e/doctor-queue-header.spec.mjs` (`cardiologyEveryDay`, `seedToday`), `test/e2e/fixture.py` (`--queue-later`).

## 2026-11-07 · With no free slot the patient tells their case in a short chat, and the desk reads its summary first
Decided: on a service with no free slot, the wizard step «Contanos tu caso» is a chat instead of an empty text box (`modules/intake/case_chat.py`, routes `POST /api/intake/case/start`, `/{token}/message`, `/{token}/finish`). It reuses the interview engine (`interview_agent.run_turn` with the new entry mode `caso`, prompts `caso_preguntas` and `caso_resumen`): an opening question with suggested answers as buttons, then one to three clarifying questions, urgency included («Para cuando necesitas que te vean»). The cap is code, not prompt: `INTAKE_CASE_MAX_QUESTIONS` (clamped 1..3, default 3) closes the chat after that many answers whatever the model says; the patient can stop earlier with «Listo, enviar» or by saying so. The interview's deterministic emergency floor (`dialogue.urgent_now`) runs before the model and answers guardia / 107; the summary of such a chat always reads urgency `emergencia`. On closing, one AI reading writes the summary for the desk (what they want, urgency alta/media/baja/emergencia, why, key facts) over the transcript scrubbed by `core/pii.py`; without a model (no key, breaker open, `TURNOS_AI_DISABLED`, module intake off, any model failure) `start` answers `disponible: false` and the wizard keeps the plain box, a chat that loses its model mid-way closes, and the summary falls back to the patient's own words with the urgency read from the urgency answer. The request is never blocked: Confirma waits for the summary up to 15 s, otherwise `bind` computes it. The request creation calls the intake hook `case_chat_bind`, which moves the summary and the transcript into the new column `booking_requests.case_chat` (Alembic 0039, nullable jsonb, `{resumen, mensajes}`) and deletes the anonymous `chat_messages` rows of channel `caso`; `patient_note` keeps the patient's words, so every older reader keeps working. Per IP only a new chat counts, in its own window (`INTAKE_CASE_PER_HOUR`, default 20 an hour; over it the wizard keeps the plain box): the answers are already capped per chat, and counting them in the interview's 12/h left the third patient behind one carrier's shared address without the chat. In the panel the request detail (pending and resolved) shows «Su caso»: the urgency as words with its dot, what they want, why, the facts, then «Ver conversacion», which loads the whole chat from `GET /api/admin/requests/{id}/case-chat`, a clinical read written to `core/access_log` (action `caso`, denied attempts too). The queue payload carries only the summary; `db_wire` hides `case_chat`. Both legal texts name the new chat (the AI filter list and the access log list); the AI register lists `case_chat` with its notice `ai.notice.case` and the risk file `docs/compliance/risk/case_chat.md`. The reference-deployment counterpart's points from 19.10 are in the copy: the desk evaluates the case and offers what fits (a slot later, telemedicine, spontaneous demand, an extra slot), a health worker decides, emergencies go to guardia or 107, buttons instead of a blank page. · Why: Product owner, voice of 2026-11-07: people should not face an empty box, they answer a couple of clarifying questions, urgency among them, and the desk sees a summary first and the full chat on demand. · Rejected: a new chat engine (the interview already has the PII scrub, the emergency floor, the fake mode and the breaker); the cap in the prompt only (a model can ignore it); storing the chat only in `chat_messages` (the desk would need the anonymous key, and retention is per channel); sending the whole transcript in the queue payload (an unlogged clinical read). · Cost: an abandoned chat (no request sent) stays in `chat_messages` under channel `caso` until the chat retention purge, which is off unless the installation sets `TURNOS_CHAT_RETENTION_DAYS`; one more model call per closed chat. · Undo: revert the commit; the column stays (0039 is forward-only) and older code ignores it. · By: Product owner (chat instead of the box, a few questions with urgency, summary first then the chat), agent (engine reuse, cap in code, column on the request, logged read).
Source: `modules/intake/case_chat.py`, `modules/intake/interview_agent.py`, `modules/intake/interview_patient_router.py`, `modules/intake/hooks.py`, `ai/prompts.py`, `core/db_chat.py`, `core/access_log.py`, `core/db_wire.py`, `core/schema_model.py`, `core/personal_data.py`, `core/ai_register.py`, `alembic/versions/0039_booking_case_chat.py`, `modules/turnos/booking_create.py`, `modules/turnos/booking_queue.py`, `modules/turnos/booking_requests_router.py`, `modules/turnos/service.mjs`, `admin-app.js`, `static/platform/admin-solicitudes.mjs`, `patient_web/legal.py`, `docs/compliance/risk/case_chat.md`; `test/test_case_chat.py`, `test/admin-case-chat.test.mjs`, `test/service-no-slots.test.mjs`, `test/e2e/journey-j21-case-and-authorised-overbooking.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-07 · Print: table columns are sized for paper right before printing; Chats dates in one short form
Decided: on paper a table's columns no longer inherit the screen's widths. `static/platform/print-head.mjs` (`sizeTables`, run on `beforeprint` and when the print media query turns on) marks cells and the print layer of `human-rounds.css` reads the marks, the screen never does: a column that shows nothing in the whole table (no text, a dash, no dot or picture once the controls are off) gets `data-print-empty` and is not printed; every column first gets its narrowest width (its longest piece that cannot wrap: a word, a person with the age, a doctor's name, an e-mail address, a phone), and what the sheet has left goes to the columns of short cells (40 characters at most), cheapest first, as one line (`data-print-nowrap`); running text (a question, a note) always wraps and takes what is left. Under the print styles the browser measures (a hidden copy of the cell at min-content and max-content, so icons and an address printed from `data-copy` count); before them, the text measured on a canvas. When the narrowest widths alone pass the sheet (Pacientes: the e-mail addresses), Chrome lays the page out up to 1.5 times wider and shrinks it onto the paper, so the room is 1.5 sheets. A person, a doctor's name and a phone never wrap inside (`.pat-person`, `.pat-doc-n`, `td a[href^="tel:"]`); the doctors of a patient print all, wrapping between the names (the «+N» is for hover). The DNI copy icon is off the paper. Two grids that are not tables got their paper tracks: the request list (the check box track is empty, the AI reading gets 160 px, «Sin orden adjunta» is one line) and the Wiki tree (the expand arrow's track goes, the category title no longer falls into it one letter a line). The admin «Chats» list showed «Viernes 6 de noviembre, 21:58» beside «Vie 6 nov, 17:21» (the long form for today and yesterday through `relativo`, the server's offset left as Postgres sent it): the server now sends both times on the installation clock (`core/tz.iso`) and every row uses the short shared form `dates.corta`. · Why: the product owner's hub 07.11, the demo's Pacientes PDF printed «Laura Abdala, 39» in three lines and the doctors in three beside a wide «Contacto» and an empty «Servicio más usado»; all demo screens printed and compared before and after · Rejected: per-screen print widths (every table needs the rule; the screen widths are wrong for paper); `overflow-wrap:anywhere` for e-mail addresses (they broke into three lines and the sheet still shrank); a fixed character width estimate (the copy icon, the status dots and the font made it 30 px short per column, so the table ran past the shrunk sheet) · Cost: a table wider than the sheet at its narrowest still prints shrunk (Pacientes at 2/3); a table with row-spanning cells keeps the browser's widths · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent
Source: `static/platform/print-head.mjs` (`paperCell`, `probe`, `sizeTable`, `sizeTables`), `human-rounds.css` (`@media print`: `data-print-empty`, `data-print-nowrap`, `.sol-row`, `.kn-category-row`), `static/platform/admin-patients.mjs` (`.pat-doc-n`), `modules/chat/admin_chats_screen.py`, `modules/chat/admin_chats.html`, `test/e2e/print-layer.spec.mjs`, `test/test_admin_chats_screen.py`.

## 2026-11-07 · «Cómo prepararse»: the name column has no width cap
Decided: the name column of the «Cómo prepararse» list no longer declares `max: 340`. · Why: with a long word list beside it the table does not fit, so the engine takes its shrinking path, where widths start at `min(natural, max)`: the name started cut at 340 px and the list kept the rest, while the engine's growth past `max` only runs when the row has spare room. On the demo at 1440 that cut «MAPA / Presurometría (monitoreo ambulat…» and «Ecografía pélvica / ginecológica / transvagi…». Without the cap the word list shrinks first and the names stay whole · Rejected: `clip: true` on the word list (the greedy growth counts cut cells, and thirty cut word lists still outweigh two cut names); growing past `max` after the shrinking pass in the engine (the same count problem, for every table) · Cost: none · Undo: put the cap back · By: agent

## 2026-11-07 · Panel tables: a column empty in every row is not drawn, at any width
Decided: the shared table engine (`static/platform/admin-table.mjs`) leaves out a column whose cells are empty («—» or blank) in every row of the current list, at every width and in the stacked phone layout too: header, cells and its `<col>` get `display:none` (marked `data-tbl-vacia`), its width goes to the neighbours, and it comes back on the next fit as soon as one row carries a value (the observer sweep refits a table whose hidden column gained an unmarked cell). An empty list hides nothing. Replaces the 6cc131612 rule, which only let an empty column fall first on narrow screens: at 1440 «En una línea» in «Cómo prepararse» was empty in all 30 rows and still held its header's width while «Se reconoce por» ended in «…». · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-07 (the product owner's demo frame) · Rejected: per-screen `ocultaVacia` flags (one rule in the engine, Pacientes keeps its own); hiding through the `tbl-oc-*` class (only some screens have CSS for it) · Cost: a column on by default that is empty is hidden while still ticked in the picker (the flat preference cannot tell an untouched tick from a re-ticked one) · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent

## 2026-11-07 · Panel lists: Chats, Registro de IA and Roles take the full width; a filter button in a list cell reads dark
