## 2026-11-06 · A database at an Alembic revision this code does not know is refused once per process, with a message that names it
Decided: When `alembic_version` holds a revision the running code does not have (a newer release migrated the database, then older code was deployed or restored over it), `db_schema._ensure_schema` refuses the database at the first connection, under the migration lock, first thing in `_upgrade_to_head`, before the Alembic upgrade, and remembers the refusal for that URL (`db_schema._refused`, an `UnknownRevision` naming the stored revision(s), the newest one this code knows and what to do: redeploy that release or a later one, or restore the backup taken before that migration; a downgrade is run with the newer release, never with this one; `docs/UPGRADING.md` «Older code against a newer database»). Every later connection reads the stamp with one SELECT, no lock and no Alembic, and raises the same refusal (`db.SchemaRefused`, a `DbUnavailable`, so a request gets the usual 503) until the stamp changes: once the database is restored or the newer release stamps it back, the next connection bootstraps normally with no restart. The refusal is logged once per process as an ERROR of `turnos.schema`; `/ready` answers 503 with the whole message in its database detail (`system_status.probe_db`, not cut to 200 characters), and `/admin/login` shows the outage card saying the database was updated by a newer version, with the same message to hand to whoever installs the system (the panel, «Sistema» included, cannot open without a database, so the login card is where an operator sees it). The process keeps running instead of exiting: a container that exits on boot restarts in a loop and hides the message, and a restore would then also need a redeploy. Before this, an unknown revision was not remembered: every connection took the migration lock, ran Alembic and failed with "Can't locate revision" (0.1 to 2.6 s each), which on salud after a rollback to older code would have slowed every request. It sits on top of the bridge removal (entry of 2026-11-05): `_refuse_pre_alembic` refuses a database with no stamp and pre-Alembic tables, this one a stamp the code does not know; the two do not overlap. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-06, after ab45b1ac (the shared database stamped with a revision main does not have) · Rejected: exiting the process at boot (restart loop, message lost in the container log, a restore needs a redeploy too); stamping or downgrading the database from the older code (it does not have the newer revision's downgrade); retrying the migration on every connection (the bug) · Cost: one SELECT of `alembic_version` per new pooled connection while refused · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent
Source: `core/db_schema.py` (`UnknownRevision`, `_refuse_unknown_revision`, `_raise_if_still_refused`), `core/db.py` (`SchemaRefused`, `schema_refusal`), `admin_panel/system_status.py`, `admin_panel/admin_login_screen.py`, `docs/UPGRADING.md`; `test/test_schema_unknown_revision.py`.

## 2026-11-06 · A lone test file runs on a database of its own on the test server
Decided: `scripts/turnos_managed_exec.py --profile backend-focused --test test/test_x.py` runs the file through `scripts/run_backend_focused.py`, which does for one file what `scripts/run_backend_shards.py` does for each lane: it creates `sana_turnos_test_focused_<run token>` on the test server (`TEST_DATABASE_URL`, else `DATABASE_URL`), points both variables at it, runs the file with `unittest discover`, and drops the database when the file ends (the name has the `_dbtest` run-scoped shape, so a killed run's database is reclaimed by `_dbtest.reclaim_stale_databases`). Without a reachable server the file runs as before, with a line saying so and no URL printed. Before this, a file run alone talked to the shared development database on 5432 whenever it had no `_dbtest` database of its own, and inherited whatever Alembic revision another branch's tests had left there: of 560 backend test files, 174 reach the database layer without their own database (95 of them connect or boot the app), against 172 with one. The fix is in the runner, not per file. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-06 (test_servicio_info hung for 55 minutes alone, on a clean main) · Rejected: giving each of the 174 files its own `PerModuleDb` (the same hole reopens with the next file); rolling the shared database back by hand (it moves with branches) · Cost: one database create and drop per focused run (a few seconds) · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent
Source: `scripts/run_backend_focused.py`, `scripts/turnos_managed_exec.py` (`_focused`); `test/test_run_backend_focused.py`.

## 2026-11-06 · The one-process backend run gets a database of its own too
Decided: `--profile backend-full` runs the same one-process `unittest discover -p test_*.py` through `scripts/run_backend_focused.py` (argument `test_*.py`, the only pattern it accepts), so it creates, uses and drops its own database on the test server instead of the shared development database on 5432. Found on the first full run after the entry above: the shared database was stamped with a revision main does not know, the app now refuses it once per process (entry above), and four tests that lean on it errored in the one-process run while every one of those files passed alone on its own database. The sharded gate (`scripts/run_backend_shards.py`, local-ci) already worked this way. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-06, same cause as the entry above · Rejected: leaving backend-full on 5432 (its result depended on which branch's tests ran there last) · Cost: one create and drop per full run · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent
Source: `scripts/turnos_managed_exec.py` (`backend-full`); `test/test_run_backend_focused.py` (`TestProfiles`).

## 2026-11-06 · The real-worker test harness waits for a log line under a ceiling that grows with the machine load
Decided: `test/_workerproc.WorkerProc.wait_line` (every `wait_line("corriendo")` and the other line waits of `test_queue_worker.py`, `test_business_queue_proof.py`, `test_data_backups_worker_proof.py`, `test_hsi_worker_retry_acceptance.py`, `test_ai_cache_queue_proof.py`, `test_worker_diagnostics.py`) now reads its `timeout` as the ceiling on a quiet machine and waits through `_wait.until(..., scale_with_load=True)`: the ceiling is multiplied by the 1-minute load average per CPU, clamped to 1..8, re-read on every poll and never shrinking within one wait. The signal stays the worker's own line: `worker._run_opened_pool` logs "corriendo" in the same synchronous step that sets `fase` (what `/ready` reports), so the line is the readiness event; only the fixed 20 s was wrong. Measured 2026-11-06 on the dev machine (18 CPUs): at load 55-61 a worker took 13-22 s from spawn to "corriendo" (9-15 s of it before its first line, the imports), so at load ~90 the 20 s ceiling turned four tests of `test_queue_worker.py` red on a clean `main` with the worker still alive (`exit=None`); at load ~95 a whole run had 16 red tests. The same file applies the rule to every other wait on its live workers: all its polls go through `_until` (`scale_with_load=True`), every fixed `subprocess.run(timeout=...)` / `proc.wait(...)` ceiling on a worker run takes `_wait.scaled(<the old seconds>)` (20, 30, 60, and the 15 s exit after a signal; a missed `timeout=30` on the invalid-interval boot was red twice in six runs at load 110-148), and the rolling-restart proof holds worker A's tick for `max(12, _wait.scaled(12))` s instead of 8, because worker B's whole boot must fit inside that hold. The same goes for the two other holds a second worker must outlast: the cold-boot race holds for `max(15, _wait.scaled(15))` s and counts a row as settled only once it has left `todo` and stayed the same for one scaled interval (at load ~295 a still-`todo` snapshot passed as stable), and the heartbeat-under-a-held-tick proof holds for `max(18, _wait.scaled(18))` s and ends with `cleanup()`, whose stop budget is the scaled one. The disk-alert kill-boundary proof now waits for the "accepted" line in the seam's file, not for the file: the seam's `open(..., "a")` creates it a moment before the write, and at load ~110 the SIGKILL landed in between (0 != 1). More fixed ceilings showed at load ~200-220 (three runs at once, 3/3 red): the startup-vs-startup proof keeps worker A waiting at the test release gate while worker B boots, and the seam's own 30 s gate timeout failed A's job first, so the spawning test now sets `TURNOS_TEST_SWEEP_RELEASE_GATE_TIMEOUT_S=600` (read by `queue_infra._wait_for_test_release_gate` only behind `TURNOS_TEST_SEAMS=1`; 30 s stays the default; the test always releases the gate); the same for the hsi_sync rekick gate of the two cold-boot race proofs, where worker B gave up its 30 s wait during A's boot (`TURNOS_TEST_HSI_REKICK_GATE_TIMEOUT_S=600`, read by `hsi_journal_queue._maybe_rekick_release_gate` behind the same switch); and the shutdown-with-a-pending-rekick proof adds `_wait.scaled(2) - 2` to both of its bounds, because the worker's own deadlines are wall-clock while the process overhead around them took 5-7 s at that load (zero on a quiet machine, so the 7 s worker bound and the zero-slop SIGKILL bound are unchanged there). The rapid spawn/SIGTERM stress now reports the worker's stderr tail with a non-zero exit (once 1/40 at load ~200, with no evidence). At load below 18 the ceiling is the old one, so a real hang fails as fast as before; under load it fails after at most 8 times the quiet ceiling and names the load it waited for. The worker itself is unchanged outside that test seam. · Why: pinamar-turnos hub task d1868134 (local-ci and pre-commit of every executor red on the clean `main` under parallel load) · Rejected: polling `/ready` instead of the line (the same moment, and the health port is itself announced by a line that waits the same way); a readiness file written by the worker (a product change for a signal that already exists); a flat larger ceiling (slows the failure of a real hang on a quiet machine and is still a guess under load) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `test/_wait.py`, `test/_workerproc.py`, `test/test_wait.py`, `test/test_queue_worker.py`, `core/queue_infra.py`, `modules/turnos/hsi_journal_queue.py`, `test/test_queue_sql_pins.py`, `docs/solutions/dev/test-environment-and-time.md`.

## 2026-11-06 · The worker's log line falls back to the raw descriptor when a signal re-enters stderr
Decided: `worker._log` catches the `RuntimeError` CPython raises for a re-entrant call into the stderr `BufferedWriter` and writes that one line straight to file descriptor 2 with `os.write` (unbuffered, `_STDERR_FD` so a test can point it at a pipe). The plain SIGTERM/SIGINT handler of `main()` logs through `_log` (`_SignalState.request_stop`); when the signal landed while the main thread was inside its own `print` to stderr, the handler's `print` re-entered the same buffer, the exception left the handler and the worker exited 1 instead of its graceful 0 — seen once in 40 rounds of `test_rapid_spawn_sigterm_never_orphans_worker_row` at load ~200 on 2026-11-06, with the stderr tail ending in `_handle_early_signal` → `request_stop` → `_log` → `RuntimeError`. Pinned by `test/test_worker_log_reentrant.py`, whose stream runs `request_stop` from inside its raw `write` (red before the change with that same `RuntimeError`). · Why: pinamar-turnos hub task d1868134; the real-worker stress test showed a product bug, not a slow machine · Rejected: logging from the handler through `os.write` only (the asyncio-phase callers of `request_stop` run outside a signal frame and keep the ordinary buffered stream); ignoring signals while logging (a signal must never be lost) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `worker.py`, `test/test_worker_log_reentrant.py`.

## 2026-11-07 · A worker that overruns its stop budget in the tests leaves every thread stack; a proof never has an unrelated sync sweep due while it stops
Decided: `test/_workerproc.WorkerProc` starts every worker with `PYTHONFAULTHANDLER=1` (in the child's copy of the env) and, when `stop()` overruns its budget, sends SIGABRT before the SIGKILL: faulthandler writes the stack of every thread to stderr and the failure message carries it (pinned by `test/test_workerproc_thread_dump.py`, a stand-in worker hung on a lock). The first dump named the cause of the one failure in a 20-run series of `test_queue_worker.py` under load (2026-11-07): the replacement worker of the chart-summary crash proof also consumed the translate warmup queue, the startup translation job its predecessor had scheduled 30 s out came due, and the worker sat in `translate_batch` → `translation_upsert` (the `en` catalog sweep, 190 s at load ~105) while procrastinate waited for it after the 20 s graceful timeout: a sync task runs in a thread through `asgiref.sync_to_async`, and cancelling it waits for the thread. Each sweep-recovery proof now turns the other sweep off (`TURNOS_TRANSLATE_WARMUP_OFF` / `TURNOS_SUMMARY_PREGEN=0`), and the startup proof moves its scheduled translation row an hour out before it stops the worker. The same series showed one more fixed ceiling, in the worker's own env: at load ~180 the first pooled connection took longer than `TURNOS_DB_POOL_TIMEOUT` (3 s), `ensure_schema` raised `DbUnavailable` and the worker exited 1 before its health line; `worker_env` now hands every test worker that wait times the load factor (3 s on a quiet machine; `test/test_workerproc_env.py`). · Why: pinamar-turnos hub task d1868134 (follow-up m1959298: dump before the kill, find the cause of run 6) · Rejected: py-spy (needs root on macOS); a SIGUSR1 handler inside the worker (a hung main thread in C code never runs a Python signal handler, faulthandler writes from the C handler); raising the stop budget (the sweep is 190 s, not slow shutdown) · Cost: a hung worker in a test now costs up to 10 s more before the kill · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Open: the product keeps the shape — a SIGTERM during a translate sweep cannot be honored inside the supervisor's 45 s budget, so `asgi_main` SIGKILLs the worker and the job comes back through its re-run contract; a cooperative stop check between upserts in `translate_batch` would let it exit on time.
Source: `test/_workerproc.py`, `test/test_workerproc_thread_dump.py`, `test/test_workerproc_env.py`, `test/test_queue_worker.py`.

## 2026-11-07 · Stacked catalog cards: dark values, a separator never on a line edge; Chats headers are `cnt-th`
Decided: the three catalog tables that stack as business cards with a middle-dot separator below 1024px (Servicios, Centros, Médicos) share ONE rule in `static/platform/admin-content.mjs`. The row is a wrapping flex line (`column-gap:16px`); every datum is a block and its dot is an absolutely placed `::after` in the 16px gap to its left. A flex gap does not exist at the start of a line, so the dot of a datum that opens a line falls outside the row and the row clips it (`overflow:clip`, 4px clip margin for focus rings): no more «20 h ·» at a line end, «· Próximo día» at a line start, or a dot after the last datum (an empty cell, Cuenta before the users read, is not drawn). Values read in `--text`; the labels («Horas por semana», «Próximo día», «Ocupación 14 días», dates) and the dots in `--hint-ink`, as every panel label since 903619ffc (values were `--text-2`, labels `--text-3`). The doctor's checkbox stays on the name's line as a flex item (no float). Pacientes, Chats, the queue and the other stacked lists have their own layouts without dots and are untouched. «Chats» headers wear `cnt-th` (13px, 600, grey) like Roles, Captura de síntomas and Médicos; bare `<th>` showed 16px bold dark at 1440. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-07 (the product owner's demo frame of Médicos at 390) · Rejected: inline text separators with `white-space:nowrap` pairs (a dot still ends a line when the next datum wraps) · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent
Source: `static/platform/admin-content.mjs` (catalog `@media(max-width:1024px)` block), `modules/chat/admin_chats.html`, `test/admin-list-width.test.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-screen.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-07 · «Descargar» exports the small parts of the panel as the CSV of what the screen shows; the big lists have no button
Decided (the product owner, 07.11: an export for every small part — reports, the patients of the day and so on; never the big places, never all the patients): (1) One shared button of the table engine, `Table.exportHtml({scope})` in `static/platform/admin-table.mjs`, the same icon button as «Elegir columnas» (`cnt-gear-btn tbl-export-btn`, Icons8 `download`), beside it. It sits on «Pacientes de hoy», «Mis turnos», «Mis pacientes», the doctor's calendar, Médicos, Servicios, Centros de salud, Usuarios, and the report tables without a file of their own (Búsquedas, IA «Quién gasta más»). It is NOT on Pacientes, the logs (access, AI, changes), Solicitudes, Roles or Preparaciones: those are the big or unbounded places; the reports that already download their own CSV (Estadística, recupero, SUMAR) keep it. (2) The file is what the screen shows, read from the painted DOM: the visible tables of the scope, the visible columns in their order with the visible heading text, the visible rows under the current filter, search and day; band rows, free slots, buttons and screen-only parts are left out by markup (`data-export-skip` on a table, row, heading or cell, `data-export-omit` inside a cell, `data-export` for a cell's full value or a heading's words, `data-export-group` for a grouping column such as the day, `data-export-dni` on a clinical row). (3) The server is the gate, `POST /api/admin/table-export` (`admin_panel/table_export.py`): only the screens listed in `SCREENS`, with the permission of that screen (`permissions.required_permission`), a row limit (`TURNOS_EXPORT_MAX_ROWS`, default 1000) refused with a sentence that says what to do (narrow the filter or the day), every DNI the file carries logged with `access_log.record_many(..., "descarga", ...)` before the file is written (a row that cannot be logged refuses the file), a cell that starts with `= + - @` and is not a plain number, phone, date or time gets a leading apostrophe against spreadsheet formulas. (4) CSV only, no XLSX: UTF-8 with a BOM and CRLF, separator `;` where the language writes decimals with a comma (es, pt, fr, it, de, ru, uk), `,` otherwise; the file name is the screen and the day («Pacientes de hoy 2026-11-07.csv»).
Source: `static/platform/admin-table.mjs` (`exportHtml`, `collectExport`), `admin_panel/table_export.py`, `admin_panel/table_export_router.py`, `core/access_log.py` (`descarga`), `static/platform/admin-queue.mjs`, `admin-my-appointments.mjs`, `admin-doctor-patients.mjs`, `admin-content.mjs`, `admin-users.mjs`, `admin-reports.mjs`; `test/test_table_export.py`, `test/admin-table-export.test.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-table-export.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-06 · Calendar-feed tokens (patient and doctor) are stored only as their SHA-256 hash; the full address is shown once
Decided: `users.feed_token` (a patient's `/feed/appointments/<token>.ics`) and `operators.feed_token` (a doctor's `/feed/doctor/<token>.ics`) hold the hex SHA-256 of the token, never the token, and the new columns `feed_token_hint` hold its last 4 characters; one formula for both in `core/feed_tokens.py` (`digest`, `new`: `secrets.token_urlsafe(16)`, 128 random bits, so an unsalted fast hash is enough and the lookup stays one indexed equality on the same unique index). `db_accounts.feed_token_issue(dni)` / `db_operators.operator_feed_token_issue(usuario)` create a token only when the account has none, in one atomic UPDATE (two tabs issue ONE address), and return the plaintext only then, otherwise only the hint; `feed_token_regenerate` / `operator_rotate_feed_token` rotate and return the new plaintext once; the get-or-create readers `db_accounts.feed_token_of` and `db_operators.operator_feed_token` are gone. API: `GET /api/calendar/feed` and `GET /api/admin/my-appointments/feed` answer `{url, hint}` with `url` only on the answer that created the address, else `{url: null, hint}`; NEW `POST /api/calendar/feed/regenerate` (patient session, module manifest) answers `{url, hint}` of a new address, like the doctor's existing `POST …/my-appointments/feed/rotate`. «Shown once» means one server answer carries the address: the page keeps it in memory until it is reloaded (closing and reopening the popup on the same page shows it again), and an answer lost after its write leaves the patient on the hint, with «Crear una dirección nueva» as the way out. Screens: the patient's «Agregar todos al calendario» no longer fetches the address on render; the popup asks for it when opened and shows the full address with the copy-and-follow steps plus «no se vuelve a mostrar» only on the answer that created it; afterwards «Ya tenés una dirección de calendario, la que termina en XXXX» and one button «Crear una dirección nueva» (the previous one stops updating). A doctor's «Mis turnos» does the same; reopening after a failed load retries. The admin's «Cambiar la dirección del calendario» (`reset-calendar` in `admin_users.USER_ACTIONS`) now REVOKES the token (`operator_revoke_feed_token`: token and hint NULL, the old address answers 404 at once) instead of rotating it, because a token rotated by the admin could never be shown to anyone; the doctor's next «Agregar a mi calendario» issues a new address and shows it once. Alembic 0040 adds the two hint columns and, per table, turns an empty token into NULL and then hashes every token in place (`encode(sha256(convert_to(feed_token,'UTF8')),'hex')`, hint `right(feed_token, 4)`), so every calendar app already subscribed keeps updating with no action from the patient or the doctor; the shape guard `!~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'` makes a rerun touch nothing, and it cannot skip a real token: every issuer since the feed appeared (2026-07-11) used `token_urlsafe(16)` (22 characters of base64url, never 64 hex), and no seed, script or import writes the column; a 64-character hash never meets a 22-character token under the unique constraints. A token still in the clear after 0040 (the previous build writing while both builds serve a deploy: its get-or-create on an empty row, or its rotation, which also leaves the old hint) is hashed in place, never replaced: on the next open of the address (`feed_tokens.hash_in_place`) or on the next calendar poll (`feed_tokens.heal_raw`, one indexed UPDATE on a lookup miss; a value of the hash's own shape is never compared raw, so a leaked hash never works as an address). The same change closes the second leak: `_user_public` returned `feed_token` in every `/api/account/*` answer and the operator rows of the panel carried the doctor's; both drop the hash and the hint now (`AccountUser` no longer declares it, body-contract goldens updated). `core/personal_data.REGISTRY` lists both hint columns as secrets (never exported, blanked on erasure). The regenerate POST has no CSRF token, like `/api/account/mcp/regenerate`: the patient cookie `st_ses` is `SameSite=Lax`, so a cross-site POST arrives without a session, and a forged one could only replace the address, never read it; the doctor's rotate sits under `/api/admin` behind the origin check. · Why: finding of 2026-11-06 (pinamar-turnos hub), same as the MCP token entry above: a database or backup leak handed out working calendar addresses, and a working address reads every future appointment of a patient (service, center, date) or a doctor's whole agenda. · Rejected: a salted or slow hash (needed for low-entropy passwords, not for 128 random bits; it would cost a scan per calendar poll instead of an index lookup); a new `feed_token_hash` column next to the old one (two columns for one datum and a later drop); reissuing every token (every subscribed calendar would go silent); keeping the address visible by encrypting it with an app key (a key next to the data in the same backup protects nothing); replacing a token found in the clear (it would silence the subscription made with it). · Cost: the patient sees the full address once; one who did not add it then, or wants it on a second device or calendar app, presses «Crear una dirección nueva» and the first subscription stops updating (it keeps the events it already has). While both builds serve a deploy of a test site, a calendar poll reaching the old build gets a 404 (the subscription URL itself is unchanged), and a popup opened on the old build shows an address built on the stored hash, which the new build refuses by design: that patient creates a new address. Verification: after the deploy, a calendar address issued before it is fetched on each test site and must still serve the calendar. salud.pinamar.gob.ar gets the change in its next release package, which stops the service before migrating, so it has no overlap. · Undo: a revert of the code cannot use the hashed tokens: every subscription made before the revert answers 404; the old get-or-create returns the stored hash as the token, so «Agregar a mi calendario» on the reverted build shows an address that works there, and each patient and doctor adds it again (a doctor can also «Cambiar la dirección»). The hash cannot be turned back into the token, so 0040 has no downgrade. · By: pinamar-turnos hub (hash only, show once, last 4 characters afterwards, hash in place without reissue; the second-device cost and the admin reset revoking, 2026-11-06), agent (same column, shared formula, atomic issue, lazy popup, in-place repair of a token found in the clear).

## 2026-11-07 · One doctor's day prints as HSI's «Turnos programados» sheet
Decided: GET `/admin/cola/print?medico=<id>&dia=<YYYY-MM-DD>[&auto=1]` renders a standalone A4 page (no panel chrome, `@media print` hides its one button bar, `auto=1` opens the print dialog on load) with the layout of the sheet HSI prints: a head with the doctor, the day asked for and the print date and hour (shared formatter, installation time zone), then one block per agenda window of that day (`Turnos programados (17:00 a 19:20)`), rows by time, and the nine columns of HSI's sheet (time, HSI patient id, document, names, surnames, coverage, card number, phone, state). Unknown cells are empty, never a dash or a zero. The windows come from the same schedule sources and day resolution as the slot generator (a `cerrado` exception closes the day, an `extra` one adds a window); a row outside every window (overbooking, agenda edited after booking) goes to a last block «Turnos fuera de la agenda». Landscape, because nine columns with names and coverage do not fit portrait. Buttons: «Imprimir» on a doctor's group in «Pacientes de hoy», on each day of Médicos → Calendario, and on each day heading of a doctor's «Mis turnos» (no `medico`: the server prints the linked doctor's own day). Access: the `cola` permission of the prefix; a doctor-linked account is held to its own doctor (another doctor's id answers 404 «No encontramos a ese médico.» and writes a denied `clinical_access_log` row, action `agenda_impresa`); every printed patient gets an access row through `access_log.record_many`. The page opened without a doctor answers 200 with «Elegí un médico…» (journey J28: a screen a role may open answers 200). · Why: the reference deployment's technical counterpart sent HSI's printout and asked for the same from the panel (pinamar-turnos, 2026-11-07) · Rejected: the browser's print of the queue screen (panel layout, other doctors' groups, no windows); a PDF generated on the server (one more dependency for what the browser prints well); a dash in empty cells (the hospital's sheet leaves them blank) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `modules/turnos/agenda_print.py`, `admin_panel/admin_agenda_print_screen.py`, `templates/admin/agenda-print.html`, `modules/turnos/module.json`, `static/platform/admin-queue.mjs`, `admin-content.mjs`, `admin-my-appointments.mjs`, `admin-queue.css`, `admin-direct.css`, `core/access_log.py`; `test/test_agenda_print.py`, `test/test_access_log_routes.py`, `test/admin-cola.test.mjs`, `test/agenda-print-buttons.test.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-agenda-print.spec.mjs`, `test/e2e/journey-j28-role-and-session-boundaries.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-08 · The demo seeds every doctor's appointment inside that doctor's agenda, no overbookings
Decided: every appointment the demo seed gives a doctor (today's queue, the 90-day history, approved and cancelled, and the featured patient's visits) sits on a free slot of that doctor's agenda on its day, read from the same sources as the slot generator and the printed day sheet (managed services, the doctor's center windows, the flat agenda; `cerrado`/`extra` exceptions), and no two approved appointments of a doctor share a slot. One ledger, `AgendaDemo` in `scripts/seed_demo_all.py`, is built once per seed: the featured patient's appointment with the demo doctor is reserved first (a slot later today when he works today, else his next working day), today's queue fills around it, the history takes the nearest free slot of a past day. The seed deliberately creates no overbooking: the printed sheet's «Turnos fuera de la agenda» block is covered by its own e2e, and on the demo the owner shows, a row outside the doctor's hours reads as a bug. Measured on the live demo on Sunday 2026-11-08: the demo doctor's print showed only «Turnos fuera de la agenda», because the featured appointment was «now + 18 h» (18:02 on a day without agenda) and the history used arbitrary hours; the new test counted 71 of them outside on a weekday and 72 on a Sunday with the old seed, 0 with the new one. `test/test_seed_demo_store.py` `TestSeedInsideTheAgenda` seeds on a weekday and on a Sunday and checks every doctor's row against `agenda_print.day_windows` and every approved slot for doubles. · Why: the hub's brief of 2026-11-08 (the A4 day sheet on the demo had no agenda block) · Rejected: a few seeded overbookings to show the out-of-agenda block (the e2e already shows it, and the demo day must read as a normal day) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `scripts/seed_demo_all.py`; `test/test_seed_demo_store.py`.

## 2026-11-08 · The test lane cleans up after itself: idle day-old databases, a killed runner's databases, a full VM disk

What: on 2026-11-07 at 23:40 the colima VM disk that holds the test Postgres (127.0.0.1:5433) was 100% full, and every routes tier failed with DiskFull. There were two sources:
- 281 anonymous docker volumes, from Lira's local CI and not from this repo. Its disposable `postgres:16` runs with `--rm`, but the cleanup removed it with a bare `docker rm -f`. A container removed by hand skips its auto-remove, and its anonymous data volume stays. Lira now uses `docker rm -f -v`.
- Routes databases left by runners that were killed: 46 of 56 runs of one night, 3 GB. Pre-commit's reaper TERMs the routes runner and KILLs it 10 s later, while the runner's EXIT trap is still reaping its workers before its own drop.

Changes:
- `reclaim_stale_run_databases` reclaims a namespace whose token pid no longer exists on this host after 5 minutes rather than 2 hours. The session check and the drop without FORCE are unchanged.
- `scripts/lane_janitor.py` runs from `test-db-up.sh` and from `pre-commit.sh` before the routes tier, and never fails either one:
  - it drops idle `sana_turnos_test_<suffix>` databases older than a day (creation time from the database's PG_VERSION file), which covers killed e2e, focused and shard runs;
  - after a drop, it runs `colima ssh -- sudo fstrim -av`;
  - past 85% of the VM's docker disk, it prints how to free it.

Why: each runner already drops its own databases, but only when it is allowed to finish. The backstop must not need the killed process. The pid in the token says the run is over sooner than its age does. A reused pid only makes a dead run look alive, so it is spared until the 2-hour rule. Dropping without FORCE and with no session keeps a live run safe. `hsi-db`, `sana_turnos` and `postgres` do not match the name. The disk warning is the signal that was missing on 2026-11-07. The VM disk went from 64% to 39% after the cleanup of 2026-11-08, and fstrim returned 36.8 GiB to the Mac.

By: pinamar-turnos hub (task: the source of the volumes, cleanup of every throwaway database even after a kill, a warning past 85%, fstrim; 2026-11-08), agent (pid-based reclaim with a 5-minute grace, the janitor in pre-commit as well as in test-db-up, creation time from PG_VERSION).

Source: `scripts/lane_janitor.py`, `scripts/test-db-up.sh`, `scripts/pre-commit.sh`, `test/_routes_fixture.py`, `docs/architecture/entorno-y-pruebas.md`; tests `test/test_lane_janitor.py`, `test/test_test_db_up.py`, `test/test_routes_run_isolation.py`.

## 2026-11-08 · A worker stop reaches the long synchronous sweeps: they halt between units of work, the rest waits for the next run
Decided: procrastinate cannot cancel a synchronous task body (it runs in a thread `Worker.stop()` never interrupts, and `asyncio.run` then waits for that thread), so a SIGTERM during the catalog translation waited for the whole sweep (190 s measured) and the supervisor SIGKILLed the worker after its 45 s budget. One cooperative stop, `core/worker_stop.py`: `worker._boot_and_run` opens one event per run (`begin`/`end`), the first signal sets it next to `Worker.stop()` (`request`), and a sweep takes `check()` once at its start and asks `halted(stop)` between two units of work, returning what it finished. Of the nine jobs in `worker._JOBS`, four have a long synchronous loop and now stop at its boundary: translate_warmup (between languages, between batches of `translate_batch`, between strings of its one-by-one fallback), chart_summary (between pairs), reminders (before each claim, both reminder kinds), hsi_sync (between backfill, replay, reconcile and the resolver). Three are short (waitlist, housekeeping, referrals). Two stay as they are: backup_full and data_backups are one archive or one upload each, not divisible without a partial object on the remote. Nothing outside the worker passes a stop, so the thread topology, scripts and the panel behave as before. Every unit left over is what the job's audited re-run contract already covers (stale cache rows, unclaimed reminders, durable outbox rows); the four `rerun` texts say so. A test seam, `TURNOS_TEST_SWEEP_STEP_HOLD_S` behind both test gates, holds each boundary so a subprocess proof can stop mid-sweep. Closes the "Open" of the 2026-11-07 entry on the worker's stop budget. Also: the startup defer computes the translation grace from `core.clock` (`schedule_at` = boot instant + 30 s, the same instant the row's `timestamp` argument carries) instead of `schedule_in`, so the test reads the grace off the row itself instead of comparing with the database's `now()`, another machine's clock read after a load-dependent delay. · Why: the queue-worker flake of 2026-11-06 traced every stop overrun to this sweep; a SIGKILL on every deploy during a translation is a worker that never exits clean · Rejected: running the sweep in a subprocess or killable thread (kills a provider call midway, adds a second process to supervise); raising the supervisor budget (the sweep's length grows with the catalog) · Cost: one AI call in flight (provider timeout 180 s) still cannot be interrupted, so the stop waits for at most that one batch · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `core/worker_stop.py`, `worker.py`, `core/translate.py`, `ops/translate_warmup.py`, `ops/translate_warmup_queue.py`, `ai/chart_summary.py`, `ai/chart_summary_queue.py`, `notifications/reminders.py`, `notifications/reminders_queue.py`, `modules/turnos/hsi_journal.py`, `modules/turnos/hsi_journal_queue.py`; `test/test_worker_stop.py`, `test/test_queue_worker.py` (`TestSweepStopsOnSignal`, `TestStartupSweeps`).

## 2026-11-08 · The demo has one schedule, the service's agenda, and doctors on every day of the week
Decided: in `packs/example-town` the source of truth for when a doctor works is the agenda of the services that schedule that doctor (`schedule_policy.managed_services`), the source the slot generator, the day queue, the printed day sheet and the demo seed already read. `scripts/generate_example_town_catalog.py` now copies those agendas into each doctor's own windows (`_agenda_de_sus_servicios`: tagged with the service when the doctor has more than one), so the public page's «Cuándo atiende» shows the hours the agenda books; a doctor no scheduled service manages keeps its template. Four services of the day queue (pediatrics at the maternity, nutrition at Riverside, ENT and dermatology at the hospital) move to the weekend template, Saturday and Sunday 9 to 12. The seed gives the two doctor-less services (laboratory, X-ray: «Lunes a viernes», no agenda) appointments only Monday to Friday. Measured before: 51 of the 68 demo doctors showed hours on their page that the engine did not use (Sánchez: Sunday 9 to 12 on his page, Tuesday and Thursday in the agenda), the engine had 0 doctors on Sunday and 10 on Saturday, none of them one of the 11 the seed fills, and a Sunday's «Pacientes de hoy» showed only laboratory and X-ray, both closed that day. After: 0 of 68 differ; 9 doctors work on Sunday and 19 on Saturday, 4 of each with a day queue. Tests: `test/test_demo_catalog_coverage.py` (page against `agenda_print.day_windows` for every doctor and day of a week; at least 4 doctors with a filled day on each of the 7 days, every hour inside the doctor's windows), `test/test_seed_demo_store.py` (no laboratory or X-ray on a Sunday, both on a weekday); the goldens `test/golden/doctor_pool.json` and `test/golden/site_pages.json` re-recorded (data only, the code they pin is unchanged; the pool by service is the same). · Why: the hub's brief of 2026-11-08 (the demo is shown on weekends; the Sunday day view was empty but for two closed services) · Rejected: the doctor's own windows as the source (the engine reads the service agenda whenever one manages the doctor, and saving a service agenda in the panel already clears its doctors' own windows, so a second truth would drift again); adding a weekend template to the rotation (it shifts every doctor's template) · Cost: the cardiologists who shared one service now share its hours, the variety of the old templates is gone where a service agenda rules · Undo: revert the commit and re-run the generator · By: agent.
Source: `scripts/generate_example_town_catalog.py`, `packs/example-town/`, `scripts/seed_demo_all.py`; `test/test_demo_catalog_coverage.py`, `test/test_seed_demo_store.py`.

## 2026-11-08 · The doctor's public page, the day queue and the printed sheet read one source of the doctor's windows
Decided: `modules/turnos/doctor_windows.py` is the one place that decides when a doctor works on a given day: the agendas of the services that manage the doctor (`schedule_policy.managed_services`), else the doctor's windows per center, else the legacy flat `agenda`; a `cerrado` exception drops its source that day, an `extra` one adds a window. The public page's «Cuándo atiende» (`site_availability._schedule_lines`) now walks the next 7 days through it instead of printing the doctor's own `franjas`, so a doctor with no hours of their own whose service has an agenda shows that agenda, own hours a managing service overrides no longer show, and a closed or extra day this week shows as the queue will hold it. The day queue (`doctor_appointments._doctor_agendas`, which loses `_own_windows`) and the printed day sheet (`agenda_print.day_windows`, which loses its private `_sources`) read the same function. The text of the page is unchanged («Lunes a viernes», «08:00 – 12:00», «No atiende»), with the same formatter; rows with the same day and time keep the order the schedule lists the offices in. Two small alignments with the slot generator came with it: own windows are one source per center, so closing one center no longer closes the doctor's other center in the queue, and the flat agenda's exceptions apply to its single-window form too. Measured: `test/golden/site_pages.json` (308 pages) unchanged, so 0 demo pages changed; the 68 demo doctors already matched after the demo's single-schedule change of the same day. Tests first: `test/test_doctor_page_service_agenda.py` (a doctor without hours under a service agenda, own hours the service overrides, a `cerrado` and an `extra` in the week) failed 4 of 5 before. · Why: the hub's brief of 2026-11-08 (the page showed hours the booking engine did not use) · Open: a doctor managed by a service in one place who keeps own windows for a service without an agenda: the page, queue and sheet follow the managed agenda only, the slot generator still offers those own windows for that service (the older rule that managing services replace everything; closed by the next entry) · Rejected: a second resolver for the page (the drift this fixes) · Cost: none · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-08 · A service's agenda replaces a doctor's own hours only for that service; booking and showing read one function
Decided: the rule is the one the slot generator already followed: a service with an agenda books its own agenda, and a service without one books the doctor's own windows at its center that belong to it (untagged or tagged with it). So a doctor managed by a service agenda who keeps own windows for a bookable service without an agenda attends both. `doctor_windows.for_service(doctor, service)` is now the only code that says which agendas book a service with a doctor (`schedule_policy.doctor_agendas` trimmed by `service_schedule`, moved here from `site_availability._service_schedule`), and every slot path calls it: the wizard and the doctor's grid (`_appointments_for_service`, `_appointments_for_doctor` through `_doctor_service_agendas`), the band (`_next_appointment_best`, `_doctor_next_raw`, `_next_opening`), the desk's suggestion (`booking_queue`), approval (`booking_requests`) and capacity (`booking_capacity`). `doctor_windows.sources` adds, for a managed doctor, the windows `for_service` gives each bookable agenda-less service the doctor belongs to, with that service named, so the page, the day queue and the printed sheet show what is booked. A walk-in service (`site_availability._offers_appointments` false) adds nothing: it offers no slot; a same-day opening does. On the page, offices of one center with the very same week share one strip that names them all («Consultorio Odontología general · Urgencias odontológicas») instead of the same strip twice. Measured: `test/golden/site_pages.json` 8 of 308 pages changed, 4 of them demo pages (Núñez and Karam, the two dentists of Riverside Clinic, es and en: their same-day dental emergencies now named next to Odontología general, one strip) and 4 the snapshot's own invented doctors (two offices with the same week now one strip). On the demo 14 managed doctors belong to an agenda-less service, 12 of them walk-in only. Tests first: the new case of `test/test_doctor_page_service_agenda.py` (booking Vacunación with a doctor managed by Pediatría's agenda offers Tuesday 14:00 to 15:40; the page, the queue and the sheet must hold it) failed 13 subtests before; `test/test_demo_catalog_coverage.py` compares the hours of the page as a set (a row per office). · Why: the hub's brief of 2026-11-08 (the case the previous entry left open) · Rejected: the managed agenda replacing every own window for every service (it would silently remove bookable hours staff set up for Vacunación-like services; a service saved with an agenda already clears its own doctors' bands tagged with it, `reset_doctor`); keeping a second trimming copy in the slot generator (the drift this closes) · Cost: none; salud gets it with the next release package · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `modules/turnos/doctor_windows.py`, `patient_web/site_availability.py`, `modules/turnos/booking_queue.py`, `modules/turnos/booking_requests.py`, `modules/turnos/booking_capacity.py`, `docs/architecture/admin-cola-y-agenda.md`; `test/test_doctor_page_service_agenda.py`, `test/test_demo_catalog_coverage.py`, `test/golden/site_pages.json`.

Decided: the three controls left on the translucent `--text-2`/`--text-3` scale after the panel labels, menu and tabs moved to `--hint-ink` now take it too, measured on the demo before the change at `color(srgb ... / 0.44)` and `/ 0.5`. The column names of Solicitudes (`.sol-ch`, admin-solicitudes.mjs) sort the list, so the ones not sorted are `--hint-ink` and the sorted one (`.sol-ch-on`) and the hovered one go to `--text`; the sort arrow (`.sol-sar-d`, `.sol-sar-u`) follows the header with `currentColor` instead of its own translucent `--text-2`. The login form's field labels (`.gate-row>label`, admin-gate.css) are `--hint-ink`. The «Resultados / Evolución» tabs of the patient's lab results (`.lr-tab`, account-labs.mjs, public site) take the same `--hint-ink` for the unselected tab (defined opaque in both themes), the selected one keeps `--text` with the accent underline. `test/admin-label-ink.test.mjs` pins `.sol-ch`/`.sol-ch-on` and `.gate-row>label`, and its switcher scan now also treats a selected-state class `X-on` as the twin of `X`; `test/account-labs-tab-ink.test.mjs` pins `.lr-tab`; all fail on the previous CSS. Left as they are, found by the same scan on the public site: the listing chips and sort links (`.lst-chip`, `.lst-sort`, listing.mjs) and the chat widget's thread list (`.stzc-thread`, chat-widget.mjs). · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-08 (d2086915), the last panel controls still read light grey; product owner 2026-11-04: text that names things dark, the translucent scale only for the public site's descriptive small print · Rejected: `--btn2-fg` for the lab tabs (a button token, the tabs are not buttons) · Cost: none; salud gets it with the next release package · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-08 · The public listing's column headers and sort links and the chat widget's conversation list take the dark label ink
Decided: the controls of the public site left on the translucent `--text-2`/`--text-3` scale, measured on the demo before the change at `color(srgb ... / 0.44)` (sort links) and `/ 0.5` (conversation list), now take the opaque `--hint-ink` of tokens.css (defined in both themes) when not selected: the listing's sort links and plain column headers (`.lst-sort`, `.lst-table th`, listing.mjs) and the chat widget's conversation list (`.stzc-thread`, chat-widget.mjs). ## 2026-11-08 · A doctor can approve a request; the request card retries its agenda quietly and closes with a cross in the name row
Decided: the agenda of the open request asks the request's own service when the card has no service picker (`svcDeTarjeta`: `.svc-sel` if present, else `defaultsDe(s).servicio_id`). A doctor's card has no picker, so it asked `servicio_id=` empty, got 400 and its «Aprobar» never turned on: no doctor could approve on the demo. A failed agenda no longer shows a red line with «Reintentar»: the select stays «Cargando horarios…», Aprobar stays off, and `agendaReintentar` asks again with growing pauses (1 s up to 60 s), as Mis agendas and Mis turnos already do. The header reads «Name, 73» (`Format.personaEdad`), the contact as the icons of Pacientes (`Format.contactoIconoHtml`), the DNI alone underneath; the «Volver a la lista» band is gone and the request closes with the Icons8 sf-regular cross at the end of the name row (`.sol-det-x`, 44 px on a phone, the name row sticky on the phone sheet). · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-08 (d2095142), critical review of the panel: F1 (the doctor cannot approve), F7, F8, F13 · Rejected: an error line with a retry button (the desk cannot fix a network blip and reads it as its own fault); a separate back band (one more row before the name on every phone) · Cost: none; salud gets it with the next release package · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.
Source: `admin-app.js`, `static/platform/admin-solicitudes.mjs`, `static/icons/close-sf-regular.svg`; `test/e2e/admin-solicitudes-doctor-approve.spec.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-solicitudes-agenda-race.spec.mjs`, `test/lineage-admin-approve-requested-slot.test.mjs`, `test/admin-solicitudes-direct.test.mjs`.

## 2026-11-08 · One screen header for the panel: create action on the title's line, bare icons, no counters
Decided: every panel screen shares one header rule (end of `admin-direct.css`). The screen's create action (`.adm-title-action`: «Crear turno», «Nuevo usuario», «Nuevo médico/servicio/centro», «Nuevo rol», «Nueva página», «Nueva preparación»…) sits at the right end of the title's own line (`.adm-h1row`) at every width; the title wraps inside its room, never the button. Screen-level icon actions (Solicitudes' «Ver turnos de hoy», Mis turnos' «Agregar a mi calendario», Médicos' «Invitar» and «Cargar lista») are bare Icons8 sf-regular icons named by their tooltip, 44 px targets, no frame at rest; frame and fill on hover, keyboard focus, open or pressed. The list search has no frame at rest either (a magnifier, the frame on hover, focus or once it holds text), and on a phone the list row stays one line (search takes the room left, icons keep theirs); the 44 px row of all 11 lists (a013f7cd7) is unchanged; the phone's filter toggle stays beside the search while the filters are closed. The title's line is 60 px tall on every screen, with or without an action, and the desktop title is 18 px (was 14 px). At compact width «Cerrar sesión» moves into the «Menú» instead of being a second header line; the Reportes switcher is one sideways-scrolling line on a phone. Counters are gone from the claims of the review: Solicitudes' «N pendientes» beside the title and «Pendientes (N)», the Registro de IA pending line, Mis turnos' week stat, the Usabilidad group tabs «Corregidos (N)»… (the story sentence names only the non-zero parts). The Solicitudes verdict column grows to 176 px and the AI's short reading wraps instead of an ellipsis. Pacientes de hoy (/admin/cola) keeps its own copy of the same look in `admin-queue.css`. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-08 (d2095148), critical review of the panel, cross-cutting themes 1-3 (A1, B1, C5, D3, D4, E2, F4, F2; A2, C8, F5; A4, C4); product owner 2026-11-08 on the giant header that wraps, product owner 2026-10-14 «never make counters» · Rejected: a per-screen toolbar row under the title (the second line the product owner named); round icon buttons (he asked for the icon alone, the shape only on hover) · Cost: none; salud gets it with the next release package · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-08 · Panel text is shown whole: names wrap, charts fit their labels, key text is dark
Decided: on the panel screens named by the 08.11 critical review (clipped text, small grey key text), nothing that identifies a row or a value ends in «…» or under an edge at 390 or 1440 px. The shared engines carry it where they can. `admin-charts.mjs` sets every chart label at 13 px in the label ink, measures it and wraps it in two lines when it does not fit; a treemap tile shows a name only when it fits whole, and the names that do not fit are listed under the chart (`.sana-tm-rest`). In `admin-direct.css`, the specialty report's table turns into cards at 720 px and below. The table engine lets the cell that opens a row (`.cnt-td-link`) wrap to a second line instead of ending in an ellipsis; measuring still reads it on one line. The screens keep their own fixes. In Chats, the first question wraps. In Preparations, the synonyms are separate words that wrap, and the full list is kept in the title. In Settings, the network's long name and the greetings are fields as tall as their text, and the network name stays one line: Enter does nothing, pasted line breaks become spaces. The doctor's schedule note is shortened and set in the key ink, in the panel and in the server message. The AI prompt cards set what they do and where in the key ink, in one sentence. The «Pacientes de hoy» queue and the screen header are out of scope (other slices).

## 2026-11-08 · Panel screens: what repeats in every row is said once, no internal names on screen, no subtitles under titles

Decided: the panel screens lose three kinds of small print found by the critical review of 2026-11-08. (1) What every row repeats is said once or not at all: the doctor cards of Médicos on a phone show only the values (hours, next day, occupancy), not the three labels again in every card (the column name stays in the value's tooltip); the Chats «Canal» column appears only when the channels differ (today every chat is «Sitio»); Roles lose the «Activo» column that said «Sí» in every row, only a switched-off role is marked «inactivo»; «Mis agendas» says the length most bands share once («Turnos de 20 min») and each band only a different one (`format.schedule_band(band, common=…)`), a doctor's next consultorio at the same center continues the card instead of repeating «doctor · center», and «pedile a administración…» is said once under the other centers, not after each; the AI register says the model and host, risk and state that most functions share once above the table, a row shows only where it differs (the printable dossier keeps every row whole), and the provider's profile key is not shown. (2) No internal names, keys or environment variables on screen: the AI register and prompt screen read «Cuando alguien sube su orden desde la web…» without module or function names (the source `ai/prompts.REGISTRY` and the catalog say the same, `test_ai_register_i18n`); the cost report names each AI operation as staff call it («Análisis de la orden», «Resumen del paciente», «Lectura de análisis de laboratorio») and an unknown one «Otra operación de IA», never the stored key; Apariencia and Configuración say «la configuración del servidor» instead of `TURNOS_*` names and show the dictation model once; the report assumptions drop the variable names; a doctor's center is shown by name, never by its id. (3) No subtitle or footnote under a title: the reports lose every `rep-sub` line and the three ledes (Médicos, Pacientes, IA); what a subtitle carried moves into the title («Quiénes se registran: sobre todo de 30 a 44 años») or into the chart's «Cómo leerlo» (heatmap: tinted cells without a number are lower pressure); «No se mide» of Recorrido moves into its gear menu; the per-device table skips internal addresses and hides itself under two devices; «Estado» shows one «Dominio» line when the domain is fine. Kept on purpose: every «Cómo leerlo» under a chart (the product owner 2026-09-16) and the AI and dictation warnings (law, decision of 2026-10-14) — only moved. Pacientes de hoy (/admin/cola) and the screen header were not touched (other work). The «3 lecturas» label of the order analysis went with it: the AI reads an order once. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-08 (d2105008), critical review of the panel, cross-cutting themes 7 (B4, C9, C10, D2, D15, F11), 8 (C6, C7, D7, E8, B10) and 12 (C13, D10, E12, E13); product owner 2026-11-02: what repeats on every row goes outside the brackets, no internal names on screen · Rejected: a legend row above every table (a second line the product owner did not ask for); dropping the «Cómo leerlo» texts (his word of 2026-09-16) · Cost: 62 strings per generated language re-translated · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent.

## 2026-11-08 · Report charts: a crowded scatter numbers its points, a phone heatmap keeps 13 px, white text only on near-solid tiles

The chart engine keeps every label whole on a phone. A scatter label is tried on both sides of its point: above it, beside it, one or two steps higher, or under it inside the plot. A name that has no room there (a crowded cluster on a 390 screen) gets a number next to its point and is listed with that number under the chart (`.sana-sc-rest`), instead of ending in an ellipsis or landing on the X axis numbers. On a phone the heatmap narrows its cells (down to 14 px) instead of shrinking the whole drawing, so the hour and day labels stay at 13 px. In the line chart the dates sit 4 px lower so a long first date no longer touches the «0» of the Y axis, and when the two series end close together the second value goes under its point. Text over the accent switches to white only at 0.8 opacity or more (`OP_TEXTO_BLANCO`). On the treemap's middle tier (0.62) and on mid heatmap cells, white measured 2.6 to 2.8:1 on both themes, and dark ink is about 6:1.
Source: `static/platform/admin-charts.mjs`; `test/admin-charts.test.mjs`.

## 2026-11-08 · Panel declutter, second pass: the doctor cards' legend, Estado rows only when wrong

Decided after the interface review of the first pass. On a phone the Médicos cards keep their unlabelled hours, next day and occupancy, and the three column names are said once, on one line over the list, in the cards' order and with the same dot; every other column name and the select-all box stay hidden there. Removing the labels without that line left «40 h · Mañana» and a bar with no meaning. Estado shows HTTPS only when the site does not arrive over HTTPS and the data pack only when it is broken: healthy, they read «Sí» and the pack's folder name. «Mis agendas» states the shared appointment length at 16 px semibold, since it is said nowhere else. The report summary cards drop the sentence on how to read the doctors' curve and «the monthly trend helps anticipate demand»; reading notes stay in «Cómo leerlo» inside each report.
Source: `static/platform/admin-content.mjs`, `admin_panel/system_status.py`, `static/platform/doctor-workspace.css`, `static/platform/admin-reports.mjs`, `locales/es/admin.reports.json`; `test/admin-content-direct.test.mjs`, `test/test_system_status.py`.

## 2026-11-08 · Panel: a doctor's phone tabs carry one word each, and no panel text uses the translucent grey scale

On a phone (900 px and under) a doctor's four screens are one row of tabs. Before this change the row was 488 px wide on a 390 screen, so «Mis agendas» or «Pacientes de hoy» sat past the edge. Each tab now shows one word: Hoy, Turnos, Pacientes, Agendas (`admin.shell.nav.tab.*`, translated). The full name stays the link's `aria-label` and the desktop sidebar's text. The row fits 320 px without scrolling, and Cuenta stays the person icon in the header. On the Account screen «Cerrar sesión» used to join that row and widen it to 601 px; it now closes the screen instead, as the last element of `<main>`. Other roles keep «Menú», where sign-out is still the last item.

The panel draws no text with `--text-2/--text-3/--text-4`. The humanrounds theme mixes them at 38–50 % opacity, which reads as light grey on the demo. 138 `color:` rules in 15 panel files moved to `--hint-ink` (the product owner 2026-11-04: captions in dark grey; product owner 2026-09-16: the translucent scale only for the public site's small print). That covers meta lines, empty states, the crumb, the account's meta, centre ratings, the legal paragraph and Solicitudes' empty states. Left as they are on purpose: placeholders and disabled controls; «Pacientes de hoy» and «Crear turno» (another change in flight); calendar-grid and week, which the public site shares; and backgrounds, borders and fills on the scale.

Why: Product owner said no to a second header line on 2026-11-08, so the tabs are shortened rather than wrapped. Rejected: sideways scrolling (a tab hidden past the edge was the finding itself); hiding a tab behind «Más». Undo: revert the commit.
Source: `templates/admin/base.html`, `admin-direct.css`, `admin-gate.css`, `static/platform/admin-*.mjs` (12), `static/platform/doctor-workspace.css`, `modules/intake/admin-entrevistas.mjs`, `locales/*/admin.shell.json`; `test/admin-panel-ink-scale.test.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-doctor-tabs.spec.mjs`, `test/admin-queue.test.mjs`.

## 2026-11-08 · Stacked catalog cards: contact icons stay on the address line

On a phone or tablet the Servicios, Centros and Médicos rows become cards whose parts flow on one wrapping line with a dot between them. The card rule made every link in a part inline, so it also hit the contact icons (phone, WhatsApp): each icon is a block glyph inside its link, and inline it broke the part into one icon per line above the address, with the dot left hanging beside the first one. The rule now keeps text links inline and leaves the icon links their own tap box (44 px on a phone); the icon pair never splits across lines, and the first icon steps back by its box's inset so the dot stands as far from the phone as from the address. Fixed in the shared card rule of the panel's content module: of the three screens only Centros shows contact icons in its cards today, so only it changes on screen. A part that opens a line already has its dot clipped and an empty part already leaves the card; both are now measured in a real browser at 360, 390 and 834 px.
Source: `static/platform/admin-content.mjs`; `test/admin-catalog-cards-browser.test.mjs`.

## 2026-11-08 · Panel: the AI notice sits with the AI text; switched-off and empty parts are not drawn

Two themes of the 08.11 critical review of the panel. The owner's words: «don't make… little footnotes» (21.10), «if there's no content, why to show all the ui?» (26.10), «Hide the empty tiles…» (05.10).

The AI notice (Decreto 742/2026, `core/ai_register.py`) cannot go, so it moves next to the text it labels instead of being a band over the screen:
- Solicitudes: the clinical notice is the caption of the list whose first column is «Lectura IA», no longer a band above the search. In an open request it is repeated right under the AI verdict, and only when the request has an AI reading, the way the day queue does (`data-ai-notice-inline`, which the disclosure CSS leaves alone).
- Doctor, service and centre forms: the translation notice is the caption right above «Guardar», because saving is what hands the text to the AI. It used to be a band over the whole form. In Médicos it shows only on «Información básica», the one tab with a translated field; a doctor's own session gets none. It also shows on a new form, since saving a new item translates it too. The reviewer asked to drop it there; that was rejected, because the rule is to label AI output wherever it is produced.
- The dictation notice in the shared composer stays before the textarea (decision of 2026-10-14).

Switched-off and empty parts:
- Service form: with «Se saca turno» off, the booking limits are hidden like the schedule. The announcement fields show only while their checkbox is ticked.
- Apariencia: a feature with no key or no agreement is one line: its name, «Sin configurar» and a link to Ajustes › Integraciones. This covers anti-bot without a Cloudflare key, chat dictation without a Fal key, and RENAPER sign-in with neither the simulator nor the IDP credentials. An installation already set to RENAPER keeps both cards, so it can switch back.
  - `GET /api/admin/auth-mode` now answers `renaper_conectado`.
  - The voice status counts the simulated AI as configured, the same rule as the patient's microphone.
  - Ajustes › Integraciones gains a Fal block for `FAL_KEY`.
  - On a phone the state wraps whole under the name, so the name no longer breaks at its hyphen. An empty status line has no height, so it no longer leaves a band of space under each block (ui review).
- Usuarios: the column chooser and «Descargar» follow the filters' threshold (`Filters.filtersWorth`, 10 rows). The search stays.
- Reports (Estadística, Costos de IA, Capacidad):
  - A figure that prints as zero is not drawn: «0 Sin marca todavía», «US$ 0,0000» cost tiles, and a spend row of US$ 0,0000.
  - A table column that is zero on every row is dropped, for example «Sin marca» once every visit is marked.
  - A chart whose every point prints zero is not drawn: consultas per hour, now printed with the table's two decimals through the new `opts.fmtValor` of `Charts.lollipop`, and 14-day occupancy.
  - Consultas per hour also leaves out each point that prints 0,00, and with fewer than two points left there is no chart. The specialty keeps its 0,00 row in the table (ui review).
  - The Solicitudes notice stays above the list. The «Lectura IA» column is data, not the notice the decree asks for, and that notice is already the only one on the screen. The reviewer's request to drop it was rejected.
  - A treemap of one part is not drawn: every report treemap goes through `treemapDeVarios`, which needs two parts with a value. A section whose only body was the treemap goes with it. A section with a title that states the finding, an action or a list keeps those. The 80 px one-tile band of 7cd8d902e stays in the engine for other callers.
  - The one-row device table was already hidden on main.

Why: a control or a figure that cannot say anything costs the reader a look for nothing, and a notice in a band reads as boilerplate about the whole screen instead of a label on the AI text. Rejected: removing the AI notices (the law requires them); greying out off features (the owner wants them not shown at all). Undo: revert the commit.

## 2026-11-08 · Solicitudes: an order is a paperclip, groups by reason, five rows a group, «Contactar»

The owner's words on the pending list: it keeps saying «no order» on every row, while nobody writes «no attachment» in a mail client; the page is too long by default; the hospital asked for approve, reject and a way to keep talking to the patient; no dividers. The hospital's technical counterpart had described the old workflow as reviewing the list and contacting the people.

- A pending row no longer says «Sin orden adjunta» or the reason. A request with an order carries the Icons8 paperclip (`chat-attach-sf-regular.svg` as a mask in the text colour); one without carries nothing. The grouped queue has no AI-reading column at all: the name sits by the checkbox and the paperclip follows the name.
- Groups by meaning, no repetition: «Aprobar según sugerencia», then one «Revisar a mano · <reason>» group per review reason and one group per rejection reason, the reason in the title. «Rechazar todas» acts only on its own reason, and its confirmation quotes the exact text the patients will get by email.
- Each group shows five rows and the open request, and loads twenty more inside itself. On the demo the admin's phone page went from 5181 px to 1571 px.
- No divider lines between rows or groups; spacing separates them.
- The open request has «Contactar» under Aprobar and Rechazar: WhatsApp, mail or phone, with a text that names the requested service. The product has no staff-patient thread (decision of 2026-10-14), so this is the closest thing to «keep talking».
- Under the request's sheet on a phone the page behind it does not scroll.
- The AI notice (Decreto 742/2026) stays where it was.

One screen changed: Solicitudes draws its own list (`admin-solicitudes.mjs`), not the panel table engine. Rejected: a reason chip on every row (repeats the group title; in a narrow track «Dudosa» broke letter by letter); collapsing whole groups by default (the desk would not see that a group exists); a chat thread with the patient (not in the product, a separate decision). Undo: revert the commit.

## 2026-11-08 · Panel: one button system; lists and rows of figures instead of boxes

The panel review of 2026-11-08 found buttons of six heights and three looks, and boxes around things that group nothing (owner: «same font size and same design», «way less bounding boxes»).

- Buttons:
  - Every panel button is one height: 36 px under a mouse and 44 px on touch, from the shared tokens in `admin-direct.css`.
  - The screens' own secondary buttons share one rule: a border, no fill, the fill on hover. They are the hours editor's add, Ajustes' small buttons, Mis agendas' add/copy and the holidays toggle.
  - The modules' private sizes are removed: Solicitudes' 28 px group buttons and framed filter toggle, the 40 px workspace buttons, and the 48/40 px Guardar/Deshabilitar of the catalog cards.
  - The portrait and «Cargar lista» file pickers take the panel's height and their own width.
  - «Quitar centro» stays a text control.
  - A form's actions stay one row on a phone («Revisar lista · Cancelar»), wrapping only when they don't fit.
  - Icon-only toggles (the filter beside a list's search) stay bare icons with the frame on hover and focus, the panel's rule from the product owner's R3.
- Apariencia:
  - The logo is picked with the panel's button («Elegir archivo», the label the doctors' import already used) over a hidden input, not the browser's English «Choose File».
  - «Logo» is a label over its row, like «Color» and «Código»: the preview, then the button centred on it.
  - The dictation block says only that dictation is on. The model name of the speech service is a service detail, not a setting on that screen.
- Reports:
  - The figures (`.rep-kpi`) are a row of numbers without a box each.
  - Centros is a column of text per center: its name, then its quotes, with no card, no grey band and no second decorative quotation mark (the quote text carries its own).
- Mis agendas:
  - The admin with more than one doctor sees one table (Médico, Centro, Horario; one row per window, the doctor named once). Each name links to the doctor's card.
  - A doctor keeps the cards (decision of 2026-10-27).

Not touched: Pacientes de hoy and Mis turnos (another change in flight), the screen header, the AI notice (the law requires it) and the dictation warning.

Why: one control that does one job looks the same everywhere, and a frame is earned only by something that groups. Rejected: 44 px buttons at every width. It is a touch rule, and under a mouse it made the forms twice as tall as their fields. Undo: revert the commit.

Decided:

The calendar's card shows the paper first like the queue's, and with no paper it is one column with no «Solicitud sin orden adjunta» line. The one-column rule now comes after the two-column one; before, the queue's card without a referral kept an empty 340 px column.

- Storybook: the «Médicos por especialidad» story asked for three demo portraits renamed in 475ae1541 (three 404s in the console). It now uses existing synthetic faces, and a test checks that every picture a story names exists.

## 2026-11-09 · Reports: one name and one count for each step of the booking path; one search text for Solicitudes and Pacientes

Decided:
- The Gestión funnel, its hero sentence and its daily series take the steps of Recorrido with their names and numbers («Pidieron turno», «Les dieron turno», «Fueron al turno»…) from one call, `reports_funnel.summary`; the funnel's own labels («Se aprobaron», «Se presentaron», «Total pedidas») and its own loss sentences are gone, the Recorrido sentences about where requests stay behind are shown under the funnel instead. The percentage under a step names the step it is a share of («75% de «Pidieron turno»»).
- «Les dieron turno» is one definition (`db_reports._ever_approved`): approved now, or approved or restored at some point; an approval later cancelled still counts. Recorrido, the Gestión funnel, the hero's «resolvió N» and the daily line use it; before, the daily line counted only the current status and gave a smaller number than Recorrido for the same period.
- Recorrido's hero speaks of requests («10 solicitudes en el período»), as its steps count requests, not patients.
- The overview tile «Pacientes» and the first number of the Pacientes report are one figure with one name (`figuraPacientes`): patients who asked for an appointment in the period when there is activity, else registered accounts; the report shows «Cuentas registradas» after it only when the first figure is not that. Before, the tile said 68 and the report opened on 117 under another name.
- Solicitudes and Pacientes share the search text «Paciente o médico» (one key, `admin.queue.filters.buscar_placeholder`, the one Pacientes de hoy uses): all three find a patient by DNI or name and by the doctor (Solicitudes by the assigned doctor, Pacientes through `q=` since the product owner's word of 2026-11-02). The two keys they had are dropped from the ten languages.
- The wiki's golden-question marks are the Icons8 «ok» / «cancel» pair (a check and a cross in a circle, `golden-ok`, `golden-fail`): the plain cross is the close button's and only `.hr-x` draws it (`test/close-icon.test.mjs`).
- Gestión leaves out Recorrido's advice to read the rejection reasons in Gestión when rejection is the biggest loss: the reader is already there.
- The photo zoom's previous/next arrows and the wiki's open/closed arrow are Icons8 masks (`dir-card-arrow`, `expand-arrow`, sf-regular) in 44 px buttons, no Unicode glyphs; the golden marks are named for screen readers («Encuentra su artículo» / «No encuentra su artículo»). «Cerrar foto» on the doctor page goes through i18n (`doctor.close_photo`).
- Left as they are: the attendance names of Recupero and Estadística («Se presentaron» / «No se presentaron»: attendance of covered consultations, another population), and the Gestión KPI «Aprobadas %» (share of decided requests, another measure).
- `test_availability_pages` frozen bytes re-pinned: 2b0d35329 had moved the photo close button onto `.hr-x` without updating them (red on main).
- Code review of the commit (high, 09.11): the share under the first step is printed against «Eligieron un horario» only when that step is «Pidieron turno» (the request a hold became); with the steps reordered or «Pidieron turno» off the two populations are not nested and no share is printed there. «Cerrar foto» reads the catalog in the page's own language (`es`): the doctor page is a fixed-Spanish document, and one control announcing itself in the visitor's language would switch the screen reader's voice mid-page. Left as they are: the hero's «resolvió N» keeps counting every request ever given a turno whatever steps are shown (one number, one source — a step turned off hides the echo, not the fact), and the Gestión payload keeps its documented counts (`aprobadas` current status vs `con_turno` ever-approved, disambiguated in `reports.py`'s docstring; the store computes them for the KPIs anyway).

Why: critical review 08.11, theme 4 (E7, A11) and R17: The product owner saw the same thing named two ways and two numbers for one tile.
Undo: revert the commit.
