## 2026-11-10 · Sentry on the two test sites: the turnos-salud project, one environment per site, all three launch signals
Decided: `pinamar.humanrounds.org` and `demo.humanrounds.org` now send to the same Sentry project as the municipal installation (turnos-salud) with `TURNOS_SENTRY_SIGNALS=failures,digest,crons` and a DSN set in each Coolify app. The environment tag keeps them apart: `TURNOS_SENTRY_ENV=pinamar-test` and `demo`, while the municipal installation reports as `salud` (read from the project's own events and environment list on 2026-11-10; the code default is `production`, which none of the three uses). The values were set on the apps only: Coolify copies a new variable to the PR-preview list on its own, and those three copies per app were deleted the same day, so a preview never reports; after an environment rebuild, check that no `TURNOS_SENTRY_*` variable is flagged preview. A failure code, the daily digest and the Crons check-ins of a test site are read by filtering the environment; an alert on a missed check-in of `turnos-worker` must be scoped to the `salud` environment or it fires for the test sites too. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-10, the reference deployment's technical hub proposed the three signals; the test sites run the same code and worker as the municipal installation, so a silent failure there (mail, backups, the HSI chain) shows before it reaches a release package · Rejected: a second Sentry project (a new DSN and a second place to read, nothing the environment tag does not already separate); leaving `production` as the environment (it would mix with a real tag later); `all` (same three features today, but a fourth feature would switch on unseen) · Cost: the demo's daily regeneration and the test sites' own noise land in the project under their own environments · Undo: delete the three variables from each Coolify app and redeploy; the events stay under their environments · By: agent
Source: `core/error_tracking.py` (`init`, `TURNOS_SENTRY_ENV`), `core/launch_signals.py` (`TURNOS_SENTRY_SIGNALS`, `FEATURES`); Coolify applications `sana-turnos` and `sana-turnos-demo`.

## 2026-11-10 · Sentry on the two test sites: switched off again, the municipality's alert rules have no environment filter
Decided: `pinamar.humanrounds.org` and `demo.humanrounds.org` send nothing to Sentry again: `TURNOS_SENTRY_DSN`, `TURNOS_SENTRY_SIGNALS` and `TURNOS_SENTRY_ENV` are deleted from both Coolify apps (no preview copies either) and both were redeployed; the containers report no DSN and log no Sentry line (`core/error_tracking.py` is inert without a DSN). This reverses the entry above of the same day. Read-only look at the project's alerting that morning: five rules, none filtered by environment (the new alert system: four on the project's issue stream, one org-wide for Seer pull requests), three of them mail the provider team and one mails issue owners, falling back to all active members; the first-seen/regression rule and the high-priority rule fired at 04:05 ART on the two test issues the first run emitted (TURNOS-SALUD-5 and -6, now resolved). The three cron monitors (`turnos-worker`, `turnos-waitlist`, `turnos-reminders`) were created by our own check-ins and carried only the `pinamar-test` and `demo` environments (nothing from `salud`); each has a failure detector with no workflow of its own, so a missed check-in becomes an issue in the project's stream and hits those same rules. Their `pinamar-test` and `demo` environments were deleted (the monitors stay; the municipal installation re-creates its own environment when it sends check-ins). No rule or monitor setting of theirs was changed. · Why: pinamar-turnos 2026-11-10: the test sites must not wake the municipality's people, and the rules are theirs to edit, not ours; a site that stops sending without its monitor environment removed would also raise missed-check-in incidents · Rejected: keeping `failures` only (a first-seen error on a test site still mails the team); asking the municipality to add an environment filter first (leaves the test sites noisy until they act, and the rules are not ours to edit); muting the monitor environments (a mute is a setting on their monitor and the sites would keep sending) · Cost: no Sentry signal from the test sites (their logs and the demo's own checks remain); to bring it back, the municipality's rules must filter on the `salud` environment first, then re-add the three variables and re-check the alert list · Undo: set the three variables again on each app and redeploy; monitor environments reappear with the first check-in · By: agent
Source: `core/error_tracking.py`, `core/launch_signals.py`; Coolify applications `sana-turnos` and `sana-turnos-demo`; the project's alert rules and monitors read through the Sentry API.

## 2026-11-10 · Booking wizard, Confirmá: «Enviar solicitud» on the phone's first screen, no calendar before sending, «Mañana» carries its date
Decided: the Confirmá step no longer shows «Agregar al calendario» (the Google Calendar link and the .ics built in the browser from the chosen slot); the success screen keeps its link next to «Fecha y hora» to the saved request's file (`/api/appointment/<id>.ics`) and «Mis turnos» keeps its own. With that line gone «Enviar solicitud» ends inside the first 390 x 844 screen. The day a patient reads is «Mañana, miércoles 11» instead of a bare «Mañana»: one new formatter, `dates.diaFechado` (used by `amigable`, so every surface that writes «day, hour» through it changes at once: wizard, account requests, desk create-appointment, patients list), mirrored by `site_schedule._friendly_date`, one new catalog key `date.tomorrow_dated` («Mañana, {day_lower} {n}» / «Tomorrow, {day} {n}»). `dates.dia` keeps returning the bare word, because listing, queue, my-appointments and content screens compare it with `date.tomorrow` or cut its first word. The send button's top margin moved from an inline style to `.wz-cta-send`. · Why: plan of the patient's main path 2026-11-10: on a phone the button sat below the fold (bottom at 846 of 844), and an event offered before the request exists is a promise the hospital has not made yet; «Mañana, 08:00» reads as «in the morning» · Rejected: keeping the calendar line folded under a disclosure (still a step before the button, still nothing to schedule); shrinking the big date (it is what the person came to confirm); changing `dia` itself (breaks the comparisons named above) · Cost: the Google Calendar one-click link exists nowhere now, only the .ics file after sending; catalog keys `service.calendar.*` stay unused until a later cleanup · Undo: restore `calendarioHtml` and its CSS from the parent commit and the e2e it pinned; point `amigable` back at `dia` · By: agent

## 2026-11-10 · Pre-consult interview: an appointment with no known doctor is never invited
Decided: the interview is offered only when the appointment has a known doctor (the assigned one, else the doctor of the block the patient chose) and that doctor's effective switch is on. With no doctor, or an id the catalog does not have, there is no offer whatever `INTAKE_ENABLED` says: no button after booking or in the account, no line in the received and approved emails, no day-before reminder. The rule lives in the one hook every surface asks (`interview_offer_for_request`). For an approved appointment the assigned doctor decides (emails, reminder, and now the answer of an automatic confirmation, which used to ask about the id the wizard sent); for a request still pending, the doctor of the chosen block. `INTAKE_ENABLED` keeps its one meaning: the value a doctor without an override inherits. The page `/entrevista/<id>` is unchanged: an interview already started stays reachable, and nothing links to the page once the offer is gone. · Why: the reference deployment reported the confirmation email inviting patients to the interview while it was switched off for every professional. The panel shows one checkbox per doctor and no control for the system default (`POST .../settings {"sistema"}` has no caller in the front end), so an administrator who unticks every doctor cannot switch the fallback off; and an interview that belongs to no doctor is shown on no screen (`interview_state_for_day` skips groups without a doctor) · Rejected: keeping the system default for doctor-less requests and exposing the switch in the panel (two switches for one feature, the rule the single checkbox removed); offering when some doctor of the service has it on (the invitation would still be read by nobody unless that doctor is the one assigned); changing `intake_admin.enabled_for(None)` itself (the QR door, the queue columns and the doctor's own settings call it with a doctor and rely on the inherit rule) · Cost: on an installation with the system default on, a pending request with no chosen doctor is invited only once a doctor is assigned (the approved email and the day-before reminder), not in the received email · Undo: revert the commit · By: agent
Source: `modules/intake/hooks.py` (`interview_offer_for_request`), `modules/turnos/booking_create.py` (`_step_auto_confirmation`), `notifications/send.py` (`_entrevista_cta`); tests `test/test_intake_settings.py`, `test/test_create_request_steps.py`.

## 2026-11-10 · HSI visible in the panel: the state is always named, `/admin/hsi` never redirects
Decided: slice 7 of «HSI is visible in the panel» deliberately changes the HSI-off contract of 13a5a46ff (review 03.11: «off — no redirect to a real screen, no HSI anywhere»): with the server-side push off, the panel still names HOW a turno reaches HSI here, honestly. One reading for the three places — `admin_panel/hsi_status.py`, `snapshot()`: `servidor` (the HSI connector is selected and the push is not skipped), `extension` (the server does not send and the desk's browser extension is the path on this site: there is something to install, or the desk's «Cargar en HSI» buttons are drawn — `hsi_extension.install()` or `store_url()`; availability of the path, not proof that a receptionist has it running right now) or `apagado` (neither), each with its reason as one whole sentence (never a translated half glued to another), plus every center's institution number in HSI. «Sistema» shows it as the HSI card's first row («Modo») with the centers' row under it and, only in server mode, the journal rows (the last transmission — only what HSI actually received, never an item the journal closed as skipped or superseded — as an instant with the installation's offset, which the client renders «hace N minutos» while fresh and from six hours on as the installation's day and hour, never the reader's clock: `dates.mjs` `instanteServidor`; then queue and errors, from one reading of the journal, `hsi_status.read_journal()`, shared with «Conectores») after the existing probe; the card is always there, connector selected or not — only a selected Enterprise bridge replaces it with its own card (previously the card vanished without a selected HSI connector). «Conectores» shows the same sentence above its switch, with the journal line (server mode only) and a same-site link to `/admin/hsi` with the centers' summary. `/admin/hsi` shows the state as the screen when there is no connector (title, reason, the centers' table: a center without its number is «Sin número», marked with the warning dot only in extension mode — the extension is what reads it; the server-side push sends to ONE institution per installation until slice 11) and never asks the closed journal API; `GET /api/admin/system/hsi` (solo-admin, no-store) serves the same snapshot. The center form's «Institución en HSI» accepts an ASCII number of 1-9 digits, not all zeros (Unicode digits that pass `str.isdigit()` are refused), stored without leading zeros, and a refused save lands at the field, not in a toast; empty stays allowed and only marked in the state surfaces. Duplicates of a number between centers are NOT rejected yet: the hospital counterpart has not answered whether two centers may share an institution number. Read-only by construction: the registry, two settings, the extension's files and the catalog; no socket without the server push, no write anywhere.
Why: pinamar-turnos slice 7 (2026-11-10): «HSI apagado» reading as a broken sync is a false alarm — the extension is the path in use on the test sites today; the panel never shows a dead end without naming the way out.
Rejected: keeping the redirect and the hidden card (the old contract hid a working installation path); warning about a missing center number in server/off modes too (the server does not read it; slice 11 decides); rejecting duplicate numbers now (waits for the counterpart's answer).
Cost: «Sistema» on the test sites shows the HSI card yellow while centers lack their number (honest, actionable); in the rare «connector selected but skip» configuration `/admin/hsi` shows both the mode title and the journal hero.
Undo: revert the commit; the snapshot API, the card rows and the screen state go back to the 13a5a46ff contract.
Source: `admin_panel/hsi_status.py` (new), `admin_panel/system_status.py`, `admin_panel/system_status_router.py`, `admin_panel/settings_admin.py`, `admin_panel/admin_hsi_screen.py`, `patient_web/catalog.py`, `core/db_hsi.py` (`hsi_last_confirmed`), `core/permissions.py`, `static/platform/admin-hsi.mjs`, `static/platform/admin-system.mjs`, `static/platform/admin-settings.mjs`, `static/platform/admin-content.mjs`, `static/platform/dates.mjs` (`instanteServidor`), `admin-direct.css`, `locales/`; tests `test/test_system_status.py`, `test/test_admin_hsi_screen.py`, `test/test_settings_admin.py`, `test/test_catalog.py`, `test/test_db_hsi_store.py`, `test/admin-hsi-lifecycle.test.mjs`, `test/admin-settings-ui.test.mjs`, `test/e2e/hsi-disabled.spec.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-forms-audit-catalog.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-10 · Demo: the walkthrough scenario lives all day, not only at seed time

Decided: the demo's showcase state is kept alive by a five-minute keep-alive (`demo_refresh._mantener_escenarios`, run on every tick of the daemon, not part of the refresh ledger), which re-asserts two things. (1) The demo's own catalog overlay (`seed_demo_all._asegurar_catalogo_demo`): the walkthrough service `medicina-general-riverside-clinic` is open every day 08:00–20:00 at 20-minute slots (a catalog-overlay row, never an edit of the generated `packs/example-town` pack — the overlay doc is rebuilt from the pack's current base item on every call, so a pack change flows through), and its doctor `gonzalez-juan` carries `entrevista_habilitada: true`, which turns the symptom-capture block on for the demo doctor only (`intake_admin.enabled_for` reads the per-doctor override); this is also what made `/admin/consult` open for him (`t.entrevista` needs the intake object attached). (2) Sofía Herrera's same-day appointment with the demo doctor stays approved and ahead of the clock (`_asegurar_turno_destacado`): a lapsed slot moves to the doctor's next free agenda slot of the same day, and once the day's last slot has passed the appointment stays where it was, approved, on today's list (the doctor lands on «Hoy», and «Iniciar consulta» does not depend on the hour) until the midnight reseed — never tomorrow's list and never a slot outside the agenda, which the 2026-11-08 decision keeps off the demo; a seed run after the last slot gives her the day's last free slot the same way; a cancellation from the patient demo is restored on its own slot within one tick (the next free one only when somebody took it), each row in its own transaction, and her appointment with another service comes back only while its slot is still free — the patient session is the only one that writes in demo mode, so the keep-alive's plain marker-scoped UPDATE is the seed re-asserting its own fictional rows, the same write style `seed()` itself uses (no `appointment_events`; the seed's rows never had them). Her second, days-out approved appointment with cardiology keeps «Mis turnos» non-empty whatever a visitor cancels. The one-click doctor entry (`/admin/demo-doctor-access`) now lands on `admin_navigation.default_path_for_role(rol)` — `/admin/cola` («Hoy») for a doctor, the same screen a real doctor's login lands on. A row that carries a sample order image now belongs to the person **on the image** (`_ORDEN_HOLDERS`): row, AI reading and patient account name the same person, `dni` in the reading is the DNI the image prints (None for the images that print none — the reading then closes on the name with the real reader's phrase «el nombre del paciente coincide»), so the referral card never shows one name beside another person's picture; wrong-document rows keep a regular patient, as the image is an order about nobody. Two images name the seed's own first two patients: each is ONE person with that patient (same DNI and account, the patient takes the birth date and sex the image prints), so «Pacientes» never lists the same name twice with one email. The overlay's `horario` is «Lunes a domingo de 8 a 20 h»: the field is parsed (`hours.mjs`), and «Todos los días…» parsed to no opening hours. A failed overlay write does not stop `seed()`, a seed that cannot be imported does not stop the daemon, and the doctor entry falls back to `/admin/account` when no screen is open to the role. · Why: plan slice 1 of «the demo shows the scenarios all day» (hr-core-1110, 2026-11-10): the walkthrough appointments existed only until the clock passed them or a visitor cancelled them, the demo doctor worked weekdays only (a Sunday or late-evening demo had no «Iniciar consulta»), and the referral card paired each picture with a stranger's name · Rejected: editing the generated `packs/example-town` docs by hand (the header says NO editar a mano, and the pack is an e2e fixture); a second demo doctor pool (the service's pool is three general-medicine doctors, all moved to the same every-day agenda — acceptable for a sandbox); freezing the overlay row once written (a pack change would never reach the demo); moving an appointment through `db_booking` helpers (`reassign_appointment` demands a move event the seed's rows never had); printing the order images' own dates (2024–2026 dates would read every order as «vencida» — the 90-day freshness the panel shows needs a fresh computed date; matching the printed dates means regenerating the images, left as a separate slice); overflow slots after 20:00 so her appointment is always ahead of the clock (rows outside the doctor's hours read as a bug on the day sheet); moving the lapsed appointment to tomorrow's first slot (the evening «Hoy» lost her card) · Cost: the demo's own overlay makes the walkthrough service's three doctors work every day 8–20 (demo only, never salud); the keep-alive adds two small DB reads per five minutes · Undo: revert the commit; the overlay rows remain in the catalog until deleted · By: agent
Source: `scripts/seed_demo_all.py` (`_ORDEN_HOLDERS`, `_asegurar_catalogo_demo`, `_slot_libre_destacado`, `_asegurar_turno_destacado`, `_turno_destacado`, `_campos_orden`, `_seed_patient_accounts`, `_seed_featured_patient`), `ops/demo_refresh.py` (`_mantener_escenarios`, `_loop`), `ops/demo_installation_router.py` (`get_admin_demo_doctor_access`), `docs/demo-installation.md`; tests `test/test_seed_demo_store.py` (`TestOrderHolders`, `TestDemoCatalogOverlay`, `TestFeaturedAllDay`, Sunday seeds), `test/test_demo_refresh_completion.py` (keep-alive safety), `test/test_demo_installation.py` (doctor entry lands on «Hoy»), `test/demo-walkin-hours.test.mjs` (the overlay's hours parse as a whole week).

## 2026-11-10 · Panel: one left edge for the Solicitudes list, and the phone-width regressions of the 42f8ddc32 round repaired

Decided:
- The Solicitudes list keeps one left edge with the screen title and the search's magnifier: the list's own side padding is the panel gutter (`--adm-gutter`, 16px) at every width (before: 14px at desktop, 12px under 760px), and what each row's first 44px slot draws sits on it — the row's checkbox (`justify-content:flex-start` in both `admin-solicitudes.mjs` and `admin-direct.css`; before it floated centred 4px in) and the group header's arrow (its 24px button reaches 8px outside so the arrow itself starts on the edge). The group header's first slot is the same 44px the rows use (was 24px, so the group title stood 20px left of every patient name, against the product owner's 2026-08-06 rule the comment itself cites). The group title now ellipsizes before the group's «Aprobar todas» button on a phone (nowrap without a clip overlapped the button at 390/360).
- The wiki's `.kn-toggle` arrow sits on the left edge in a 44px touch zone that no longer leaves a 360px screen (42f8ddc32 had given it `margin-left:-24px`): the negative margin is `calc(-1 * var(--adm-gutter,16px))` with `justify-content:flex-start`, so the icon starts on the edge and the button's far side stays inside the screen.
- A panel heading keeps room for its longest word: `.adm-h1row>.adm-h1:has(~ .adm-title-action)` is `min-width:min-content` (was `0`, which squeezed «Crear turno» to 46px beside the triage bell at 360).
- Test contract, `admin-phone-gutter`: a control with no frame and no fill is measured by what it draws — the lists' search by its magnifier, an icon button by its contents — while its box may still reach the screen edge but never leave it; and a line cut by a strip that really scrolls sideways (the reports' tab bar) is not an edge finding: the cut says there is more, a swipe brings the item in whole.
- Test contract, `admin-left-edge`: an icon drawn as a CSS mask over an empty element is a mark (the wiki tree's arrow); the line of column names above a list's rows (a table's `thead`, the Solicitudes `.sol-cols`) is a header line, not content — the first row's own first cell is the content edge.

Why: 12 red test files on a clean main (m2289183): four phone-layout specs (admin-phone-gutter, admin-left-edge, admin-shell, admin-screen) against real regressions of the 42f8ddc32 round, plus stale expectations (frozen bytes, golden pages, journey flows) that the ddafa07d8/2b0d35329 rounds had moved.
Undo: revert the commit.
Source: `static/platform/admin-solicitudes.mjs`, `static/platform/admin-knowledge.mjs`, `admin-direct.css`; tests `test/e2e/admin-phone-gutter.spec.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-left-edge.spec.mjs`, `test/admin-knowledge-direct.test.mjs`, and the stale-expectation repins (`test/test_availability_pages_steps.py`, `test/golden/admin_screen_pages.json`, `test/test_chat_degraded.py`, `test/e2e/admin-forms-roundtrip.spec.mjs`, `test/e2e/journey-j16-referral-approval-reminder.spec.mjs`, `test/e2e/journey-j19-new-patient-signup-booking.spec.mjs`, `test/e2e/journey-j20-book-for-a-child.spec.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-solicitudes-detail-in-view.spec.mjs`).

## 2026-11-10 · Reports: one word, one measure — resolution hours of those that waited, the request-to-appointment span named for itself, unmarked attendance as the desk's action
Decided:
- «Horas hasta resolver» is the median over requests that actually waited for a decision: requests born approved (`resolved_by` starting `mesa:`, `medico:` or `auto:` — the turno is created approved, nobody resolved anything) are left out. On data where every same-instant approval counted, the tile read «0,0 h»; its label now says what it counts, «Horas hasta resolver (mediana de las que esperaron)» — not «de las resueltas», the words of the tile next to it («Aprobadas, de las resueltas», which counts every decided request, the ones born approved too): two tiles never share words over different sets. The three spans of Gestión are three names now: hours to resolve (of those that waited), days from request to appointment («Días del pedido al turno (mediana)», the tile and the hero's fallback phrase «Del pedido al turno pasaron {actual} días (mediana)»), and how long the pending ones have waited («La mitad de las {n} solicitudes pendientes lleva ese tiempo o más sin resolver.»); «espera» names only the lista de espera, and «Aprobadas, de las resueltas» says its denominator. Vocabulary: resolving a request is «resolver / resueltas / sin resolver» (as the hero «El equipo resolvió»); «respuesta» stays with the patient confirming, never the desk deciding.
- The Recorrido step to the turno (`turno_paso`) counts only requests approved now: a request that had a turno and was rejected afterwards never arrived at it. Before, the step counted it as arrived, so the loss to the next step came out smaller than its own reasons.
- Unmarked attendance is an action, not a statistic: the biggest unmeasured block of «Fueron al turno» (turnos past their hour with no attendance marked) now carries a phrase naming the screen where it is marked, «La asistencia se marca en {pantalla}: hasta entonces no se sabe si fueron.», with that screen's name as a link inside the sentence (server: `_ACCIONES`/`_PANTALLAS` in `reports_funnel.py`, always sent with the phrase, together with `motivo`, the reason's key, so «already said» is told by the reason and not by translated words; client: `fraseAbrible` drops the link when `#adm-nav` has no such href, so the reports-only role reads the same sentence with no link to a screen that would refuse it). It appears once — in the hero when it is the biggest loss, else under the Gestión funnel and next to the Recorrido step's losses. `Charts.frase` renders the sentence with the screen's name as the link (only `/admin/…` hrefs, only names that are part of the sentence); `Charts.hero` keeps a linked sentence one flex item.
- The demo seed's today-queue rows resolve 1–5 h after asking (was: the same instant), like the history rows' 3 h: the demo's «Horas hasta resolver» tile shows a plausible median instead of «0,0 h».
- On a phone (640px and below) a report page no longer draws the list of the other reports under the breadcrumb: eleven names never fit one row, the row scrolled sideways with its end cut by the right edge (00f657794), and wrapped it was three or four lines above the report. The breadcrumb's «Reportes» leads to the directory and gets a 44px target there; wider screens keep the list. Considered and not taken: one word per report (still two or three rows in ten languages) and a disclosure or select for the list (one more control above every report).
- Left as they are: «Aprobadas %» (share of decided requests — another measure, decision of 2026-11-09), the «Cómo leerlo» lines (the product owner's word, 2026-09-16) and the hero's «El equipo resolvió N».
Why: critical review 08.11, R42/R43 — one word for one measure; a tile that prints «0,0 h» without saying it counts the wrong population reads as broken, and unmarked attendance was a dead number. The phone list: nothing sits past the edge (the doctor's tabs, 1edf140a7, same rule).
Undo: revert the commit.
Source: `admin_panel/reports.py`, `admin_panel/reports_funnel.py`, `core/db_reports.py`, `scripts/seed_demo_all.py`, `static/platform/admin-charts.mjs`, `static/platform/admin-reports.mjs`, `admin-direct.css`, `locales/`; tests `test/test_reports_funnel.py`, `test/test_db_reports_store.py`, `test/admin-charts.test.mjs`, `test/admin-reports-disclosure.test.mjs`, `test/admin-reports-lifecycle.test.mjs`, `test/admin-clip-rules.test.mjs`.

## 2026-11-10 · Chat: the booking proposal says the day as its own sentence
Decided: the chat's booking proposal (`ai.booking.proposal`) takes one `{when}` from the shared formatter `site_schedule._friendly_date(day, hour)` as its own sentence: «¿Confirmás la solicitud para Sofía Herrera, Cardiología? Mañana, miércoles 11, 09:00. Respondé Confirmar.». The template no longer wraps `{date}` and `{time}` in «el … a las …», which read «el Hoy» / «el Mañana, sábado 11 a las 09:00». es and en by hand, the other eight regenerated for this key only.
Why: every shape the formatter returns (today, tomorrow with its weekday, a date this year, a date with its year) starts with a capital and carries its own hour, so only a sentence of its own reads right in every case.
Rejected: lowercasing the formatter's output to keep the article (the long dates would still need «el» and today/tomorrow would not).
Undo: revert the commit.
Source: `modules/chat/chat_tools.py` (`_tool_request`), `locales/*/ai.json`; test `test/test_chat_booking_proposal_when.py`.

## 2026-11-10 · Chat: the patient confirms a booking with the word of their own language
Decided: the chat's transport-only confirmation (`chat_tools.confirm_booking_reply`) accepts the word the proposal asked for in the patient's language: a new catalog key `ai.booking.confirm_word` (es «Confirmar», en «Confirm» by hand; the other eight generated for this key only — ru, uk, ja, zh, de, fr, it, pt). The proposal prints that same word, in quotes, through `{word}` (es «Respondé «Confirmar».», en «Reply “Confirm”.»; `ai.booking.proposal` regenerated for that key only), so the word asked for and the word accepted can never drift apart: the earlier Japanese proposal asked for «確定» while a standalone translation said «確定する». The proposal is written in the conversation's language (chat_agent's turn language, passed as `ToolInvocation.language`; the date too), not the page's — before, a Russian conversation on a Spanish page or on WhatsApp got a Spanish proposal. It stores that language (`lang`) and the reply is matched in it; a proposal stored before this change falls back to the conversation's language, then the request's. The match is case-, accent- and space-insensitive and trims punctuation, spaces and quotes at both ends («», 「」, “”, «!», «。») without folding to ASCII, so Cyrillic and CJK replies survive (`ascii_words` deleted them, which is why a Russian or Japanese patient could never confirm, and why «confirm» plus a refusal in another script read as «confirm»). The Spanish and English words that were always accepted (confirmar, confirmo, si confirmo, confirm, yes confirm) stay accepted in every language, through the same fold. Anything else — a bare «yes» in the patient's language, the word plus more text or a refusal in another script, the word plus an emoji («Confirmar ❌»; an emoji is no longer dropped), «ok» — still retires the proposal without booking.
Why: in seven of the ten catalog languages the proposal asked the patient to answer with a word the check never accepted, so a chat booking in those languages could not be confirmed.
Rejected: a list of synonyms per language (the confirmation must be one explicit word the patient was shown, not an interpretation); folding to ASCII (deletes non-Latin scripts).
Undo: revert the commit.
Source: `modules/chat/chat_tools.py` (`_is_confirm_reply`, `_confirm_fold`, `confirm_booking_reply`, `_tool_request`, `ToolInvocation.language`), `modules/chat/chat_agent.py` (`_run_tool_call`, `_answer_tool_call`, `_run_turn`), `locales/*/ai.json`, `locales/*/_source_hash.json`; tests `test/test_chat_confirm_words.py`, `test/test_chat_booking_proposal_when.py`, `test/test_chat_agent_steps.py`.

## 2026-11-10 · Center form: optional number fields say «opcional», not a sample number

Decided: in the panel's center form, «Institución en HSI» and «Coordenadas» no longer show a gray sample (`1086`, `-37.1009, -56.8731`) when empty; they show «opcional», like «Zona» and «Nota de ubicación» in the same form (`admin.content.catalog.common.optional_placeholder`). The field's error text keeps its example («Poné un número, por ejemplo 1086.»): there it is an instruction, not a value. Patient forms keep their format hints (DNI, phone, postal code). The coordinates' sample was also their only format hint, and the form silently dropped anything that was not «lat, lng» — saving `-37,1009, -56,8731` erased the stored pair. Now four comma-separated parts are read as two numbers with a decimal comma (the es-AR decimal keypad types commas); any other non-blank shape goes to the server as typed, which refuses it (not two numbers, or out of range), and the refusal lands at the field with the format example («Pegalas como las da Google Maps, por ejemplo -37.1009, -56.8731.»); blank still clears the pair. The server stays the one check — the client does not duplicate it. «Institución en HSI» is 18ch wide so «opcional» fits whole in the longest generated language (ru/uk, 13 letters, plus the input's padding). Not done: a swapped pair (lng, lat) inside both ranges is still accepted.
Why: on a screenshot of an empty center the gray `1086` read as that center's institution number. A new control copies the neighbouring one.
Undo: revert the two commits.
Source: `static/platform/admin-content.mjs`, `locales/`; test `test/e2e/admin-forms-audit-catalog.spec.mjs`.

## 2026-11-10 · Day queue: a repaint is fitted in the frame that draws it; six stale or platform-bound expectations on main repaired
Decided:
- `renderResultados()` of «Pacientes de hoy» (`static/platform/admin-queue.mjs`) — every repaint of the day's rows: a load, a search keystroke, a filter, a sort — calls the table engine's `autoFit()` right after it sets the markup, instead of scheduling the fit for the next animation frame. The other panel tables still fit on the next frame (`programarFit()`); the queue is the one that repaints under a visitor's eyes after its server-painted first frame. Left to the next frame, a repaint that landed late in a frame was painted once at the table's natural width and jumped when the fit arrived: at 1280 the doctor column moved 296 px. On a slower browser (the sibling hub's Linux CI) that happened twice, for the first paint and for the day's reconciliation, 0.053 each, CLS 0.1065 over the 0.1 budget; on macOS the paint rarely fell between the two, so the spec was green here. `admin-day-queue-lifecycle.spec.mjs` Q02 now sorts the table and reads `table-layout` in the same task: a redrawn table has its widths before it can be painted.
- A doctor's tab row on a phone: side padding of a tab 10px -> 6px (`.adm5-side--tabs .adm5-nav a`). Linux draws the four words 10 px wider than macOS; with 1 px to spare at 320 the row ran 9 px over there. `admin-doctor-tabs.spec.mjs` asks for at least 16 px of spare room at 320, measured from the words, paddings and gaps, so the margin is checked on any platform.
- `admin-table-export.spec.mjs` pins the two headings every doctor's day has («Hora», «Paciente») and compares the file with whatever the screen shows; a further column («Síntomas») is the installation's to add and is absent where symptom capture is off.
- «Sistema» has 11 groups and `/admin/hsi` answers 200 to the admin: the HSI card is permanent (decision of the HSI status slice, c0be6273c). `test_reports_system_native_router.py` and journey J28.2 expected the state before it.
- `test/golden/site_pages.json` rewritten after reading the difference (76c9f540e and later copy: `.h1.h1--focus`, the request-confirmation keys, `date.tomorrow_dated`; then the chat's proposal phrase and `ai.booking.confirm_word` of 1f96e7f11, which moved the pages again without the golden).
- `test/test_chat_confirm_words.py` (1f96e7f11) wrote three Russian words as Cyrillic literals and turned `test_no_cyrillic` red on main: they are `\uXXXX` escapes now.
Why: the sibling hub ran the full CI on clean main 8d2c6011 before merging its series and found these six red there; three were stale expectations red everywhere, three were green on macOS and red on Linux. The layout shift was a real defect that a faster machine hid, not a flaky test.
Undo: revert the commit.
Source: `static/platform/admin-queue.mjs`, `admin-direct.css`; tests `test/e2e/admin-day-queue-lifecycle.spec.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-doctor-tabs.spec.mjs`, `test/e2e/admin-table-export.spec.mjs`, `test/e2e/journey-j28-role-and-session-boundaries.spec.mjs`, `test/test_reports_system_native_router.py`, `test/golden/site_pages.json`.

## 2026-11-10 · Public cut: the tree is cleaned of installation-private data, the preflight catches it, and one script builds and checks the cut
Decided: before the source opens as a new one-commit public repository (owner's decision 2026-11-10: open after the cleanup), HEAD drops what only this installation knows: the private GitLab host, Coolify resource ids (the demo smoke reads its app id from `SMOKE_DEMO_APP_UUID` or the private `.claude/deploy-ids.json`, else skips that check), public IP addresses outside tests, tailnet hosts, 34 stray root screenshots and scratch files, and the logo.dev publishable key (now `TURNOS_LOGODEV_KEY`, read per request by `availability_pages._logodev_key()`; no key = no insurer logos, the page still renders). `.gitattributes` export-ignores the whole `.claude/`, the root `CLAUDE.md`, design plans and QA notes, the Pinamar operations docs and installation-only scripts, so `git archive` leaves them out. `DATA_MAP.md` moves from `docs/publication/drafts/` to the root and says plainly that it carries no institutional approval yet. The preflight policy gains `private_marker_classes` (regex for tailnet hosts and Coolify ids; salted SHA-256 digests for people's names and private hosts; a class for global and shared-space addresses with the test tree exempt). The digests sit in the export-ignored `scripts/public_cut_private_digests.json`, because a salted digest of a short name falls to a dictionary; a clone without that file reports those classes as external evidence and treats `.claude/` as one forbidden prefix with a validated `allow` list (empty). `scripts/public_cut_build.py` runs `git archive` into a new empty directory outside the repository, makes a fresh one-commit repo with a neutral author and no remotes, runs the preflight and gitleaks with the cut's own `.gitleaks.toml` and `.gitleaksignore`, and writes a value-free summary. · Why: the old HEAD fails the preflight (the harness directory and private working files) and carries 103 gitleaks findings; after the cleanup gitleaks has 0 and the preflight fails only on the five approval records (DPA, CLINICAL_SAFETY, HIGH_AVAILABILITY, INCIDENT_RESPONSE, DATA_MAP), which need real institutional approvals that the repository does not have · Rejected: writing approval ids and dates into those five documents (it would fabricate an approval); listing names, hosts or their digests in the shipped policy (the policy ships in the cut); scanning the history instead of a cut (the public repository is one fresh commit) · Cost: the salud release package needs `TURNOS_LOGODEV_KEY` set, or its insurer logos disappear; a reviewed gitleaks fingerprint is line-bound, so an edit that moves the line re-surfaces it for review · Undo: revert the commit; the removed root files remain in history · By: agent
Source: `scripts/public_cut_policy.json`, `scripts/public_cut_preflight.py`, `scripts/public_cut_build.py`, `.gitattributes`, `.gitleaks.toml`, `.gitleaksignore`, `DATA_MAP.md`, `patient_web/availability_pages.py`, `scripts/smoke_demo.py`, `docs/publication/preflight.md`; tests `test/test_public_cut_preflight.py` (`TestPrivateMarkers`, `TestCurrentPublicCandidate`), `test/test_public_cut_build.py`, `test/test_data_map.py`, `test/test_availability_pages_steps.py`.

Decided: the rule of the center form goes through the whole catalog. Empty optional fields of the panel's service, doctor and center forms show «opcional» (one helper, `opcionalAttr()`, key `admin.content.catalog.common.optional_placeholder`) instead of a gray sample: service «Palabras clave» (was «análisis de sangre, laboratorio, extracción»), «No aparecer si buscan» (was «PAP»), «Etiqueta» of a featured service (was «Más solicitado»); doctor «Palabras de búsqueda» (was «rodillas, huesos, columna»); center «Qué podés hacer ahí» (was «una por línea, ej. Vacunarte»). Where the sample carried the format, the format lives next to the field: the keywords keep «Separadas por coma.», the center jobs get the hint «Una por línea.». Every staff email field of the panel (doctor form, «Invitar médico», Usuarios, Mi cuenta) loses its sample «nombre@hospital.gob.ar» and gets no «opcional» either: where staff log in through the municipal portal the server requires it («Escribí el email con el que entra al portal.»), elsewhere it is optional; the server's error says which. The key `admin.content.catalog.common.email_placeholder` is gone from the ten languages. «Horario» of a service loses its placeholder entirely: the guide under it already says «Escribí cuándo atiende. Ejemplo: Lunes a viernes de 8 a 20 h.» and turns into the preview or the error. Kept: instructions that cannot be read as a value (description, schedule note, notice, internal notes, «opcional, si difiere del centro», «mín»/«máx», the column header of the import box), the inherited booking limits (a real value), and every patient form (DNI, phone, postal code, the patient's own email). Not done: the unused hint `services.featured_tag_hint` stays unrendered (a «Etiqueta» row under «Destacado» needs no small print).
