## 2026-11-13 · Anonymous analytics stream kept in the installation's own database, read through a key-guarded export API
Decided: the pages people visit, their searches and booking steps (slots shown, submit, done, error) are written to the installation's own Postgres (tables `analytics_events`, `analytics_schedule`, `analytics_keys`, Alembic 0044), only when `TURNOS_ANALYTICS=1` (off by default; NOT enabled on salud without the municipality's consent). An event carries an allowlist of fields only (`analytics_events.FIELDS`, pinned by a test): a 23-character id, time, route template plus public slug, referrer host, language, device class, a visit id (`jid`, kept in the tab's sessionStorage, rotates after 30 minutes) and a page-load id (`pl`); never an IP, User-Agent, cookie, persistent id, DNI, name, e-mail or query string. Search text is cut down to the public catalog vocabulary (other words become a mask, numbers and addresses drop the whole text). Robots and webdriver pages are dropped; ingest is rate-limited per IP in memory. Once a day after 04:00 the public schedule (hours, slots, taken, free per doctor, center, service and day, 60 days ahead, counts only) is snapshotted; events are pruned after `TURNOS_ANALYTICS_RETENTION_DAYS` (400). The data is read by the Reportes screen «Visitas» and by `GET /api/analytics/events` and `/api/analytics/schedule` (NDJSON, keyset paged by the `X-Next-After` header, `since`/`until`/`type`/`limit` up to 5000, 60 requests a minute per key): a Bearer key the admin issues and revokes in the panel (only its SHA-256 hash is stored, the value is shown once); a wrong, revoked or over-limit key gets a bare 401/404/429.
Why: the product owner (13.11) wants the Turnos usage stream for their own conclusions without exposing patients, and wants it inside the installation, with API access so the data can be pulled out. An external bucket would add a processor of data and a second place to secure; the installation's database already holds more sensitive data, and the stream is counts and public vocabulary.
Rejected: a Backblaze bucket as the base store (kept optional, not built); reusing `searches` (free text of patients, no steps); logging IP or User-Agent (identifiers).
Undo: set `TURNOS_ANALYTICS=0` (everything stops; the tables stay); or revert the commit (forward-only revision, tables remain unused).
Source: `app/modules/turnos/analytics_events.py`, `analytics_export.py`, `analytics_schedule.py`, `analytics_report.py`, `analytics_api_router.py`, `app/core/db_stream.py`, `static/platform/analytics.mjs`, `alembic/versions/0044_analytics_stream.py`; tests `tests/test_analytics_events.py`, `tests/test_analytics_stream.py`.
By: agent hr-analytics-stream

## 2026-11-13 · Analytics stream: review findings fixed, each with a red test first
Decided: (1) the referrer travels as a bare host (`analytics.mjs` sends `hostname`); `ref_of` reads a bare host, `host:port` and a full URL alike, so «De dónde llegan» no longer counts every visit as direct. (2) The booking funnel is monotone: a stage is counted only for visits that reached it or any later stage (`_funnel`, walked backwards along `visit > profile > slots_shown > submit > done`), and the search stage is a subset of visits; a visit that skips a stage (a deep link) still counts in the later ones. (3) `record_search` passes through the same gate as `/api/ev` (`analytics_export._admit`: robots refused, then the ceilings). (4) Reports read the window with `db_stream.events_iter` (keyset on `(ts, id)`, 5000 rows a call) and aggregate while the rows pass; `events_since` is gone. (5) Disconnecting the MCP assistant draws a «desconectado» state with an explicit «Conectar un asistente de nuevo» link; a token is issued only when the person presses it. (6) An empty schedule snapshot is retried after 2 h, not every 10 min; the export counts the ADDRESS (salted hash, memory only) before it looks up the Bearer key (300/min) and then the key (60/min); the export holds back events younger than 10 s (`_LAG_S`) because an id is made before its row commits, so a cursor that moved past a newer id could skip an older one; the ingest ceiling per address went from 120 to 1200 batches a minute (a hospital NAT is one address) and a second ceiling of 60 a minute per visit id stops a single looping browser; neither key is stored (salted hash in memory).
Not a bug: the comparison of `ts` with naive time in `prune`: `sweep_once` passes `clock.now()` (aware) and the column is `timestamptz`, so both sides are instants.
Why: the review of the first release found the figures of Reportes → Visitas could be wrong; the data has to be right before anyone draws conclusions from it.
Rejected: a persistent id or an IP column to rate-limit better (identifiers); a database-side aggregate per report (the rows are folded in Python in one pass, memory bounded by the number of distinct visits).
Cost: the export lags 10 s behind the live stream; a visit's per-visit ceiling is a guess to be tuned from real traffic.
Undo: revert the commit; no schema change.
Source: `app/modules/turnos/analytics_events.py`, `analytics_export.py`, `analytics_report.py`, `analytics_api_router.py`, `searches.py`, `app/core/db_stream.py`, `static/platform/analytics.mjs`, `account.mjs`; tests `tests/test_analytics_events.py`, `tests/test_analytics_stream.py`, `tests/account-mcp-disconnect.test.mjs`.
By: agent hr-analytics-fixes

## 2026-11-13 · ChatGPT connects to /mcp through a minimal OAuth 2.1 on top of the patient account
Decided: ChatGPT (web and iPhone app) accepts a remote MCP server only with OAuth 2.1 (authorization code + PKCE S256, dynamic client registration, redirect on chatgpt.com) or with no auth; it cannot send a static Bearer, so the token from Cuenta never worked there. /mcp now advertises OAuth: `/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource`, `/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server`, `POST /oauth/register`, `GET|POST /oauth/authorize` (consent screen after the patient signs in) and `POST /oauth/token`; a 401 on tools/call carries `WWW-Authenticate: Bearer resource_metadata=...` and tools/list carries `securitySchemes: oauth2`. It is stateless: client_id and code are HMAC-signed short-lived blobs (key from the admin signing key, one domain per purpose, consent bound to the DNI, code 60 s, single use best-effort per process, PKCE required). The access token IS the patient MCP token (`users.mcp_token`, hashed, 30 days sliding), so «Desconectar el asistente» and «Generar uno nuevo» revoke an OAuth connection too; one connection per patient. Registration accepts only https redirects on chatgpt.com, chat.openai.com, openai.com, claude.ai, claude.com. The well-known and /oauth paths are exempt from the pinamar site Basic Auth; salud stays closed by the portal gate, by design. Cuenta tells the patient that ChatGPT needs only the server and OAuth.
Why: product owner 2026-11-13: ChatGPT on the iPhone cannot connect; OpenAI docs (developers.openai.com/apps-sdk/build/auth and /api/docs/guides/developer-mode) list OAuth or no auth as the only options.
Rejected: a database table of clients and codes (stateless needs no schema or PII entry); a second token type (it would need its own revoke path); no-auth /mcp (the tools act on a patient).
Cost: a code replayed against another process inside 60 s is not caught by the memory set (PKCE and the TTL still bind it); one connection per patient, a new OAuth connect replaces the previous token.
Undo: revert the commit; no schema change.
Source: `app/modules/chat/mcp_oauth.py`, `mcp_oauth_router.py`, `mcp_api.py`, `module.json`, `app/auth/site_auth.py`, `templates/oauth-consent.html`, `static/platform/account.mjs`, `locales/*/account.json`; tests `tests/test_mcp_oauth.py`, `tests/test_mcp_api_router.py`, `tests/test_site_auth.py`.
By: agent hr-chatgpt-mcp

## 2026-11-13 · MCP OAuth: a native client may register a loopback redirect (RFC 8252)
Decided: `/oauth/register` accepts `http://127.0.0.1`, `http://[::1]` and `http://localhost` redirect URIs, any port and path, next to the https hosts of the known assistants. No other scheme, no other host, no userinfo, no fragment; the consent page «Permitir» is unchanged.
Why: Codex CLI returns the code to a loopback listener and was refused at registration (live check on the demo, 2026-11-13); the product owner's hub decided to allow it.
Rejected: widening the https host list; a fixed port (native clients pick a free one).
Cost: a malicious local program could register a loopback URI, but the code then goes to the user's own machine and still needs the patient's explicit consent and the PKCE verifier.
Undo: revert the commit; no schema change.

## 2026-11-13 · MCP OAuth single use lives in Postgres; the analytics export is bounded
Decided: an authorization code (and a consent) is taken once for the whole installation through a hash row in `alert_claims` (`db_analytics.claim_daily_alert`, fail closed when the database is down), not through process memory; a malformed `resource` or a non-ASCII `code_challenge` answers 400 or the consent page, never 500. The export rate table has a ceiling (`_MAX_KEYS`, swept under one lock, then per-visit counters dropped); the schedule cursor is base64url JSON so a pipe in a name cannot break it; `top_searches` glues typing fragments in SQL (`lead` over the session) with the window and prefix test shared with the stream report in `app/core/search_collapse.py`; a portal rebinding revokes the MCP token only when one is set (`only_if_set`), so a mismatched binding writes nothing per request.
Why: code-review high findings on c5c632e09 and earlier; each was confirmed by a red test first.
Rejected: moving the analytics stream into `worker._JOBS` (a daily, idempotent, non-outbound thread under the `has_snapshot` guard gains nothing from a queue); a new table for codes (`alert_claims` already is an atomic one-time claim).
Cost: issuing a token needs the database up (the exchange refuses otherwise); the rate ceiling loosens briefly under a flood; a cursor issued before the deploy is refused once (the reader restarts the page).
Undo: revert the commit; no schema change.
Source: `app/modules/chat/mcp_oauth.py`, `app/modules/turnos/analytics_export.py`, `analytics_api_router.py`, `analytics_report.py`, `app/core/db_analytics.py`, `app/core/search_collapse.py`, `app/cuentas/accounts.py`, `tests/test_mcp_oauth_once.py`

## 2026-11-14 · WhatsApp inbound shared by prod and demo: the webhook forwards the sibling's messages
Decided: pinamar and the demo share one Meta app and one phone number, so Meta delivers every inbound message to ONE callback. The receiving installation looks at the sender: `TURNOS_WA_FORWARD_NUMBERS` (the sibling's test recipients) plus `TURNOS_WA_FORWARD_URL` (the sibling's webhook) make it re-send those messages, re-signed with its own `META_WA_APP_SECRET` and marked `X-Turnos-WA-Forwarded` (a forwarded request is never forwarded again), and not process them itself. A demo installation with `TURNOS_WA_TEST_RECIPIENTS` set processes only its own numbers and drops the rest, so a Si/No reply from a demo patient can no longer touch a pinamar appointment, and the reverse.
Why: TASK-23, replies to demo reminders were landing on the pinamar webhook.
Rejected: a second Meta app (needs a second number and verification); routing by message text (ambiguous).
Cost: an extra hop (5 s timeout, best-effort, logged) for sibling messages; the two installations must share the same app secret.
Undo: unset the two envs (no forwarding, previous behaviour); revert the commit.

## 2026-11-14 · The worker waits for the panel settings before it registers jobs
Decided: before `_register_jobs` the worker polls the catalog settings row (`worker._await_settings_db`, 120 s bound, 3 s step, ends on a stop request) and then drops the settings cache. `config.get` reads an unreadable catalog as "no overrides", so a worker that booted before the web process finished migrating saw `TURNOS_BACKUP_ENABLED` as 0 and registered no encrypted backup until the next deploy.
Why: TASK-24. The in-app encrypted backup to Backblaze B2 on pinamar had run every day since 2026-10-30 (integrity verified, one scratch restore with row counts on 11-05) and stopped after the 2026-11-13 08:00 deploy: the worker log said "encrypted installation backups: disabled", the hourly due check was no longer scheduled.
Rejected: a second host cron dump to B2 (it would duplicate the verified archive and need a new key pair and a second place to watch); registering the job even when unconfigured (an unconfigured installation would gain a dormant job surface).
Cost: a worker boot can take up to 120 s longer when the catalog stays unreadable.
Undo: revert the commit; no schema change.
Source: `app/worker.py`, `tests/test_worker_settings_wait.py`
By: agent hr-data-23-24

## 2026-11-14 · Login and recovery: Turnstile host, lockout by DNI, atomic OTP, Secure cookie (M-5, TASK-72)
Decided: (1) a development Host (`localhost`, a one-label name, `*.local`) skips the Turnstile gate only when the verified peer is loopback or private; a public peer sending that Host still faces the gate. (2) Patient login and reset-by-code lock one DNI after 10 failures in 15 min (429), whatever the IP; success clears the counter. (3) `verify_otp` claims the attempt and burns the code each in one conditional UPDATE, so parallel guesses cannot exceed the 5-attempt cap. (4) The patient session cookie carries `Secure` when the public origin is https.
Why: security review 2026-11 (M-5): the Host header is typed by the client, the per-IP brake is bypassed by rotating addresses, the OTP cap was read-then-written, and part of the cookies lacked `Secure`; red tests first (`tests/test_security_review_login.py`, `tests/test_db_accounts_store.py`).
Rejected: `Secure` from `X-Forwarded-Proto` for this cookie (a header the client can drop; the origin setting is the installation's own word); a lockout persisted in Postgres (in memory like the IP brakes, a restart clears it).
Cost: someone typing a DNI's password wrong 10 times locks that patient out for 15 min (the lockout is the point); an http dev origin keeps cookies without `Secure`.
Undo: revert the commit; no schema change.
Source: `app/auth/turnstile.py`, `app/core/proxy_trust.py`, `app/cuentas/ocr_dni.py`, `app/cuentas/accounts.py`, `app/core/db_accounts.py`, `tests/test_security_review_login.py`

## 2026-11-14 · Seven low findings closed: recipe link expiry, TOTP restart, bounded upload read, /setup, demo sessions, recovery timing, authenticator change (L-1, TASK-73)
Decided: (1) a prescription/order link stops opening 365 days after the consultation (`printable.LINK_DAYS`; no reissue path exists yet, so the span is long). (2) Starting a new TOTP enrolment on an account that already has one asks for the current password (not where the portal vouches for staff). (3) The lab upload reads at most `MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES + 1` bytes. (4) `/setup` is closed (404) whenever the owner password is set in the environment. (5) The demo's one-click admin and doctor sessions live 12 hours. (6) Password recovery sends its mail and code after the answer, in a detached thread, so an existing and an unknown DNI/email take the same time and return the same body. (7) The session cookie carries a stamp of the account's 2FA secret (`ts`, like `cs` for the password): enrolling, replacing or removing the authenticator closes every other session, and the browser that did it gets a fresh cookie; a cookie without the stamp stays valid.
Why: security review 2026-11 (L-1); a test per item (`tests/test_security_review_low.py`, `tests/test_setup.py`).
Rejected: a hard error for an expired link (the patient asks the desk for a fresh document either way); failing signup with the same answer for a taken email (the form cannot work without telling the visitor; the DNI case moves to the desk claim, TASK-68).
Cost: a recovery mail failure is logged, never shown; demo visitors re-enter after 12 hours.
Undo: revert the commit; no schema change.

## 2026-11-14 · Branch codex/hsi-cancel-uncertainty-20260925 is retired: the HSI work is in main, the native-module migration is not needed
Decision: the 23 commits of the 2026-09-25 branch are closed part by part. (1) The 14 HSI commits (EMR create identity and lease, uncertain create/cancel/reassign, unconfigured connector, move generations, settlement fencing, atomic acknowledgement): the code is already in main under the new layout (`app/modules/turnos/hsi_journal.py`, `_degrade_unconfigured` is the same text; the tests `test_hsi_outbox_identity`, `test_hsi_worker_fence`, `test_emr_bridge_conformance`, `test_hsi_cancel_ordering` exist), and nine `docs/solutions/hsi/` notes that main lacked are carried over in this commit with `tests/` paths. (2) Storybook commits (`197d716aa`, `75c705dd9`, `558d62d7e`, `d875a15b6`, `6c345ea5b`, `7db2e36ff`): every story and fixture of the branch already exists in `stories/` and `.storybook/`; the component-story inventory and contract (`docs/roadmap/component-story-*`, its test) is not carried over, because it was an advisory guard over a component list that main has since rewritten, and a regenerated inventory has no consumer. (3) `Migra Solicitudes y servicios del panel a modulos nativos` (`dccab07ea`), `Migra entrevista previa a modulos nativos` (`79c8a50b2`), `Empaqueta procesos web y worker fuera de la raiz` (`8c0e496c6`), `static/platform/admin-shell.mjs`, `test_runtime_entrypoints.py`, `test_i18n_baseline_entrypoint.py` and the five `docs/solutions/dev/` notes about them: main reached the same goals another way (`app/` layout, `module.json` manifests, one entrypoint `app/asgi_main.py`, editorial controllers already destroyed on remount), and the branch's files target the pre-move paths, so they would not apply.
Why: the branch was held as unmerged work; each part was compared with main by code symbols, test files and added lines (53-85% of the HSI lines and 84-91% of the Storybook lines are literally in main).
Rejected: merging or cherry-picking the branch (paths predate the 2026-11 layout; the parts that matter are already in main); keeping it as an open branch (the refs stay for now only because the repo hygiene hold lists them; the hold can be lifted).

## 2026-11-14 · Staff-auth bypasses: demo cookie, stamp-less cookie, own credentials, 2FA start, login lockout by account, upload before parsing, Secure by origin (security review findings 8-11, 14, 17, 19)
Decided: (1) The cookie of the demo's one-click sessions carries a demo flag (`d`); with the demo mode off the cookie is no session, so turning the demo off with the same signing key no longer leaves an admin behind. (2) A session cookie without the authenticator stamp (`ts`) is no session wherever the account has a live stamp (an account without a 2FA secret has its own fingerprint, so this is every account): the one-time cost is a re-login for cookies minted before the stamp existed. (3) Editing your own account to set a TOTP secret or a password asks for the current password (`actual`), except where the portal vouches for staff. (4) `2fa/start` treats a failed read of the account as a refusal (503), never as a first enrolment. (5) `/api/admin/login` also counts failures per account name (key `u|<name>`, same limit and window as the per-IP one), answered the same for an existing and an unknown name. (6) The lab upload route declares no multipart parameters: the session answers first (401), then the declared length (411 absent, 413 over the cap plus 1 MiB of framing), and only then the form is parsed, so an anonymous or oversized body is never spooled to disk. (7) The staff cookie is `Secure` also when the installation's public origin is https, not only on `X-Forwarded-Proto`.
Why: independent review of HEAD d4f65b252; a test per item (`tests/test_staff_auth_bypasses.py`).
Rejected: a per-account lock that blocks the real owner for good (the window is 5 minutes and a good login clears it); keeping stamp-less cookies valid for a grace period (it keeps the revocation hole open).
Cost: everyone signs in once after the deploy; an attacker can slow a known account's login for 5 minutes (the same limit already applies per IP); an anonymous upload without a file is 401 instead of 422.
Undo: revert the commit; no schema change.
Source: `app/auth/admin_auth.py`, `app/admin_panel/admin_users.py`, `app/admin_panel/admin_account.py`, `app/ai/lab_results_router.py`, `tests/test_staff_auth_bypasses.py`
By: agent hr-sec-staff-auth

## 2026-11-14 · A sign-up on a taken document number is a desk claim, not a 409 (TASK-68, step a)
Decided: with `TURNOS_DNI_CLAIMS` on (the default; the demo forces it off in `_SAFETY_DEFAULTS`, the e2e harness sets it to 0) the first password sign-up on a DNI is stored as an `unverified` claim in the new table `dni_claims` (Alembic 0045), and a second sign-up on the same DNI is no longer refused: it succeeds under a minted synthetic key with a `pending` claim naming the number it asked for. The claimant logs in with the DNI and their own password (the login also tries the pending claimants of that DNI), and every account answer carries `dni_verificacion` (`unverified` / `pending`) so the next steps can hide clinical data until the desk confirms. One open claim per account (partial unique index); no foreign key, so the 18-key registry stays as it is, and `dni_claims` sits in `personal_data.REGISTRY` and in the archive/move lists. With the flag off the old 409 stays. The photo sign-up is the default path of the open-source engine and lives only on the demo; salud and pinamar use the portal.
Why: security review 2026-11 (M-1): anyone knowing a DNI could register it first and lock the real owner out, or learn that a DNI is registered from the 409.
Rejected: a new column on `users` (the claim needs its own status and history); a foreign key from the claimant to `users` (would change the count of 18 and block the desk's key move); keeping the 409 (the owner stays locked out).
Cost: the answer to a taken number now differs from a free one by `dni_verificacion` (accepted: the form cannot work without telling the visitor); each claim needs the desk (steps b and c).
Undo: set `TURNOS_DNI_CLAIMS=0`; the table stays harmless.
Source: `app/cuentas/accounts.py`, `app/core/db_accounts.py`, `app/cuentas/accounts_store.py`, `alembic/versions/0045_dni_claims.py`, `tests/test_dni_claims.py`
By: agent hr-sec-review

## 2026-11-14 · An account the desk has not confirmed reads no clinical data (TASK-68, step b)
Decided: `app/cuentas/claim_gate.py` answers one question, `hidden(dni)` (an open `dni_claims` row on the account's key, flag on). Hidden accounts may book, but their own request list and detail (`booking_patient`), the calendar feed, the lab results page, the signed lab link and the assistant tools `mine` and `lab_results` answer as if there were nothing. `TURNOS_DNI_CLAIMS=0` hides nothing. Review of step a: one number takes at most `MAX_PENDING_CLAIMS` (5) open claims, beyond that the old 409 (bounds the pile of accounts and the password hashes a login on that number tries); erasing a person deletes only the claims their own account made, never the claims others made on their number (that would turn a pending claimant into a confirmed one); the claimant's coverage is looked up under the minted key, never the number's registry, so a claim never reads the holder's insurer.
Why: security review 2026-11 (M-1): the first account on a number was a window into the real owner's history and lab results.
Rejected: gating inside `current_user` (booking needs the session); gating `informes_de(dni)` (it is called for dependents too, the account is what is unverified).
Cost: a new password sign-up on an installation without the portal sees an empty history until the desk resolves the claim (step c); the answer to a sign-up still tells a free number from a taken one by `dni_verificacion` (accepted: the form cannot work otherwise).
Undo: `TURNOS_DNI_CLAIMS=0`.
Source: `app/cuentas/claim_gate.py`, `app/modules/turnos/booking_patient.py`, `app/modules/turnos/calendar_feed.py`, `app/ai/lab_results_router.py`, `app/modules/chat/chat_tools.py`, `tests/test_dni_claims.py`
By: agent hr-sec-review

## 2026-11-14 · Doctor-access bypasses, the patient list, CSV formulas and the patient login (security review addition)
Decided: (1) `doctor_has_patient` counts a pre-consult interview only when it is `matched` (a QR interview takes any DNI from an anonymous visitor); (2) `db_clinical.DOCTOR_DEAD_STATUSES` (rechazada, cancelada) leaves the roster and `staff_can_see` for a doctor, so the order, the referral file and the case chat close with the request (the day queue's change feed still shows a cancelled turn on purpose); (3) the requests list GET no longer assigns the suggested doctor (a read wrote a row before its access row; the panel pre-selects the suggestion and the approval or assign POST does the write, logged first); (4) `/admin/chats` logs web and WhatsApp rows too (a digest of the key as the target, never the phone number); (5) the day queue's interviews without an appointment are logged with `record_many`; (6) `/api/admin/patients` records every listed patient (action `ficha`, target `lista`) and a doctor who finds someone else's exact DNI gets identity only, no services, doctors, appointment counts or ranking; (7) report CSVs go through `table_export.safe_cell` (formula guard); (12) the recovery by code has its own counters (per DNI, per address) and no longer fills the login's; (13) a login attempt is counted before the password is checked, in one step with the limit test; (15) an unknown email is limited like a known one (the same 429); (16) a full pool of senders never delivers inline, for an account that exists or not: both wait the same for the oldest sender, then the work is dropped; (18) `POST /api/account/login` takes `application/json` only and goes through the Origin / Fetch Metadata check of the panel.
Why: security review 2026-11, second round: each one was a way to read or lock something the role or the account did not own.
Rejected: forbidding extra fields in the login body (the content type already closes the cross-site form and `text/plain` fetch); a Turnstile on the recovery by code (the counters and the address limit bound it without a new client step).
Cost: a doctor's Mis pacientes from an unmatched QR interview is not filtered by `matched` (the text is the visitor's own entry and every read of a patient stays closed by `doctor_has_patient`); a recovery asked while 32 senders are busy for over 2 s is dropped and must be asked again; a cancelled or rejected request no longer opens for the doctor.
Undo: revert the commit; no schema change.
Source: `app/core/db_clinical.py`, `app/modules/turnos/booking_requests.py`, `doctor_appointments.py`, `booking_queue.py`, `app/modules/chat/admin_chats_screen.py`, `app/admin_panel/admin_patients.py`, `reports.py`, `app/cuentas/accounts.py`, `accounts_router.py`, `app/core/admin_csrf_middleware.py`, `tests/test_sec_bypass_doctor_access.py`, `tests/test_security_review_login.py`
By: agent hr-sec-review

## 2026-11-14 · TASK-68 step c: the desk resolves a document-number claim in one transaction

**Decided:** `db_accounts.resolve_claim(claim_id, approve, operator)` closes a claim made by step a/b in a single transaction, exposed as `GET /api/admin/dni-claims` and `POST /api/admin/dni-claims/{id}/resolve` (permission `crear_turnos`, never a doctor; recorded in the access log as `reclamo`, a row that cannot be written refuses the resolution) and as a strip above the roster in Pacientes. Reject archives the claimant's account. Approve of an unverified claim only closes it. Approve of a pending claim moves the current holder's account to a random `ANON-<hex16>` key, then moves the claimant onto the number with `change_dni_in`; the clinical identity stays with the number. If both accounts carry data of their own that cannot be merged, the answer is a 409 `conflicto` and nothing changes.

**Why:** a closed claim counts as «verified» in `claim_gate`, so rejecting without archiving would have verified the claimant. One transaction keeps the 18 RESTRICT keys and every `personal_data.REGISTRY` table consistent: a test covers each moved, cascaded and kept column.

**Rejected:** merging two accounts automatically (loses data silently); deleting the holder (RESTRICT keys, no erasure without the data-subject flow); letting a doctor resolve claims.

**Cost:** the desk handles claims by hand; live appointments of a rejected account are not cancelled.

**Undo:** revert the commit; resolved claims stay in `dni_claims` with their state.

**Source:** product owner, TASK-68 go (m2735783), 2026-11-14.

**By:** agent hr-sec-review.

## 2026-11-14 · TASK-68 review: what was accepted on purpose

**Decision:** left as is after the code review of the three steps: the cap of 5 open claims per number and the 200-row list can be filled by other people's sign-ups (the desk sees the number and removes them, a reject archives the claimant); an approved claimant keeps `identity_kind=synthetic` (nothing reads it for access); a claimant's successful login counts toward the number's lock key; the approve dialog uses one text for `unverified` and `pending`; the patient site does not yet explain `dni_verificacion` on empty lists; a GET of the request list no longer assigns the suggested doctor (approving applies it).

**Why:** each needs either a product screen, a wider migration or a decision of the desk's workflow, and none opens clinical data or locks the real owner out of the desk path. **Undo:** each is a separate small follow-up.

**Source:** code review of TASK-68, 2026-11-14.

**By:** agent hr-sec-review.

## 2026-11-15 · Turnstile dev-host test hands the gate a signed peer

**Decision:** the application is NOT changed. Under `TURNOS_ASGI=1` the verified peer exists only as the signed `proxy_trust.HEADER` marker (and only with a real signing secret): the socket address is ignored, so a request without it has an unknown peer, counts as public, and the Turnstile gate runs even for a development Host (fail closed, M-5). `test_turnstile.TestGate.test_hosts_de_dev_no_gatean` therefore sets `TURNOS_PROXY_TRUST_SECRET` and gives each dev-host request `proxy_trust.sign("127.0.0.1")` next to the loopback `client_address`, so it is green with `TURNOS_ASGI=1` and `=0`, the way `test_turnstile_client_ip.py` and `test_admin_login_throttle.py` already do it.

**Why:** the loopback `client_address` added in 40421bc4b decides the peer only in the legacy mode; the asgi gate (`local-ci-asgi`) kept the test red on 40421bc4b and its parent. **Undo:** revert the commit.

**By:** agent hospital.

**Decision:** `tests/golden/site_pages.json` is recorded again with `test_site_page_snapshots.py --write` on the current code. 4fcf07b6b added a cluster to `packs/example-town/clusters.json`, so the pack digest no longer matched and `test_every_page_is_what_it_was_before_the_functions_were_cut` skipped itself; the coverage guard of the full gate counts that skip as unexpected and turns the run red (hidden until now behind the Turnstile failure). Read before recording: the same 308 pages, every page grew by the same amount per language (Spanish +1573 or +1602 bytes, English +72 or +101), which fits one shared fragment carrying the new cluster (byte bodies are not kept in the golden, so this is the inference, not a diff).

**Why:** a golden that skips guards nothing; the diff above is the new cluster and only it. **Undo:** revert the commit.

**By:** agent hospital.

## 2026-11-15 · Novedades: patient entries only, the reference deployment's entries only on its own sites

**Decision:** every `_NEWS` item carries who sees it, as marker classes that compose with `P(…)`: `S(…)` panel/staff change, kept in the file and the translation sweep but never on `/news`; `R(…)` names the reference deployment (its domain, programs, people or its site's look), shown only where the `ar-b-pinamar` pack is in the chain (legacy single-dir mode: where the city is Pinamar); `X(…)` no longer true, hidden everywhere. Items are never deleted or reordered: the position is the translation key. The lede that repeated the H1 is gone; «A pedido» is a `.subtabs` pair of links (Todas | A pedido) to `/news` and `/news/pedidos`, not a checkbox that navigates; a date with one item is a paragraph; older months fold into one closed `<details>` each once the open part holds 8 items (`/news/pedidos` stays all open). Where the site's brand is the platform's own name, the 8 July entry names the site as «los turnos de salud de {city}» instead of «la plataforma detrás de Human Rounds», and any leftover «Pinamar» in an entry renders as the installation's city.

**Why:** the demo published the reference client's domain, programs and the look of another site, and the page mixed panel changes into a patient page. The full history open was ~40,000 px on a phone, and a 3x full-page capture of it came out garbled (the reviewer's «cut-off»); folded it is ~5,600 px and captures whole. The `novedades-changelog` skill now asks for the marker on every new entry, and `tests/test_novedades.py` holds the list of names the demo must never publish. **Undo:** revert the commit.

**By:** agent news-slice (demo review 2026-11-15).

## 2026-11-15 · One emergency number per installation, one phone link rule, informational pages reordered

**Decision:** pack prose names the emergency number as `{{sitio.emergency_number}}`, never a literal (the engine's own packs: the example-town Guardia notice, the global preparation text, example-town enlaces). `site_identity.expand()` resolves it AFTER `pack_l10n.localize`, in the loaders (`site_centers._links`, `site_catalog._services`, `site_catalog._preparations`), so the translation cache keeps hashing the canonical text and the canonical rows (`localize=False`) keep the placeholder. The client tap-to-call hook links the number the footer shows, not a hard-coded 107. Every `tel:` href is `format.tel_href` / `telHref` (E.164 for a complete number, the bare digits for 107/112), so a link dials exactly the number `tel_text` prints. /telemedicine puts «No es para emergencias» with that number right after the one-sentence lede, before any button; a button is drawn only for a real https target (never a `.example` placeholder); `quien` / `costo` / `cta_nota` are plain facts, steps only if a pack gives them. /blood-donation shows where and when (the `lugar` center, else the main center; `horario` if known, else the next step as body text) and the center's phone/WhatsApp buttons. The informational pages share one body size (17px) and one notice style (`.info-alert`: theme text on the panel); phone buttons keep their content width. The emergency line is shown where a patient may need it — the footer, telemedicine and the Guardia notice — not on every list (P15). The Pinamar pack keeps literal numbers (107 matches its sitio.json) so the municipal installation's current engine never shows raw placeholders.

**Why:** the demo showed 112 in the footer and 107 on telemedicine, a blood phone that dialled another number than it printed, and a telemedicine button to a placeholder domain (demo review 2026-11-15, D1, D2, D10, D11, D16, D28, D32). **Undo:** revert the commit of this entry.

**By:** agent hr-public-review-info.

**Decision:** the listing engine (`static/platform/listing.mjs`) that draws the specialty page, the center page and /doctors follows the 2026-11-15 demo review. Every fact in a row is 16px ink, with no tabular digits (Manrope's tabular «1» made «12:30» read «1 2:30»). A booking button carries ONE label at every width, `Sacar jue 19 nov, 08:00` (verb + `Dates.corta`); the long desktop form and the hidden mobile twin are gone. Buttons, «Con orden», «Más horarios» and the remedy link are at least 44px tall. «Con orden» and «Sin orden» share weight and colour; the «i» and the search magnifier and the select chevron are Icons8 files painted through a CSS mask. On desktop the listing stays in the content column: this revokes the 2026-07-23 full-viewport breakout (listing.mjs and its copy in `site_shell.py`). The walk-in wording is «sin turno, por llegada» (P19), since «orden» already means the doctor's referral on these pages. The «Ver más» panels of the shell (`details.more`) turn their typed «+»/«–» into the Icons8 chevron.

**Why:** the review found two date shapes in one column, a fact at 13px grey, two button colours, the referral hidden on a phone and the table starting left of the page title at 1440px. **Undo:** revert the commit of this entry.

## 2026-11-15 · Public site doctors and centers: one list feeds every count, row and order

**Decision:** from the demo review of 2026-11-15. (1) Doctors' lists (`/doctors`, the doctors of `/d/<cluster>` and of `/center/<slug>`) sort soonest availability first, then name (`site_listing._soonest_first`), with no `rank:"popularidad"`; this narrows the 2026-07-14 popularity rule to services and studies. (2) On `/d/<cluster>` the per-center rows are read from the doctors listed below (`site_page_doctors._center_rows_from_doctors`): a bookable service row stays only when a listed doctor books into it, and takes their soonest turno, links and «orden» (empty when they disagree); walk-in rows and a cluster with no doctors keep the service rows. (3) The doctor's title follows the pack field `genero` ("f"/"m"), never a guess from the name; the filter value stays the generic title; English stays neutral. (4) A doctor row whose service belongs to another area names it («Cardiólogo · Ergometría»). (5) A no-slots cell carries `proximo.remedio = {texto, href}`: the soonest turno with another doctor of the same specialty or at another center, else «Contanos tu caso» in the row's own wizard. (6) The home card's «N médicos» is `site_doctors._cluster_doctors(cid)`, the same list the page shows; the bare services+doctors number in «Más especialidades» is removed. (7) A specialty page never suggests its own name as a search example. (8) Center intros (demo pack and Pinamar pack) name only services the center lists and drop «con derivación» where the doctors take no referral; a demo test pins it.

**Why:** the reviewer read numbers, labels and rows on one page that disagreed with the page itself; one source per fact removes the class. **Undo:** revert the commits of this entry (and 66b89b8 on the Pinamar data branch).

**By:** agent hr-public-review (data slice).

## 2026-11-15 · Panel catalog (doctors, services, centers): less noise, rare fields folded

**Decision:** (1) Lists: the occupancy cell is the five bars alone (the «N/5» next to them repeated the bars); a service without appointments leaves «Médicos» empty instead of a dash-like filler («Sin médico» stays where a patient books, R102); a column that has nothing in any row of the current list is not drawn, except the always-on ones and the sorted one. (2) Doctor card: the tabs stay on one line and scroll sideways on a phone, the chosen tab is brought into view; three or more days in a row read as a span («Lu a Vi 08:00–12:00», key `admin.doctor.days_span`); the «Especialidad general» line under the specialty is not written when it has the same name as the specialty. (3) Service card: «Configuración interna» (category, general specialty, age, booking limits, search words, featured) is a native `<details>` (`.cnt-fold`), folded on an existing service and open on a new one. (4) Center card: the location note, the date the address expires, the zone, the coordinates and the HSI number sit under «Ubicación exacta e HSI», open only when the center carries one of them; the label «Para qué se usa» lost its «(JTBD)»; a photo circle with neither photo nor initials is not drawn. A field error inside a folded group opens the group before it takes the focus (`revealFieldErrorControl`). Folded fields stay in the DOM, so saving reads them as before.

**Why:** the product owner asked (2026-11-15) for a pass of the panel on UI policy and usability: progressive disclosure, less text, no needless counters. **Rejected:** folding the doctor's «Límites de reserva»: the block was laid out as plain rows on 2026-10-14 and `admin-hours-editor-runtime` pins it without `<details>`; hiding inherited booking-limit placeholders (decision 2a of 2026-11-03). **Cost:** three e2e specs open the fold before they fill a field in it. **Undo:** revert the commits of this entry.

**By:** agent fable-adm-catalogo.

## 2026-11-15 · Reportes: less on every report, the rest on demand

**Decision:** the thirteen reports and their front page lose what they repeated or reserved, and keep every figure.

- The report's name in the breadcrumb opens the list of the other reports; the row of twelve links above each report is gone.
- No control has a caption beside it: the period select is named by its own choice, the three filters of Recorrido by their empty choice («Centro», «Servicio», «Médico»). On a phone the filters wait behind one funnel button next to the period and stay open while one of them is set.
- A tile carries a pill only when its figure asks for a look. Pills that praised a figure («Buen ritmo», «Dentro del día», «Prioridad clínica», «ROI») and the captions «proyección» / «proyectado a 30 días» are removed; the method is under «Supuestos de esta estimación», which opens on demand.
- A sentence already said by the page's headline is not printed again in a section; the heatmap names its three widest gaps once each instead of every cell.
- Visitas reads the stream's codes as words (origin, device, language, kind of page), counts the searches (the server names their count `veces`; the client read `n` and the block always said «Sin datos»), draws no section with nothing to say, and puts «Acceso por API» behind one line that opens.
- A card of the front page says one sentence about its figure; the login ring does not repeat the percentage; in one column a card is as tall as its content.

«Cómo leerlo» under the charts stays (owner, 2026-09-16), and sections of a report still never collapse (report-design law 9): only assumptions and the API keys, which are not findings, open on demand.

**Why:** owner's request for a pass over the panel for progressive disclosure and for text, counters and labels nobody needs. **Undo:** revert the two commits of this entry; `static/root/admin-direct.css` is the panel's shared stylesheet, and the rules touched there are all `.rep-*`.

**By:** agent fable-adm-reportes.

## 2026-11-15 · A closed assistant (MCP) installation answers 404 on the whole /mcp

**Decision:** when `mcp_api.connection_closed()` is true (salud: patients enter through the municipal portal) the application itself refuses every request to `/mcp` and `/mcp/` (POST initialize, ping, tools/list, tools/call; GET, HEAD, DELETE), with or without a token: the endpoint hands the request to the terminal router (`app/routers/native_terminal.py`), so the answer is byte for byte what a path that never existed answers for that method (JSON 404 for POST/DELETE, the HTML 404 page for GET). The check runs before the body is read or a token is looked up, so a token issued before the closure calls nothing. The stored tokens are not revoked: if the installation is opened again on purpose they work again, which is what opening means. pinamar.humanrounds.org (test portal) and the demo (passwords) keep the connection unchanged. This closes the cost listed in the 2026-11-13 entry («tokens issued before the flag keep working on /mcp»).

**Why:** an HTTP check of the salud configuration showed the account refusing a token while `/mcp` still answered initialize and tools/list anonymously and an earlier token still returned the DNI in `whoami`; the municipal gateway closes the path outside, but the application must not rely on it. **Rejected:** 403 (says the path exists and is guarded; 404 matches the rest of the closed surface). **Undo:** revert the commit; no schema change. **Proof:** `tests/test_mcp_security.py` `TestClosedInstallationAnswersNothing`.

**By:** agent mcp-closed.

**Why:** ui-expert review of 2026-11-15 on the demo editor «Lectura de la orden médica». **Rejected:** keeping the chip with zero right padding (the border alone still reads as a gap). **Undo:** revert the commit. **Proof:** `tests/test_prompts.py` `TipografiaDescripcionesTestCase`, `tests/admin-prompts.test.mjs`.

## 2026-11-15 · The demo seed books every doctor's agenda and gives most doctors an account

**Decision:** `scripts/seed_demo_all.py` gets two more steps after the walkthrough patient. `_seed_agendas` books every doctor of the catalog that has an appointment-mode service: each doctor has its own share (30-65 %, deterministic per doctor; the walkthrough doctor about 35 %), 125 % of it tomorrow down to 75 % in two weeks, with a per-day spread of +-8 points, capped at 85 % (50 % for the walkthrough doctor) and always leaving at least one free slot; the last 56 days get about 90 % of the share as approved visits marked attended (about 86 %) or missed, and about one cancelled visit in eight booked (on a slot left free). Today is untouched (`_seed_today_queue` owns it). Each slot is booked at the center whose window it falls in (`doctor_windows.sources`, the same windows the day sheet and the public page read) under the window's own service, else the doctor's first booking service of its specialty at that center; a walk-in window is never booked. Every approved appointment already in the database (a demo visitor's own booking survives a reseed) is entered in the slot ledger first, so the seed goes around it and the one-appointment-per-doctor-and-minute index never trips. Patients come from a pool of 1 200 synthetic people without accounts (DNI 31230000 + k*53, clear of the seed's 30000000 + i*137), children for pediatrics (booked by an adult of the pool), adult women for gynecology. The agenda rows carry the contact suffix `+agenda@demo.humanrounds.org` (`MARCA_AGENDA`, still inside the demo marker, so reset removes them) and `cola_del_dia` ignores it, so tomorrow's agenda never reads as tomorrow's seeded queue and the midnight refresh still reseeds. Tests that are not about the agendas switch them off (`LLENAR_AGENDAS`) to keep each seed fast. `_seed_doctor_accounts` gives about 60 % of the doctors an account (deterministic per doctor: profile complete, logged in, invited, not invited), one throwaway password hash per run; an existing account is left alone, so a reseed adds none. The bulk insert (about 21 000 rows) is one prepared statement run with `executemany` (`sqlcore.Prepared.run_many`): a statement per row cost 6 s per seed, now the whole seed takes 2-4 s.

**Why:** the demo read as dead: 11 appointments on 8 549 slots of the next 14 days, «Ocupacion 14 dias» filled for 1 doctor of 68, «Cuenta» empty for 67, every point of the reports at zero. **Rejected:** random data (the seed stays deterministic and its snapshot test holds); accounts for every doctor (the column must show all states); booking today (it would collide with the day queue the walkthrough is built on). **Undo:** revert the commit; the next demo reseed removes the rows (they carry the demo marker); the doctors' accounts are not marked rows and stay (neither seed nor reset deletes an operator), removable from the panel. **Proof:** `tests/test_seed_demo_store.py` `TestAgendasBooked`, `tests/test_sqlcore.py` (`test_a_prepared_statement_runs_over_a_batch_and_an_empty_batch_writes_nothing`).

**By:** agent demo-fill.

## 2026-11-15 · The post-deploy smokes measure the appointment cell of the public lists in a browser

**Decision:** `smoke_prod.py` and `smoke_demo.py` end with `scripts/smoke_layout.py`, which runs `scripts/smoke_listing_layout.mjs` (Playwright) on a center's page, `/d/cardiologia` and `/doctors` at 390 and 1440: in every appointment cell «Más horarios» sits under the booking button with its text inside its frame, and the button and the slot date each stay on one line. The basic-auth credential travels in the environment; a worktree without `node_modules` borrows the main checkout's Playwright.

**Why:** the UI review of 2026-11-15 caught a phone frame of the demo center page with «Más horarios» beside the button, its text out of its outline and «Mañana, 08:00» on two lines. It did not reproduce on the deployed build (both sites, three kinds of table, two widths: all in place), so the frame most likely came from the rollout window; either way only a browser sees it, and the HTTP smokes were green over it. The same picture was real in one place: with the chat panel open, `chat-widget.mjs` keeps a container-query copy of the listing's phone layout, and that copy still put «Más horarios» inline beside the button; it now mirrors `listing.mjs` (its own line, outline button), so a change to the listing cell must be mirrored there. The measurement script is excluded from c8's `all` (it only runs against a live site), and the browser context is `es-AR` so the demo, which reads Accept-Language, shows the Spanish labels. **Rejected:** a CSS-text unit test (a green test of CSS text does not prove the layout); screenshot diffing (fragile against the daily demo data). **Undo:** drop the call from both smokes. **Proof:** `tests/smoke-listing-layout.test.mjs` (the broken frame's geometry fails on all three counts), `tests/test_smoke_layout.py`, `tests/test_smoke_prod_assets.py`.

**By:** agent public-uix3.

## 2026-11-15 · Capacity report: a service's own schedule counts once per doctor it manages

**Decision:** in «Capacidad y temporada», `reports._capacity_14d` multiplies the slots of a service's own schedule by the number of doctors that service manages (`schedule_policy.managed_services`, the relation the calendar and the booking engine use), at least once. A service without a schedule of its own still sums its cluster+center doctors' windows.

**Why:** with every doctor's agenda booked on the demo, the report showed Cardiología 161 % (619 of 384), Ginecología 128 %, Salud mental 117 %: the service schedule was counted once while it applies to each of its doctors (Cardiología 384 places counted against 1 440 real ones). After the change the demo reads 14 to 52 % per cluster, in line with the doctors' own Ocupación. **Rejected:** summing each doctor's `doctor_windows.sources` per cluster (a second capacity model next to the report's; the multiplier keeps the existing one). **Undo:** revert the commit. **Proof:** `tests/test_reports_nuevos.py` `CapacidadTestCase.test_agenda_propia_cuenta_una_vez_por_medico_que_la_atiende`.

**By:** agent demo-fill.

## 2026-11-15 · Reports on a filled demo: charts that fit, a weighted capacity headline, the path out of decided requests, honest demo data

**Decision:** six fixes found by reading every report on the filled demo. Charts (`admin-charts.mjs`): `linea` widens its right margin to the widest last value, so «3.214» is no longer cut to «3.2…»; `lollipop` moves every value label past the dashed reference line when one would run over it (the phone case of «Capacidad y temporada»); `piramide` keeps room for the longest bar's count (a fixed 14 cut «19» to «9» and «1.010» to «0» on the women's side, both widths). Capacity: the specialties with no hours lead the hero only when together they hold at least `_CAPACITY_UNMET_SHARE` (2 %) of the period's requests; below that the headline goes to a specialty at 90 % or more, and only with none of those to the largest one without hours (never the «every specialty covered» line while one has no hours). Recorrido: the lead phrase counts out of the requests whose path is decided (`total - en camino`); the ones still pending or with the turno ahead stay in the «Aparte» phrase. Login gate: the 2FA prompt (`need_totp` without `error`) is a step of the login, not a `login_error`. Demo seed: the future agenda is booked only as far as it would be by now (a visit asked for 1 to 20 days ahead; 130 % of a doctor's share tomorrow, 46 % in two weeks) with its requests spread over the days they were asked; an anonymous visit stream for 35 days sized by each day's requests (`_seed_traffic`: event ids keep the export's 23-hex time-sorted contract, the visit id `jid` starts with `demo-`, which a real base36 jid never holds, and the next seed deletes by it; no event later than now); seeded accounts sign up on days spread over the last 120 (the daily reseed made all of them «new today»); a doctor overlay naming a center the catalog lacks goes back to the pack's row.

**Why:** on the demo, «Vacunas y prevención: 1 solicitud» led capacity against 4 000 turnos; «4188 de 10238 llegaron hasta Fueron al turno» counted 4 000 turnos still to come; «Visitas» showed 6 visits next to 1 091 patients with a turno; the login panel read 9 of 13 sessions with an error, and every one was password OK, code, login OK 30 s later; the current week showed 3 242 requests against about 2 000 in every other, because the flat future fill was booked in its last two days; Carlos Wu showed «Centro sin nombre» from a September overlay row on Pinamar's «caps-sur». **Rejected:** deleting the 9 mislabelled login rows on the demo (real events, they leave the 30-day window by 2026-11-25); a funnel denominator of past turnos only (the steps chart keeps the whole period). **Undo:** revert the commit; the next demo seed deletes the `demo-` stream. **Proof:** `tests/admin-charts.test.mjs`, `tests/test_reports_insights.py` `test_sin_oferta_de_peso_minimo_no_es_el_titular`, `tests/test_reports_funnel.py`, `tests/admin-login-2fa.test.mjs`, `tests/test_seed_demo_store.py` `TestSiteTraffic`, `test_the_last_week_asks_for_no_more_visits_than_the_ones_before`, `test_a_doctor_overlay_on_an_unknown_center_goes_back_to_the_pack`.

**By:** agent demo-fill.

## 2026-11-15 · One number formatter for the panel and the reports; Médicos occupancy as a percent

**Decision:** every number the panel and the reports print goes through one pair, `I18n.formatNumber` / `formatPercent` (`static/platform/i18n.mjs`, re-exported by `format.mjs`) and `i18n.format_number` / `format_percent` (`app/core/i18n.py`, re-exported by `format.py`), held equal by `tests/fixtures/number-format.json` (`tests/number-format.test.mjs`, `tests/test_number_format.py`). Separators come from a per-language table, not `Intl` (es-AR `Intl` leaves 4-digit numbers ungrouped, which put «4188 de 10238» next to «4.185»): es/pt/it/de «.»/«,», en/ja/zh «,»/«.», fr narrow no-break space, ru/uk no-break space; trailing zeros dropped; Python rounds half-up like `Intl`. A percent gets one decimal only where it carries meaning (under 10, or when rounding would show 100 from below) and is glued to its sign («52%»). `tn`/`tn_or` format a numeric `{n}` the same way. Médicos «Ocupación 14 días» is that percent, right-aligned like the hours (the five-square bar is gone); a doctor without an account reads «Sin cuenta» in the cell's dark ink, never an empty cell (the table engine hides an all-empty column). The scatter chart drops a label candidate that sits nearer another point than its own, draws a leader when a label is raised, and writes the X-axis title on the chart at the axis end. Chart labels audited on every report: a scatter label avoids the dashed reference line first and, when its only free place crosses it, gets a halo in the page colour (`paint-order:stroke`); line-series names get their own row above the plot; a treemap tile too short for name-over-value writes «Name value» on one line before falling to the list under the chart. Money keeps the installation currency's locale but always groups thousands (`useGrouping:"always"`).

**Why:** the UI review of 2026-11-15 on the filled demo: squares that needed a legend, an empty «Cuenta» that the report calls «Sin cuenta de acceso», a Médicos phone card whose second line started 58 px in, «Camila Levin» drawn on Emiliano Molina's point, and two spellings of the same number on one screen. **Kept:** the three-segment attendance bar of the doctor's queue header (owner decision 2026-10-27). **Rejected:** `Intl.NumberFormat` alone (no grouping of 4-digit numbers in es); a decimal on every percent (noise above 10). **Undo:** revert the commit. **Proof:** `tests/number-format.test.mjs`, `tests/test_number_format.py`, `tests/admin-charts.test.mjs` (scatter ownership), `tests/admin-content-ui.test.mjs`, `tests/e2e/admin-left-edge.spec.mjs` (Médicos at 390).

**By:** agent panel-uix7.

## 2026-11-15 · Report charts: a categorical palette of their own, labels and numbers next to their own points, a median caption off the points and its line; one denominator on the Recorrido card

**Decision:** charts colour categories with their own tokens `--cat-1..6` in `tokens.css` (#2a78d6, #d95926, #12855e, #6250d6, #a87000, #c94a7a, in that fixed order, each with its hex as the `var()` fallback; validated with the dataviz validator on #f4f1ea and #fff: every colour at least 3:1, normal-vision floor 17.2, worst colour-blind pair 6.7, legal with the legend and direct names). The scatter keeps a point's name only where its own point is nearer by a clear margin (8 px, or half the distance to a neighbour closer than 16 px); otherwise the name tries the next place or becomes a number listed under the chart, and the number too first looks for a place that is clearly its own point's. The median's caption is placed after the points, along its line, above or below, where it covers no point and does not touch the line, with a page-colour halo. A lollipop value that would cross the reference line goes just before its own point on a halo, not into a shared column past the line. «Dónde ya rinde la agenda» says how many doctors sit at y=0, and with how many weekly hours, from the data, in the one reading line («N de M, con H h semanales, sin turnos aprobados todavía…»); the separate «H h ofrecidas todavía no muestran turnos» sentence is gone. The Gestión heatmap reading names the tint («cuanto más oscura, mayor»), not «sin número = presión menor»: on a phone the darkest cells drop their number too. Recorrido: the lead phrase carries its own share (`hero[0].pct`, n over the decided requests) and the summary card prints that share, so the big number and the «n de total» under it share one denominator. In Recorrido's step list a step's own action replaces the reason it names: «3 sin asistencia marcada — se marca en Pacientes de hoy» (`accion_paso`); Gestión, where the action stands alone, keeps the whole sentence.

**Why:** UI review of 2026-11-15: the five centres of «Dónde ya rinde la agenda» were all grey (the chart borrowed the avatar tokens, which the humanrounds theme collapses to grey) under a caption saying «coloreado por centro»; at 390 «Emiliano Molina» and «Camila Levin» sat about 100 px from their points with nothing tying them; the median caption ran through Laura Katz's point; the fixed «la mayoría cae en y=0» read false on a full demo; the Recorrido card read 43 % over «4.252 de 6.066» (70 %); «Fueron al turno» read «3 sin asistencia marcada. Asistencia sin marcar: 3. Se marca en Pacientes de hoy.»; Estadística's «Consultas por hora ofrecida» printed «1,02» and «0,4» in a column far from their points. **Rejected:** fixing the caption text instead of colouring by centre (the legend already names centres, and the centre is the comparison the chart is for). **Undo:** revert the commit. **Proof:** `tests/admin-charts.test.mjs` (scatter ownership with numbers, median caption clear of points, palette, lollipop values next to their points), `tests/admin-reports-summary.test.mjs` (heatmap reading), `tests/test_reports_funnel.py` (`hero[0].pct`, `accion_paso`, Gestión sentence).

**Follow-up, same day:** every treemap tile keeps one layout, name over value: a tile too short for the value shows the name alone and its value goes to the list under the chart (or, with `valoresEnLista`, to the list the report already prints, as in Recupero) - rejected: running name and value together on one line in the small tiles («PAMI 5») while the others stack them. A short tile trims only its top and bottom padding, so its name starts on its neighbours' left edge. Spanish report copy says «bloque», «clic» and «enlace», not «tile», «click» and «link»; «login» stays (the name of the «Panel de login» report and of its sessions).

**By:** agent panel-uix7.

## 2026-11-15 · Reportes: the Capacidad card names the rule its report applied; a point label stands nearer in height to its own point

**Decision:** the Capacidad y temporada headline carries the rule it applied (`regla`: `sin_horas`, `atencion`, `ok`, `sin_medida`), from one server function `_capacity_rule` (the 2 % unmet-demand share included), and the Reportes card writes its finding from that field instead of re-deriving it from the specialities without hours. A card can no longer say «Vacunas y prevención tiene demanda reciente y ninguna hora de agenda» while its report says the capacity covers the demand. In the scatter, a name or number stays by its point only if no point beside it (within 48 viewBox units across) is as level with the text's middle line as its own point; otherwise the point takes a number and the name goes to the list. Every label that names a point carries `data-punto="x,y"`, so the browser check measures it against its point. **Why:** review 15.11 by ui-expert: the card and report disagreed on the demo; at 390 «Camila Levin» sat at the height of Emiliano Molina's point, hers lower. The page's own «sumar horas ahí» advice follows the same `regla` (`atencion`), so the client keeps no 90 % threshold of its own; the card's «cubre» sentence keeps the page's qualifier «en todas las especialidades con datos». A label raised with a leader line is tied to its point by the line and skips the height rule; a point exactly as level as the own one does not take the label. **Rejected:** copying the 2 % threshold into the card's JS (two places to drift). **Undo:** revert the commit. **Proof:** `tests/admin-reports-disclosure.test.mjs` (the card follows every rule the server can name, on the same data; no client threshold), `tests/test_reports_insights.py` (`regla` on the headline), `tests/admin-charts.test.mjs` (vertical nearness at 340/390/1100/1440).

**By:** agent panel-uix7.

## 2026-11-15 · The `asistente` permission no longer shows pre-consult interviews in «Chats» (security review M-4)

**Decision:** the «Chats» list (`/admin/chats`, permission `asistente`) shows the rows of the pre-consult interview engine (`channel` `interview` and `caso`, «Contanos tu caso» being the same engine before a request is sent; the first 240 characters of what the patient told about their health) only to a session that reads every patient's clinical data: the administrator or an account holding `todos_los_pacientes`, read from the permissions the screen gate verified for the request. Any other holder of `asistente` sees the web and WhatsApp conversations as before; the two channels are left out in the query (`db_chat.chat_conversations(exclude_channels=…)`, before its 200-row limit, so the list does not shrink), so nothing about them is looked up or recorded. **Why:** security review 2026-11, M-4 (least privilege): `asistente` means editing what the assistant knows and how it answers, and reading the assistant's conversations; the interview is part of the clinical record. There is no built-in `asistente` role: the permission is held by the administrator and by any custom role granted it. No desk screen depends on it: the day queue, «Pacientes de hoy», the appointment card and the triage colour are gated by `cola`, `solicitudes` and `atencion_medica`, and the interview APIs (consult, red alerts, chart summary) already answered 403 to `asistente` alone (now pinned). Doctors keep reading their own patients' interviews on their screens, with the access log as before. **Rejected:** taking `/admin/chats` away from `asistente` (it would lose the web and WhatsApp questions it uses to write wiki articles); showing a scoped doctor's own interviews in the list (they already have them in the consult). **Undo:** revert the commit. **Proof:** `tests/test_admin_chats_screen.py` (`asistente` alone: no interview or case row, no lookup, no log row; with `todos_los_pacientes` and for the admin: shown and logged; the interview APIs and the alert acknowledgement refuse it; its own screens stay mapped to it), `tests/test_db_chat_store.py` (excluded channels leave before the limit).

**By:** agent asistente-interview.

## 2026-11-15 · Mi cuenta: the eight-second deadline says the load is late, it no longer abandons it

**Decision:** in `templates/account.html` the eight-second timer no longer stops the Account load. At the deadline the page shows the recovery copy («No se pudo cargar el formulario. Recargá la página.») only if nothing is drawn in `#cuenta-host` yet, and keeps loading; a healthy late answer (the module or `/api/account/me`) replaces that copy with the form or the account. A refused module or a failed session read is still a failure: it stops the load and shows the same copy (`failed`). Generation ownership is unchanged: pagehide and a persisted pageshow still invalidate an in-flight load, also one past the deadline. This replaces the pinned rule of 2026-09-07 «the eight-second timeout blocks a late module mount».

**Why:** TASK-42, the flaky e2e «a rejected Account import shows recovery copy and recovers on reload». A probe on lobo delayed the session read or the account-form module: 3 s and 7 s ended in the form, 9 s ended on the recovery copy for good, although the answer came a second later; CPU throttling alone (x4 to x40) kept the form under 2.1 s. On munk at load 170+ the reload in that test took longer than 8 s and stayed on the recovery copy (04.11). A patient on a slow phone or a busy server saw «reload», and after reloading met the same race again. Before the fix, 720 runs of the spec on lobo (quiet and at load up to 321) gave no failure: the deadline is reached only on a slower machine or network. **Rejected:** a longer deadline (moves the threshold, keeps the dead end); covering only the imports and not the session read, as the queue screen does (the probe stalled on a slow module as well). **Not changed here:** the 16 admin and study screens with the same hard deadline (their controllers draw into a skeleton that the recovery copy replaces, so a late mount cannot simply land); reported as a follow-up. **Undo:** revert the commit. **Proof:** `tests/account-bootstrap-lifecycle.test.mjs` (a late healthy mount replaces the recovery copy; the deadline never covers a drawn account; a deadline before pagehide cannot mount the obsolete import), `tests/e2e/account-delayed.spec.mjs` «a session read / an Account module held past the eight-second deadline still ends in the form» (red before the fix, green after; a series of 270 runs quiet and 540 at load up to 360 on lobo, all green). **Accepted as is:** the late copy is the existing failure text with its reload link (a reload now reaches the form too; a separate «still loading» text in ten languages is its own change); the deadline still ends at `Account.ready`, before the signed-in dashboard's own `/api/requests` read, as before.

**By:** agent flake42.

## 2026-11-16 · Admin and study screens: one shared loader whose eight-second deadline says the load is late and keeps it

**Decision:** the 17 screens that carried their own hard eight-second watchdog (`templates/admin/` account, appearance, cola, content, doctor-workspace, hsi, mis-turnos, my-patients, pacientes, plan, preparations, reportes, settings, system; `templates/auth-test.html`, `templates/study/boot.html`, `app/modules/intake/admin_consult.html`) now boot through one module, `static/platform/screen-boot.mjs` (`bootScreen({host, recovery, load, mount, …})`). At eight seconds, if the controller has neither loaded its dependencies (`loaded`) nor become ready, the screen's own recovery copy is shown **beside** the screen — a `.screen-load-slow` block before the host, or the screen's status line where it has one (`slowIn`: the queue's `#queue-load-status`, content's `#content-status`, the doctor workspace's recovery node) — and the load goes on; a healthy late answer mounts the screen and takes the copy down. A refused import, a throwing mount, a rejected `ready` or the controller's `onError` still stop the load and show the copy inside the screen (`failedIn`, the host by default). Generation ownership is kept: pagehide stops an in-flight load, a persisted pageshow starts it again (or reloads, as the study page did), and the copy's reload acts only for the current generation. Same rule as Mi cuenta (2026-11-15), one implementation instead of 17.

**Why:** the hard watchdog stopped and disposed the load at eight seconds, so a healthy answer arriving at nine left the screen on «reload» for good — on a slow phone or a busy server a reload met the same race. The copy goes beside the screen, not into it, because these controllers draw into the skeleton the host holds; a copy that replaced it would leave the late mount nothing to draw into. **Rejected:** 17 local copies of the Mi cuenta fix (the same race fixed 17 ways); a longer deadline (moves the threshold, keeps the dead end). **Not changed here:** `templates/account.html` (already fixed, its own copy rules), the readiness timers of `confirm.mjs` / `offer.mjs` and the alerts widget in `templates/admin/base.html` (they bound a single request, not a screen's load). **Undo:** revert the commit. **Proof:** `tests/screen-boot.test.mjs` (12 unit tests: late copy, late mount clears it, failures stop, pagehide/pageshow ownership, reload only for the current generation; plus every one of the 17 screens imports the loader and keeps no `setTimeout(…, 8000)`), `tests/e2e/admin-screen-slow-load.spec.mjs` (queue, reports and the intake consultation: the module held past eight seconds through a route, released on a signal, still draws the screen and takes the copy down; red on the old templates), and the screens' lifecycle specs rewritten from «a late module cannot mount» to «the late module mounts». **Accepted as is:** if `screen-boot.mjs` itself fails to import the screen shows no recovery copy (the same as any failed first-party module; it is fingerprinted and served with the shell); the late copy is each screen's existing failure text with its reload button, not a separate «still loading» text.

**By:** agent watchdog16.

## 2026-11-16 · A late screen says «La carga tarda más de lo normal.», not its failure text

**Decision:** the eight-second late copy of the shared loader is one shared text, «La carga tarda más de lo normal.» with a «Recargar» button: `templates/_screen_load_late.html` (`<template id="screen-load-late">`, new key `page.load_late` with its Spanish fallback, the button reuses `admin.content.common.reload`), included once by `templates/admin/base.html` for every panel screen (the usability study screen included), by `templates/study/runner.html` and by `templates/auth-test.html` (which now has its own `#tid-load-slow` place, so its failure box stays a failure). `screen-boot.mjs` clones it at the deadline (`late` option, default `screen-load-late`) and falls back to the screen's recovery copy only on a page without it. A real failure keeps the screen's own text («No se pudo cargar esta sección.» and the like) where it always showed. The key is translated into the eight generated languages by `scripts/translate_locales.py` (only this key was pending).
