The text is phrasing content (a `<span>`) because a status line that receives it may be a `<p>` (`#content-status`).

**Undo:** revert the commit; no schema change. salud.pinamar.gob.ar shows it with its next release package.

**Proof:** `tests/screen-boot.test.mjs` (the late copy is the shared one and not the failure text, also in a given slow place and beside an element recovery; a failure still shows the screen's text; every late-loading page carries the template exactly once, with the key's Spanish fallback), `tests/e2e/admin-screen-slow-load.spec.mjs` (the queue held past eight seconds shows «La carga tarda más de lo normal.» and «Recargar», never «No se pudo cargar», above readable rows), the reports and plan lifecycle specs.

**By:** agent watchdog16.

## 2026-11-15 · Patient account after the ui-expert review: every section on a phone, a live-only badge, one naming

**Decision:**

- **Phone section menu**: under 900px the account rail is a horizontal scroll strip (44px targets, 16px, "Cerrar sesión" in it), the active section scrolls into view; icons stay on desktop only.
- **"Mis turnos" badge** counts live requests only (not cancelled/rejected, not past).
- **Pending requests** get their own section "Esperan confirmación del hospital" with ONE note; a past pending request reads "El hospital no llegó a confirmarla" and offers the rebook action. In history the left column carries status + request date, the right column only the appointment date.
- **Demo reseed** deletes the featured patient's visitor-made requests (by DNI / requester DNI), so the duplicate cancelled rows don't pile up.
- **Card header**: date stacked over "Al calendario", header stacks at a 640px container (the chat panel squeezes it to ~643px), titles wrap inside their column — fixes "CardiologíaMiércoles".
- **Forms**: labels above fields, dark ink (`--text`, not the transparent `--text-2`), DNI/date in the body font, postal code sized to content, × next to its field, "Todo guardado" only after a save.
- **Labs**: no lede paragraph, out-of-range count/"Ver todos" only when the list is partial, names and ranges in dark ink. The "se envía completa al proveedor" sentence stays: it is the registered AI notice (`ai_register.NOTICES`, tied to the legal texts) and the provider differs per installation.
- **One naming**: "Personas a cargo" (rail, heading, "Agregar persona a cargo"); "Tu asistente de IA" instead of "Asistente de IA (MCP)"; all section headings one 24px style.
- **Footer**: the account and reset pages use the shared site footer.
- **Icons**: every new glyph is an Icons8 SF Regular file painted by CSS mask; the desktop rail keeps its existing manifest-pulled Icons8 glyphs.
- Left to the public-site executor (nav, chat widget): header losing "Chat" with the panel open, chat naming (item 19), the floating chat button covering content.

**Why:** the demo review of 2026-11-15 found the account unreachable below 900px (no sections, no logout), a «Mis turnos 11» badge summing past and cancelled rows, duplicate cancelled requests, «Guardar cambios» next to «Todo guardado» and three names for the same section.

**Undo:** revert the commit of this entry.

**By:** agent hr-patient-review (part A).

## 2026-11-15 · Patient booking path: a chosen time survives the confirm step, one referral rule, two days of slots

**Decision:** a pass over the patient path service → doctor → book → confirm → upload order (hr-patient review, part B).
- A slot hold now belongs to whoever placed it (`slot_holds.owner_token`, session user or the hold token); the patient's own hold no longer hides that time from the patient, so `/book?slot=…` lands on the confirm step with the named time instead of falling back to step 1. A lost time says which one was lost (`{when}`).
- One referral rule for a service (`site_catalog.requires_referral`), read by the service page, the doctor page and the wizard; PAMI wording only where it applies.
- Copy: the action is «Pedir el turno», the confirmer is always «el hospital»; the upload subtitle no longer repeats the H1. The doctor's band says «Primer turno libre en {service}» in sentence case; the "Consultorio" line, the band subtitle and the «Hoy» chip on non-working days are gone; times as «8:00».
- Slots: a regular grid, the first two days shown and «Otras fechas» for the rest; numbers in the page font with tabular figures instead of monospace; 20px between the H1 and the confirm card.
- Upload zone: on a fine pointer the main button reads «Elegí la foto o el PDF de la orden» and the paste hint shows; on touch «Tomá una foto» and no paste hint.
- The boot reservation of the booking host is 500px from 600px width up (it was 600px): the two-day grid is 340–460px and the empty fallback ~800px, so the first-screen shift stays inside the budget for both.

**Why:** the review found a chosen time reset to step 1, three different referral rules, «Sacar» vs «Enviar solicitud» and «centro» vs «hospital» on one path, and a grid of all days that pushed the action out of the first screen. Left as they are: «Sacar turno» on entry buttons (the usual phrase), the white confirm page (product owner, 2026-10-27), the stepper hidden with one step, «Quién atiende» and «Lunes a viernes» (listing.mjs / week.mjs, outside this slice), the AI notice wording (bound to legal.py), the service's «Cómo funciona» sign (catalog data).

**Undo:** revert the commit.

**By:** agent hr-patient-B.

## 2026-11-15 · Demo pack: one doctor in one office at a time, centre copy from its services, /doctors without profile filters

**Decision:**

- **Hours.** In `scripts/generate_example_town_catalog.py` the hospital's cardiology cluster and the PAP service no longer share their doctors' hours with the consultation: electrocardiograma 12–14 Mon–Fri, ergometría 14–17 Tue/Thu, marcapasos 14–17 Mon/Wed/Fri, PAP 12–14 Mon–Fri. `tests/test_demo_catalog_coverage.py` pins two things across the whole pack. A doctor's windows never overlap on a weekday. Each window tagged with a service lies inside that service's agenda.
- **Seed.** `scripts/seed_demo_all.py` books its history on (service, doctor) pairs. Each pair's doctor belongs to the service's pool (`schedule_policy.belongs`), and the hour comes from that doctor's window for that service. Ecografía general moved from the tomography doctor to Herrero.
- **Centre copy.** A centre's description, «para qué ir» line and jobs name only services held at that centre. The hospital's copy was already fixed on main (D30/D31); Riverside, Lakeview and the Diagnostic Center now list what they actually have. A test matches every word of that copy on the stem of each other centre's service names.
- **/doctors filters.** The doctors list does not preload the visitor's age and sex filters from the account (`profile: false`). On the demo account the hidden «Adultos» filter emptied «Pediatra» and the search example «Sánchez», a pediatrician.

**Why:** the demo review of 2026-11-15 found a Cardiología appointment at 07:00, outside the service's hours. It also found Camila Levin listed in three offices on one Monday, centre pages promising services held elsewhere, and a doctors list that showed no pediatricians with no visible filter set.

**Rejected:**
- Keeping the profile preload with a visible chip, because the list pages belong to the parallel public-pages slice.

**Cost:**
- Browser specs that seed electrocardiograma-hospital at 08–12 hours (j29 and others) need a night run, and may need new hours.
- Two goldens were re-recorded (`site_pages.json`, `doctor_pool.json`), as was the admin settings body contract.

The gendered specialty title of the same review came from main (the pack's `genero` field), so this slice dropped its own name-based guess.

**Undo:** revert the commit of this entry and rerun the generator.

**By:** agent hr-patient-review (part C).

## 2026-11-16 · Slot holds: a hold linked to a request is never the account's free pick; the account key never falls back to a constant

**Decision:**

- **Linked holds.** Since 2026-11-15 a signed-in patient's holds go under one account token for every device. A hold already linked to a pending request keeps its expiry when its token takes it again (`db_slots` take: only a loose or expired hold gets the new TTL; a repeated submit with the same `idem_key` still succeeds), and `slot_holds.taken()` excludes only the caller's loose holds. Before, the same account could see its own pending appointment's time as free, and a new hold on it rewrote the linked row's expiry to 3 minutes.
- **Account key.** `slot_holds.owner_token` keys its HMAC with `TURNOS_ADMIN_SECRET`, else the admin password (as `admin_auth` signs sessions), else one random key per process. The old constant fallback made the token computable from the DNI by anyone reading the source.
- **PAMI note.** The note's audience check reads the registry's spellings too («INSSJP», «…JUBILADOS Y PENSIONADOS»), not only the word «PAMI».
- **Doctor page.** In «Cuándo atiende» a strip's service name books that service with this doctor (`?medico=`), the same link the «Atiende:» line had; the line is dropped only when the strips name every service.

**Why:** /code-review high on the patient-review branch.

## 2026-11-16 · Meta WhatsApp: a rate limit pauses every Meta send, an error code reads as words, the code goes as the approved Authentication template

**Decision:** three changes in `app/notifications/otp_send.py`, one send path (`_meta_post`) for free text, Utility templates and the verification code.
(1) A Meta rate limit (codes 4, 80007, 130429, 131048, 131056, HTTP 429, or any `Retry-After` header) opens a pause of `Retry-After` seconds (an integer or an HTTP date), 60 s without the header, never longer than 15 min, never shortened by a second limit; while it lasts no Meta send reaches the network (a logged no-op, the booking persists as always). The pause is a small file, `<data_dir>/private/meta-wa-state.json` (numbers only: until when, the code, the last error code and HTTP status and its time, the time of the last good send; Meta subcodes are not kept), changed under an exclusive `flock` on `meta-wa-state.json.lock` and written with an atomic replace, one write per outcome, because the web process and the queue worker both send and must see the same pause; an unwritable volume falls back to the process's memory. «Sistema» → WhatsApp shows «En pausa hasta …» with the cause, then «La pausa terminó: …» for 24 h.
(2) `otp_send._META_MOTIVOS` maps Meta's error codes (131047, 132001, 190 and the common ones: 0, 3, 4, 10, 100, 200, 368, 429, 80007, 1300xx/1310xx/1320xx/133010) to an i18n key under `admin.system.status.wa.meta_error.` with its Spanish fallback; an unknown code reads «Error de Meta sin causa conocida». The log line keeps `HTTPError (status) code` and adds ` — cause` (Spanish), never Meta's own message; «Sistema» shows the last error for 24 h, red for the codes waiting does not fix (`otp_send.META_CODIGOS_GRAVES`: 0, 10, 190, 200, 368, 131005, 131031, 131042, 132001, 132015, 132016) until a send succeeds again. A send held back by the pause is not a launch-signal failure (it never reached Meta). The panel and the sender read `OTP_PROVIDER` through one helper, `otp_send.otp_provider()`.
(3) With `OTP_PROVIDER=meta` the verification code goes ONLY as the approved Authentication template named by `META_WA_OTP_TEMPLATE` (body parameter = the code, plus the URL-button parameter Meta requires for the copy-code button, language `es_AR`); without the variable it stays the logged no-op it was. The Human Rounds WABA has `turnos_codigo` (AUTHENTICATION, es_AR, «Copiar código», 10 min expiry), approved by Meta on 2026-11-16. No site enables it yet: setting `OTP_PROVIDER=meta` + `META_WA_OTP_TEMPLATE=turnos_codigo` is the owner's call, and `OTP_GATING_ENABLED` only after one real code has arrived.

**Why:** before this, every send after a limit went on hitting Meta (which prolongs the limit), and each failure only said `HTTPError (400) 131056`: nobody at the desk could tell a template problem from an expired token (cards TASK-79/80/81). The pause is a file, not a table: it is operational state of minutes, both processes already share the data volume, and the panel reads it without the database. No `META_WA_TEMPLATE_LANG` variable: every template we own is `es_AR`. `META_WA_OTP_TEMPLATE` is not a connector `config_key` (those are all required; the code template is optional).

**Rejected:** a per-number queue with retries (the reminder job already re-runs; a pause is enough); logging Meta's `message` (it echoes the number and names); porting `fd836d1e4` by cherry-pick (it predates the pause and the shared send path).

**Undo:** revert the commit; delete `private/meta-wa-state.json` to clear a pause by hand (`otp_send.meta_pausa_reset()`). No schema change. salud.pinamar.gob.ar gets it with its next release package.

**Proof:** `tests/test_meta_wa_limits.py` (every limit code and 429 pause all sends without touching the network; Retry-After seconds and HTTP date, 60 s without it, 15 min cap, a second limit never shortens; another process reads the pause from the file; the file holds no phone or text; an infinite or bogus `Retry-After` falls back to 60 s; a held send raises no launch signal; 131047/132001/190/unknown read as words in the log, never Meta's text; the code template payload, its refusal 132001, the no-op without the variable, the demo; the «Sistema» rows), `tests/test_transport_contract.py`, `tests/test_logs_pii.py`.

**By:** agent meta-wa.
